FILE: prisma\schema.prisma

generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client"
  output   = "../src/generated/prisma"
}

datasource db {
  provider = "sqlite"
}

model Account {
  id                 Int      @id @default(autoincrement())
  name               String
  institution        String?
  country            String
  currency           String
  balanceMinor       Int
  accountType        String
  liquidityClass     String
  includedInNetWorth Boolean  @default(true)
  notes              String?
  valuationDate      DateTime
  createdAt          DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt          DateTime @updatedAt
}

model Asset {
  id               Int      @id @default(autoincrement())
  name             String
  assetType        String
  country          String?
  currency         String
  quantity         Float?
  costBasisMinor   Int?
  marketValueMinor Int
  riskLevel        String
  liquidityClass   String
  valuationDate    DateTime
  source           String?
  notes            String?
  createdAt        DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt        DateTime @updatedAt
}

model Liability {
  id                   Int                @id @default(autoincrement())
  name                 String
  liabilityType        String
  accountingTreatment  String             @default("NET_WORTH_DEBT")
  country              String?
  currency             String
  originalAmountMinor  Int
  remainingAmountMinor Int
  monthlyPaymentMinor  Int?
  startDate            DateTime?
  endDate              DateTime?
  includedInNetWorth   Boolean            @default(true)
  notes                String?
  payments             LiabilityPayment[]
  createdAt            DateTime           @default(now())
  updatedAt            DateTime           @updatedAt
}

model LiabilityPayment {
  id                  Int      @id @default(autoincrement())
  liabilityId         Int
  paymentDate         DateTime
  amountMinor         Int
  paymentType         String   @default("INSTALLMENT")
  status              String   @default("PLANNED")
  description         String?
  isRecurringEstimate Boolean  @default(false)
  createdAt           DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt           DateTime @updatedAt

  liability Liability @relation(fields: [liabilityId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([liabilityId, paymentDate])
  @@index([paymentDate])
}

model CashFlow {
  id          Int       @id @default(autoincrement())
  name        String
  flowType    String
  scope       String    @default("EGYPT")
  currency    String
  amountMinor Int
  frequency   String
  startDate   DateTime
  endDate     DateTime?
  category    String
  notes       String?
  createdAt   DateTime  @default(now())
  updatedAt   DateTime  @updatedAt
}

model Goal {
  id                Int       @id @default(autoincrement())
  name              String
  currency          String
  targetAmountMinor Int
  fundedAmountMinor Int       @default(0)
  targetDate        DateTime?
  priority          Int
  status            String    @default("ACTIVE")
  notes             String?
  createdAt         DateTime  @default(now())
  updatedAt         DateTime  @updatedAt
}

model ExchangeRate {
  id           Int      @id @default(autoincrement())
  fromCurrency String
  toCurrency   String
  rate         Float
  source       String
  rateDate     DateTime
  createdAt    DateTime @default(now())

  @@unique([fromCurrency, toCurrency, rateDate])
}

model Snapshot {
  id                  Int      @id @default(autoincrement())
  snapshotDate        DateTime
  baseCurrency        String
  netWorthMinor       Int
  liquidNetWorthMinor Int
  emergencyFundMinor  Int?
  notes               String?
  createdAt           DateTime @default(now())
}

model ForecastPlan {
  id                   Int      @id @default(autoincrement())
  name                 String   @unique
  forecastYear         Int
  baseCurrency         String   @default("EGP")
  openingBalanceMinor  Int      @default(0)
  safetyThresholdMinor Int      @default(0)
  notes                String?
  createdAt            DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt            DateTime @updatedAt
}

// A tradable or market-priced financial instrument.
// Examples: BTC, Saudi Aramco, AAPL, COMI, physical gold, or a mutual fund.
model Instrument {
  id                Int      @id @default(autoincrement())
  symbol            String
  name              String
  assetClass        String
  market            String
  quoteCurrency     String
  priceProvider     String   @default("MANUAL")
  providerSymbol    String?
  isin              String?
  sector            String?
  country           String?
  priceScale        Int      @default(100)
  maximumStaleHours Int      @default(48)
  automaticUpdate   Boolean  @default(false)
  isActive          Boolean  @default(true)
  notes             String?
  createdAt         DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt         DateTime @updatedAt

  holdings Holding[]
  prices   MarketPrice[]

  @@unique([market, symbol])
  @@index([assetClass])
  @@index([priceProvider, providerSymbol])
}

// A position owned in an instrument.
// Quantity is stored as text to preserve precision for crypto and fund units.
model Holding {
  id                  Int       @id @default(autoincrement())
  instrumentId        Int
  accountName         String?
  quantityText        String
  averageCostMinor    Int
  costCurrency        String
  acquisitionDate     DateTime?
  feesMinor           Int       @default(0)
  remainingCostMinor  Int?
  latestPriceMinor    Int?
  latestPriceCurrency String?
  marketValueMinor    Int?
  unrealizedPnlMinor  Int?
  lastValuedAt        DateTime?
  riskLevel           String    @default("MEDIUM")
  liquidityClass      String    @default("HIGH")
  includedInNetWorth  Boolean   @default(true)
  notes               String?
  createdAt           DateTime  @default(now())
  updatedAt           DateTime  @updatedAt

  instrument Instrument @relation(fields: [instrumentId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  transactions InvestmentTransaction[]

  @@index([instrumentId])
  @@index([accountName])
}

// A buy, sell, dividend, fee, tax, transfer, or corporate action.
// This table will later calculate realized profit and the remaining cost basis.
model InvestmentTransaction {
  id               Int      @id @default(autoincrement())
  holdingId        Int
  transactionType  String
  transactionDate  DateTime
  quantityText     String?
  unitPriceMinor   Int?
  grossAmountMinor Int?
  feesMinor        Int      @default(0)
  taxesMinor       Int      @default(0)
  netAmountMinor   Int?
  currency         String
  notes            String?
  createdAt        DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt        DateTime @updatedAt

  holding Holding @relation(fields: [holdingId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([holdingId, transactionDate])
  @@index([transactionType])
}

// One instrument can have many historical prices.
// Price date represents the market timestamp; fetchedAt records when Mizan obtained it.
model MarketPrice {
  id           Int      @id @default(autoincrement())
  instrumentId Int
  priceMinor   Int
  currency     String
  priceDate    DateTime
  fetchedAt    DateTime @default(now())
  provider     String
  sourceUrl    String?
  priceType    String   @default("CLOSE")
  isOfficial   Boolean  @default(false)
  isDelayed    Boolean  @default(false)
  isManual     Boolean  @default(false)
  notes        String?

  instrument Instrument @relation(fields: [instrumentId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@unique([instrumentId, priceDate, provider, priceType])
  @@index([instrumentId, priceDate])
  @@index([provider, fetchedAt])
}

// Audit log for scheduled or manual price updates.
model PriceUpdateRun {
  id             Int       @id @default(autoincrement())
  provider       String
  status         String
  startedAt      DateTime  @default(now())
  completedAt    DateTime?
  requestedCount Int       @default(0)
  successCount   Int       @default(0)
  failureCount   Int       @default(0)
  errorMessage   String?
  createdAt      DateTime  @default(now())

  @@index([provider, startedAt])
}

import "dotenv/config";
import { defineConfig, env } from "prisma/config";

export default defineConfig({
  schema: "prisma/schema.prisma",
  migrations: {
    path: "prisma/migrations",

============================================================
FILE: src\app\accounts\[id]\edit\edit-account-form.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useActionState, useEffect } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import {
  updateAccount,
  type AccountActionState,
} from "../../actions";

type EditAccountFormProps = {
  account: {
    id: number;
    name: string;
    institution: string | null;
    country: string;
    currency: string;
    balanceMinor: number;
    accountType: string;
    liquidityClass: string;
    includedInNetWorth: boolean;
    notes: string | null;
  };
};

const initialState: AccountActionState = {
  success: false,
  message: "",
};

function toMajor(amountMinor: number): string {
  return (amountMinor / 100).toFixed(2);
}

export function EditAccountForm({
  account,
}: EditAccountFormProps) {
  const router = useRouter();

  const [state, formAction, isPending] = useActionState(
    updateAccount,
    initialState
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!state.success) {
      return;
    }

    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      router.push("/accounts");
      router.refresh();
    }, 800);

    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [state.success, router]);

  return (
    <form action={formAction} className="space-y-5">
      <input type="hidden" name="id" value={account.id} />

      {state.message ? (
        <div
          role="alert"
          aria-live="polite"
          className={`rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm ${
            state.success
              ? "border-emerald-500/20 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300"
              : "border-rose-500/20 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300"
          }`}
        >
          {state.message}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <div>
        <label
          htmlFor="name"
          className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
        >
          Account name
        </label>

        <input
          id="name"
          name="name"
          type="text"
          required
          defaultValue={account.name}
          disabled={isPending}
          className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none focus:border-blue-500 disabled:opacity-60"
        />
      </div>

      <div>
        <label
          htmlFor="institution"
          className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
        >
          Institution
        </label>

        <input
          id="institution"
          name="institution"
          type="text"
          required
          defaultValue={account.institution ?? ""}
          disabled={isPending}
          className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none focus:border-blue-500 disabled:opacity-60"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <div>
          <label
            htmlFor="country"
            className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
          >
            Country
          </label>

          <select
            id="country"
            name="country"
            defaultValue={account.country}
            disabled={isPending}
            className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white disabled:opacity-60"
          >
            <option value="Saudi Arabia">Saudi Arabia</option>
            <option value="Egypt">Egypt</option>
            <option value="External">External</option>
            <option value="Other">Other</option>
          </select>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label
            htmlFor="currency"
            className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
          >
            Currency
          </label>

          <select
            id="currency"
            name="currency"
            defaultValue={account.currency}
            disabled={isPending}
            className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white disabled:opacity-60"
          >
            <option value="SAR">SAR</option>
            <option value="EGP">EGP</option>
            <option value="USD">USD</option>

import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { EditAccountForm } from "./edit-account-form";

type EditAccountPageProps = {
  params: Promise<{
    id: string;
  }>;
};

export default async function EditAccountPage({
  params,
}: EditAccountPageProps) {
  const { id: rawId } = await params;
  const id = Number(rawId);

  if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
    notFound();
  }

  const account = await prisma.account.findUnique({
    where: {
      id,
    },
  });

  if (!account) {
    notFound();
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-950 text-slate-100">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-6 py-10">
        <header className="mb-8">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-400">
            Mizan Local
          </p>

          <h1 className="mt-3 text-4xl font-bold text-white">
            Edit Account
          </h1>

          <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-slate-400">
            Update the balance, classification, or account details.
          </p>
        </header>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
          <EditAccountForm account={account} />
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

============================================================
FILE: src\app\accounts\actions.ts
============================================================
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

export type AccountActionState = {
  success: boolean;
  message: string;
};

function readRequiredText(formData: FormData, field: string): string {
  const value = formData.get(field);

  if (typeof value !== "string" || value.trim() === "") {
    throw new Error(`${field} is required`);
  }

  return value.trim();
}

function readAmount(formData: FormData, field: string): number {
  const rawValue = formData.get(field);

  if (typeof rawValue !== "string" || rawValue.trim() === "") {
    throw new Error(`${field} is required`);
  }

  const normalizedValue = rawValue.replaceAll(",", "").trim();
  const amount = Number(normalizedValue);

  if (!Number.isFinite(amount)) {
    throw new Error(`${field} must be a valid number`);
  }

  return amount;
}

function toMinor(amount: number): number {
  return Math.round(amount * 100);
}

export async function createAccount(
  previousState: AccountActionState,
  formData: FormData
): Promise<AccountActionState> {
  try {
    const name = readRequiredText(formData, "name");
    const institution = readRequiredText(formData, "institution");
    const country = readRequiredText(formData, "country");
    const currency = readRequiredText(formData, "currency");
    const accountType = readRequiredText(formData, "accountType");
    const liquidityClass = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "liquidityClass"
    );
    const balance = readAmount(formData, "balance");

    if (accountType === "CREDIT_CARD" && balance > 0) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message:
          "Credit-card balances must be entered as zero or a negative number.",
      };
    }

    const notesValue = formData.get("notes");
    const notes =
      typeof notesValue === "string" && notesValue.trim()
        ? notesValue.trim()
        : null;

    const includedInNetWorth =
      formData.get("includedInNetWorth") === "on";

    await prisma.account.create({
      data: {
        name,
        institution,
        country,
        currency,
        balanceMinor: toMinor(balance),
        accountType,
        liquidityClass,
        includedInNetWorth,
        notes,
        valuationDate: new Date(),
      },
    });

    revalidatePath("/");
    revalidatePath("/accounts");
    revalidatePath("/database-test");

    return {
      success: true,
      message: `${name} was added successfully.`,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Create account failed:", error);

    return {
      success: false,
      message:
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "The account could not be created.",
    };
  }
}

export async function deleteAccount(formData: FormData) {
  const idValue = formData.get("id");

  if (typeof idValue !== "string") {
    throw new Error("Account ID is required");
  }

  const id = Number(idValue);

  if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
    throw new Error("Invalid account ID");
  }

  await prisma.account.delete({
    where: {
      id,
    },
  });

  revalidatePath("/");
  revalidatePath("/accounts");
  revalidatePath("/database-test");
}
export async function updateAccount(
  previousState: AccountActionState,
  formData: FormData
): Promise<AccountActionState> {
  try {
    const idValue = formData.get("id");

    if (typeof idValue !== "string") {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Account ID is required.",
      };
    }

    const id = Number(idValue);

    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid account ID.",
      };
    }

    const name = readRequiredText(formData, "name");
    const institution = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "institution"
    );
    const country = readRequiredText(formData, "country");
    const currency = readRequiredText(formData, "currency");
    const accountType = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "accountType"
    );
    const liquidityClass = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "liquidityClass"
    );
    const balance = readAmount(formData, "balance");

    if (accountType === "CREDIT_CARD" && balance > 0) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message:
          "Credit-card balances must be entered as zero or a negative number.",
      };
    }

    const notesValue = formData.get("notes");
    const notes =
      typeof notesValue === "string" && notesValue.trim()
        ? notesValue.trim()
        : null;

    const includedInNetWorth =
      formData.get("includedInNetWorth") === "on";

    await prisma.account.update({
      where: {
        id,
      },
      data: {
        name,
        institution,
        country,
        currency,
        balanceMinor: toMinor(balance),
        accountType,
        liquidityClass,
        includedInNetWorth,
        notes,
        valuationDate: new Date(),
      },
    });

    revalidatePath("/");
    revalidatePath("/accounts");
    revalidatePath(`/accounts/${id}/edit`);
    revalidatePath("/database-test");

    return {
      success: true,
      message: `${name} was updated successfully.`,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Update account failed:", error);

    return {
      success: false,
      message:
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "The account could not be updated.",
    };
  }
}

FILE: src\app\accounts\confirm-delete-button.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import { useRef, useState } from "react";

type ConfirmDeleteButtonProps = {
  accountId: number;
  accountName: string;
  deleteAction: (formData: FormData) => void | Promise<void>;
};

export function ConfirmDeleteButton({
  accountId,
  accountName,
  deleteAction,
}: ConfirmDeleteButtonProps) {
  const dialogRef = useRef<HTMLDialogElement>(null);
  const [isDeleting, setIsDeleting] = useState(false);

  function openDialog() {
    dialogRef.current?.showModal();
  }

  function closeDialog() {
    if (!isDeleting) {
      dialogRef.current?.close();
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={openDialog}
        className="rounded-lg border border-rose-500/20 bg-rose-500/10 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-rose-300 transition hover:bg-rose-500/20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-rose-500/40"
      >
        Delete
      </button>

      <dialog
        ref={dialogRef}
        onCancel={(event) => {
          if (isDeleting) {
            event.preventDefault();
          }
        }}
        className="m-auto w-[calc(100%-2rem)] max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 p-0 text-slate-100 shadow-2xl backdrop:bg-black/70"
      >
        <div className="p-6">
          <div className="flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300">
            <svg
              aria-hidden="true"
              viewBox="0 0 24 24"
              fill="none"
              className="size-5"
            >
              <path
                d="M3 6h18M9 6V4h6v2m-8 0 1 14h8l1-14M10 10v6m4-6v6"
                stroke="currentColor"
                strokeWidth="1.8"
                strokeLinecap="round"
                strokeLinejoin="round"
              />
            </svg>
          </div>

          <h2 className="mt-5 text-xl font-semibold text-white">
            Delete account?
          </h2>

          <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-400">
            You are about to permanently delete{" "}
            <strong className="font-semibold text-slate-200">
              {accountName}
            </strong>
            . This action cannot be undone.
          </p>

          <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={closeDialog}
              disabled={isDeleting}
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-200 transition hover:bg-slate-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>

            <form
              action={async (formData) => {
                setIsDeleting(true);

                try {
                  await deleteAction(formData);
                  dialogRef.current?.close();
                } finally {
                  setIsDeleting(false);
                }
              }}
            >
              <input type="hidden" name="id" value={accountId} />

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={isDeleting}
                className="w-full rounded-xl bg-rose-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-rose-500 disabled:cursor-wait disabled:opacity-60"
              >
                {isDeleting ? "Deleting..." : "Delete permanently"}
              </button>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      </dialog>
    </>
  );
}

import Link from "next/link";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { deleteAccount } from "./actions";
import { AccountForm } from "./account-form";
import { ConfirmDeleteButton } from "./confirm-delete-button";


function formatMoney(amountMinor: number, currency: string) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
    style: "currency",
    currency,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  }).format(amountMinor / 100);
}

function AccountTypeBadge({ type }: { type: string }) {
  const colors: Record<string, string> = {
    BANK: "bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-300 ring-blue-500/20",
    CASH: "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300 ring-emerald-500/20",
    CREDIT_CARD: "bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300 ring-rose-500/20",
    BROKERAGE: "bg-violet-500/10 text-violet-300 ring-violet-500/20",
    DIGITAL_WALLET:
      "bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-300 ring-amber-500/20",
    RECEIVABLE:
      "bg-cyan-500/10 text-cyan-300 ring-cyan-500/20",
  };

  return (
    <span
      className={`inline-flex rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ring-1 ${
        colors[type] ??
        "bg-slate-500/10 text-slate-300 ring-slate-500/20"
      }`}
    >
      {type.replaceAll("_", " ")}
    </span>
  );
}

async function AccountsTable() {
  const accounts = await prisma.account.findMany({
    orderBy: [
      {
        currency: "asc",
      },
      {
        name: "asc",
      },
    ],
  });

  if (accounts.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-700 bg-slate-900/50 p-10 text-center">
        <h2 className="font-semibold text-white">No accounts yet</h2>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-400">
          Add the first bank, cash, card, brokerage, or digital-wallet
          account using the form.
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900">
      <div className="overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="min-w-full divide-y divide-slate-800">
          <thead className="bg-slate-900">
            <tr className="text-left text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
              <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">Account</th>
              <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">Type</th>
              <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">Country</th>
              <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">Liquidity</th>
              <th className="px-5 py-4 text-right font-medium">
                Balance
              </th>
              <th className="px-5 py-4 text-center font-medium">
                Net worth
              </th>
              <th className="px-5 py-4 text-right font-medium">
                Action
              </th>
            </tr>
          </thead>

          <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-800">
            {accounts.map((account) => {
              const isNegative = account.balanceMinor < 0;

              return (
                <tr
                  key={account.id}
                  className="transition hover:bg-slate-800/40"
                >
                  <td className="px-5 py-4">
                    <p className="font-medium text-white">
                      {account.name}
                    </p>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
                      {account.institution ?? "No institution"}
                    </p>
                  </td>

                  <td className="px-5 py-4">
                    <AccountTypeBadge type={account.accountType} />
                  </td>

                  <td className="px-5 py-4 text-sm text-slate-300">
                    {account.country}
                  </td>

                  <td className="px-5 py-4 text-sm text-slate-300">
                    {account.liquidityClass}
                  </td>

                  <td
                    className={`whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4 text-right font-semibold ${
                      isNegative ? "text-rose-300" : "text-white"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {formatMoney(
                      account.balanceMinor,
                      account.currency
                    )}
                  </td>

                  <td className="px-5 py-4 text-center">
                    <span
                      className={`inline-flex rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${
                        account.includedInNetWorth
                          ? "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300"
                          : "bg-slate-700 text-slate-300"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {account.includedInNetWorth
                        ? "Included"
                        : "Excluded"}
                    </span>
                  </td>

                  <td className="px-5 py-4 text-right">
                    <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
                        <Link
                            href={`/accounts/${account.id}/edit`}
                            className="rounded-lg border border-blue-500/20 bg-blue-500/10 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-300 transition hover:bg-blue-500/20"
                        >
                        Edit
                        </Link>

                        <ConfirmDeleteButton
                            accountId={account.id}
                            accountName={account.name}
                            deleteAction={deleteAccount}
                     />
                        </div>
                    </td>
                </tr>
              );
            })}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";

import {
  updateAsset,
  type AssetActionState,
} from "../../actions";

type EditAssetFormProps = {
  asset: {
    id: number;
    name: string;
    assetType: string;
    country: string | null;
    currency: string;
    quantity: number | null;
    costBasisMinor: number | null;
    marketValueMinor: number;
    riskLevel: string;
    liquidityClass: string;
    valuationDate: string;
    source: string | null;
    notes: string | null;
  };
};

const initialState: AssetActionState = {
  success: false,
  message: "",
};

function toMajor(amountMinor: number | null): string {
  if (amountMinor === null) {
    return "";
  }

  return (amountMinor / 100).toFixed(2);
}

export function EditAssetForm({
  asset,
}: EditAssetFormProps) {
  const router = useRouter();

  const [state, formAction, isPending] = useActionState(
    updateAsset,
    initialState
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!state.success) {
      return;
    }

    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      router.push("/assets");
      router.refresh();
    }, 800);

    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [state.success, router]);

  return (
    <form action={formAction} className="space-y-5">
      <input type="hidden" name="id" value={asset.id} />

      {state.message ? (
        <div
          role="alert"
          aria-live="polite"
          className={`rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm ${
            state.success
              ? "border-emerald-500/20 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300"
              : "border-rose-500/20 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300"
          }`}

          {state.message}
        </div>
      ) : null}

        <label
          htmlFor="name"
          className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
        >
          Asset name
        </label>

        <input
          id="name"
          name="name"
          required
          defaultValue={asset.name}
          disabled={isPending}
          className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none focus:border-blue-500 disabled:opacity-60"
        />
      </div>

        <div>
          <label
            htmlFor="assetType"

          >
            Asset type
          </label>

          <select
            id="assetType"
            name="assetType"

            disabled={isPending}

          >
            Country / location
          </label>

          <select
            id="country"

import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { EditAssetForm } from "./edit-asset-form";

type EditAssetPageProps = {
  params: Promise<{
    id: string;
  }>;
};

function toDateInputValue(date: Date): string {
  return date.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

export default async function EditAssetPage({
  params,
}: EditAssetPageProps) {
  const { id: rawId } = await params;
  const id = Number(rawId);

  if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
    notFound();
  }

  const asset = await prisma.asset.findUnique({
    where: {
      id,
    },
  });

  if (!asset) {
    notFound();
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-950 text-slate-100">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-6 py-10">
        <header className="mb-8">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-400">
            Mizan Local
          </p>

          <h1 className="mt-3 text-4xl font-bold text-white">
            Edit Asset
          </h1>

          <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-slate-400">
            Update the confirmed market value, cost basis,
            quantity, classification, or valuation date.
          </p>
        </header>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
          <EditAssetForm
            asset={{
              ...asset,
              valuationDate: toDateInputValue(
                asset.valuationDate
              ),
            }}
          />
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

============================================================
FILE: src\app\assets\actions.ts
============================================================
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

export type AssetActionState = {
  success: boolean;
  message: string;
};

function readRequiredText(
  formData: FormData,
  field: string
): string {
  const value = formData.get(field);

  if (typeof value !== "string" || value.trim() === "") {
    throw new Error(`${field} is required.`);
  }

  return value.trim();
}

function readRequiredAmount(
  formData: FormData,
  field: string
): number {
  const rawValue = formData.get(field);

  if (typeof rawValue !== "string" || rawValue.trim() === "") {
    throw new Error(`${field} is required.`);
  }

  const value = Number(rawValue.replaceAll(",", "").trim());

  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {
    throw new Error(`${field} must be a valid number.`);
  }

  return value;
}

function readOptionalAmount(
  formData: FormData,
  field: string
): number | null {
  const rawValue = formData.get(field);

  if (typeof rawValue !== "string" || rawValue.trim() === "") {
    return null;
  }

  const value = Number(rawValue.replaceAll(",", "").trim());

  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {
    throw new Error(`${field} must be a valid number.`);
  }

  return value;
}

function toMinor(amount: number): number {
  return Math.round(amount * 100);
}

export async function createAsset(
  previousState: AssetActionState,
  formData: FormData
): Promise<AssetActionState> {
  try {
    const name = readRequiredText(formData, "name");
    const assetType = readRequiredText(formData, "assetType");
    const country = readRequiredText(formData, "country");
    const currency = readRequiredText(formData, "currency");
    const riskLevel = readRequiredText(formData, "riskLevel");
    const liquidityClass = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "liquidityClass"
    );

    const quantity = readOptionalAmount(formData, "quantity");
    const costBasis = readOptionalAmount(formData, "costBasis");
    const marketValue = readRequiredAmount(
      formData,
      "marketValue"
    );

    if (quantity !== null && quantity < 0) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Quantity cannot be negative.",
      };
    }

    if (costBasis !== null && costBasis < 0) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Cost basis cannot be negative.",
      };
    }

    if (marketValue < 0) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Market value cannot be negative.",
      };
    }

    const sourceValue = formData.get("source");
    const source =
      typeof sourceValue === "string" && sourceValue.trim()
        ? sourceValue.trim()
        : null;

    const notesValue = formData.get("notes");
    const notes =
      typeof notesValue === "string" && notesValue.trim()
        ? notesValue.trim()
        : null;

    const valuationDateValue = formData.get("valuationDate");

    const valuationDate =
      typeof valuationDateValue === "string" &&
      valuationDateValue.trim()
        ? new Date(`${valuationDateValue}T12:00:00`)
        : new Date();

    if (Number.isNaN(valuationDate.getTime())) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Valuation date is invalid.",
      };
    }

    await prisma.asset.create({
      data: {
        name,
        assetType,
        country,
        currency,
        quantity,
        costBasisMinor:
          costBasis === null ? null : toMinor(costBasis),
        marketValueMinor: toMinor(marketValue),
        riskLevel,
        liquidityClass,
        valuationDate,
        source,
        notes,
      },
    });

    revalidatePath("/");
    revalidatePath("/assets");
    revalidatePath("/database-test");

    return {
      success: true,
      message: `${name} was added successfully.`,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Create asset failed:", error);

    return {
      success: false,
      message:
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "The asset could not be created.",
    };
  }
}
export async function deleteAsset(formData: FormData) {
  const idValue = formData.get("id");

  if (typeof idValue !== "string") {
    throw new Error("Asset ID is required.");
  }

  const id = Number(idValue);

  if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
    throw new Error("Invalid asset ID.");
  }

  await prisma.asset.delete({
    where: {
      id,
    },
  });

  revalidatePath("/");
  revalidatePath("/assets");
  revalidatePath("/database-test");
}
export async function updateAsset(
  previousState: AssetActionState,
  formData: FormData
): Promise<AssetActionState> {
  try {
    const idValue = formData.get("id");

    if (typeof idValue !== "string") {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Asset ID is required.",
      };
    }

    const id = Number(idValue);

    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid asset ID.",
      };
    }

    const name = readRequiredText(formData, "name");
    const assetType = readRequiredText(formData, "assetType");
    const country = readRequiredText(formData, "country");
    const currency = readRequiredText(formData, "currency");
    const riskLevel = readRequiredText(formData, "riskLevel");
    const liquidityClass = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "liquidityClass"
    );

    const quantity = readOptionalAmount(formData, "quantity");
    const costBasis = readOptionalAmount(formData, "costBasis");
    const marketValue = readRequiredAmount(
      formData,
      "marketValue"
    );

    if (quantity !== null && quantity < 0) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Quantity cannot be negative.",
      };
    }

    if (costBasis !== null && costBasis < 0) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Cost basis cannot be negative.",
      };
    }

    if (marketValue < 0) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Market value cannot be negative.",
      };
    }

    const valuationDateValue = formData.get("valuationDate");

    const valuationDate =
      typeof valuationDateValue === "string" &&
      valuationDateValue.trim()
        ? new Date(`${valuationDateValue}T12:00:00`)
        : new Date();

    if (Number.isNaN(valuationDate.getTime())) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Valuation date is invalid.",
      };
    }

    const sourceValue = formData.get("source");
    const source =
      typeof sourceValue === "string" && sourceValue.trim()
        ? sourceValue.trim()
        : null;

    const notesValue = formData.get("notes");
    const notes =
      typeof notesValue === "string" && notesValue.trim()
        ? notesValue.trim()
        : null;

    await prisma.asset.update({
      where: {
        id,
      },
      data: {
        name,
        assetType,
        country,
        currency,
        quantity,
        costBasisMinor:
          costBasis === null ? null : toMinor(costBasis),
        marketValueMinor: toMinor(marketValue),
        riskLevel,
        liquidityClass,
        valuationDate,
        source,
        notes,
      },
    });

    revalidatePath("/");
    revalidatePath("/assets");
    revalidatePath(`/assets/${id}/edit`);
    revalidatePath("/database-test");

    return {
      success: true,
      message: `${name} was updated successfully.`,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Update asset failed:", error);

    return {
      success: false,
      message:
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "The asset could not be updated.",
    };
  }
}

FILE: src\app\assets\confirm-delete-asset-button.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import { useRef, useState } from "react";

type ConfirmDeleteAssetButtonProps = {
  assetId: number;
  assetName: string;
  deleteAction: (formData: FormData) => void | Promise<void>;
};

export function ConfirmDeleteAssetButton({
  assetId,
  assetName,
  deleteAction,
}: ConfirmDeleteAssetButtonProps) {
  const dialogRef = useRef<HTMLDialogElement>(null);
  const [isDeleting, setIsDeleting] = useState(false);

  function openDialog() {
    dialogRef.current?.showModal();
  }

  function closeDialog() {
    if (!isDeleting) {
      dialogRef.current?.close();
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={openDialog}
        className="rounded-lg border border-rose-500/20 bg-rose-500/10 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-rose-300 transition hover:bg-rose-500/20"
      >
        Delete
      </button>

      <dialog
        ref={dialogRef}
        onCancel={(event) => {
          if (isDeleting) {
            event.preventDefault();
          }
        }}
        className="m-auto w-[calc(100%-2rem)] max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 p-0 text-slate-100 shadow-2xl backdrop:bg-black/70"
      >
        <div className="p-6">
          <div className="flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300">
            <span className="text-xl">×</span>
          </div>

          <h2 className="mt-5 text-xl font-semibold text-white">
            Delete asset?
          </h2>

          <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-400">
            You are about to permanently delete{" "}
            <strong className="font-semibold text-slate-200">
              {assetName}
            </strong>
            . The asset will be removed from net worth calculations.
          </p>

          <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={closeDialog}
              disabled={isDeleting}
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-700 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>

            <form
              action={async (formData) => {
                setIsDeleting(true);

                try {
                  await deleteAction(formData);
                  dialogRef.current?.close();
                } finally {
                  setIsDeleting(false);
                }
              }}
            >
              <input type="hidden" name="id" value={assetId} />

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={isDeleting}
                className="w-full rounded-xl bg-rose-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-rose-500 disabled:cursor-wait disabled:opacity-60"
              >
                {isDeleting ? "Deleting..." : "Delete permanently"}
              </button>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      </dialog>
    </>
  );
}

FILE: src\app\assets\page.tsx
============================================================
import Link from "next/link";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { AssetForm } from "./asset-form";
import { deleteAsset } from "./actions";
import { ConfirmDeleteAssetButton } from "./confirm-delete-asset-button";

function formatMoney(amountMinor: number | null, currency: string) {
  if (amountMinor === null) {
    return "Not recorded";
  }

  return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
    style: "currency",
    currency,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  }).format(amountMinor / 100);
}

function formatDate(date: Date) {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
    year: "numeric",
    month: "short",
    day: "2-digit",
  }).format(date);
}

function badgeColor(value: string) {
  const colors: Record<string, string> = {
    LOW: "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300",
    MEDIUM: "bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-300",
    HIGH: "bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-300",
    VERY_HIGH: "bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300",
  };

  return (
    colors[value] ?? "bg-slate-700 text-slate-300"
  );
}

async function AssetsTable() {
  const assets = await prisma.asset.findMany({
    orderBy: [
      {
        currency: "asc",
      },
      {
        name: "asc",
      },
    ],
  });

  if (assets.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-700 bg-slate-900/50 p-10 text-center">
        <h2 className="font-semibold text-white">No assets yet</h2>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-400">
          Add the first investment, property, gold holding, or
          fixed-income asset.
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900">
      <div className="overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="min-w-full divide-y divide-slate-800">
          <thead>
            <tr className="text-left text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
              <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">Asset</th>
              <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">Type</th>
              <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">Risk</th>
              <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">
                Valuation date
              </th>
              <th className="px-5 py-4 text-right font-medium">
                Cost basis
              </th>
              <th className="px-5 py-4 text-right font-medium">
                Market value
              </th>
              <th className="px-5 py-4 text-right font-medium">
                Action
                </th>
            </tr>
          </thead>

          <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-800">
            {assets.map((asset) => (
              <tr
                key={asset.id}
                className="transition hover:bg-slate-800/40"
              >
                <td className="px-5 py-4">
                  <p className="font-medium text-white">
                    {asset.name}
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
                    {asset.country ?? "No location"} ·{" "}
                    {asset.currency}
                    {asset.quantity !== null
                      ? ` · Qty ${asset.quantity}`
                      : ""}
                  </p>
                </td>

                <td className="px-5 py-4 text-sm text-slate-300">
                  {asset.assetType.replaceAll("_", " ")}
                </td>

                <td className="px-5 py-4">
                  <span
                    className={`rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${badgeColor(
                      asset.riskLevel
                    )}`}
                  >
                    {asset.riskLevel.replaceAll("_", " ")}
                  </span>
                </td>

                <td className="px-5 py-4 text-sm text-slate-300">
                  {formatDate(asset.valuationDate)}
                </td>

                <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4 text-right text-sm text-slate-400">
                  {formatMoney(
                    asset.costBasisMinor,
                    asset.currency
                  )}
                </td>

                <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4 text-right font-semibold text-white">
                  {formatMoney(
                    asset.marketValueMinor,
                    asset.currency
                  )}
                </td>
                <td className="px-5 py-4 text-right">
                    <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
                    <Link
                        href={`/assets/${asset.id}/edit`}
                        className="rounded-lg border border-blue-500/20 bg-blue-500/10 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-300 hover:bg-blue-500/20"
                    >
                Edit
                </Link>

                    <ConfirmDeleteAssetButton
                        assetId={asset.id}
                        assetName={asset.name}
                        deleteAction={deleteAsset}
                    />
                    </div>
                </td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function AssetsSkeleton() {
  return (
    <div className="animate-pulse overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900">
      {Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, index) => (
        <div
          key={index}
          className="h-20 border-b border-slate-800 last:border-0"
        />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function AssetsPage() {
  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-950 text-slate-100">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1500px] px-6 py-10 lg:px-8">
        <header className="mb-8 flex flex-col justify-between gap-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-end">
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-400">
              Mizan Local
            </p>

            <h1 className="mt-3 text-4xl font-bold text-white">
              Assets
            </h1>

            <p className="mt-3 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-6 text-slate-400">
              Track investments, property equity, gold, fixed income,
              crypto, and funds using confirmed market values.
            </p>
          </div>

          <Link
            href="/"
            className="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-800"
          >
            Back to Dashboard
          </Link>
        </header>

        <section className="grid gap-8 xl:grid-cols-[0.75fr_1.25fr]">
          <div className="h-fit rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-white">
              Add asset
            </h2>

            <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-400">
              Cost basis is historical. Market value is the amount
              included in net worth.
            </p>

            <AssetForm />
          </div>

          <div>
            <div className="mb-4">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-white">
                Existing assets
              </h2>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-400">
                Market values are read directly from local SQLite.
              </p>
            </div>

            <Suspense fallback={<AssetsSkeleton />}>
              <AssetsTable />
            </Suspense>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import {
  useActionState,
  useState,
} from "react";

import {
  updateCashFlow,
  type CashFlowActionState,
} from "@/app/cash-flow/actions";

type EditCashFlowFormProps = {
  cashFlow: {
    id: number;
    name: string;
    flowType: string;
    scope: string;
    currency: string;
    amountMinor: number;
    frequency: string;
    category: string;
    startDate: string;
    endDate: string;
    notes: string | null;
  };
};

const initialState: CashFlowActionState = {
  success: false,
  message: "",
};

function toMajor(amountMinor: number): string {
  return (amountMinor / 100).toFixed(2);
}

export function EditCashFlowForm({
  cashFlow,
}: EditCashFlowFormProps) {
  const [state, formAction, isPending] =
    useActionState(
      updateCashFlow,
      initialState
    );

  const [
    hasUnsavedChanges,
    setHasUnsavedChanges,
  ] = useState(false);

  return (
    <form
      action={(formData) => {
        setHasUnsavedChanges(false);
        formAction(formData);
      }}
      onChange={() => {
        setHasUnsavedChanges(true);
      }}
      className="space-y-5"
    >
      <input
        type="hidden"
        name="id"
        value={cashFlow.id}
      />

      {state.message && !hasUnsavedChanges ? (
        <div
          role="alert"
          aria-live="polite"
          className={`rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm ${
            state.success
              ? "border-emerald-500/20 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300"
              : "border-rose-500/20 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300"
          }`}
        >
          <p>{state.message}</p>

          {state.success ? (
            <Link
              href={`/cash-flow?scope=${cashFlow.scope}`}
              className="mt-3 inline-flex rounded-lg bg-emerald-500/15 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-200 hover:bg-emerald-500/25"
            >
              Back to cash-flow forecast
            </Link>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {hasUnsavedChanges ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-amber-500/20 bg-amber-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-amber-300">
          You have unsaved changes.
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <div>
        <label
          htmlFor="name"
          className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
        >
          Cash-flow name
        </label>

        <input
          id="name"
          name="name"
          required
          defaultValue={cashFlow.name}
          disabled={isPending}
          className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none focus:border-blue-500 disabled:opacity-60"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
        <div>
          <label
            htmlFor="flowType"
            className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
          >
            Type
          </label>

          <select
            id="flowType"
            name="flowType"
            defaultValue={cashFlow.flowType}
            disabled={isPending}
            className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white disabled:opacity-60"
          >
            <option value="INCOME">
              Income
            </option>

            <option value="EXPENSE">
              Expense
            </option>
          </select>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label
            htmlFor="scope"
            className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
          >
            Scope
          </label>

          <select
            id="scope"
            name="scope"
            defaultValue={cashFlow.scope}
            disabled={isPending}
            className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white disabled:opacity-60"
          >
            <option value="EGYPT">
              Egypt
            </option>

            <option value="SAUDI">
              Saudi Arabia
            </option>

            <option value="SHARED">
              Shared / Global
            </option>
          </select>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label
            htmlFor="currency"
            className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
          >
            Currency
          </label>

          <select
            id="currency"
            name="currency"
            defaultValue={cashFlow.currency}

            : "Save changes"}

}

import { notFound } from "next/navigation";

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

import { EditCashFlowForm } from "./edit-cash-flow-form";

export const instant = false;

type EditCashFlowPageProps = {
  params: Promise<{
    id: string;
  }>;
};

function toDateInputValue(
  date: Date | null
): string {
  if (!date) {
    return "";
  }

  return date.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

export default async function EditCashFlowPage({
  params,
}: EditCashFlowPageProps) {
  const { id: rawId } = await params;
  const id = Number(rawId);

  if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
    notFound();
  }

  const cashFlow =
    await prisma.cashFlow.findUnique({
      where: {
        id,
      },
    });

  if (!cashFlow) {
    notFound();
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-950 text-slate-100">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-6 py-10">
        <header className="mb-8">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-400">
            Mizan Local
          </p>

          <h1 className="mt-3 text-4xl font-bold text-white">
            Edit Cash Flow
          </h1>

          <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-400">
            Update the amount, timing, country scope,
            frequency, or classification.
          </p>
        </header>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
          <EditCashFlowForm
            cashFlow={{
              id: cashFlow.id,
              name: cashFlow.name,
              flowType: cashFlow.flowType,
              scope: cashFlow.scope,
              currency: cashFlow.currency,
              amountMinor:
                cashFlow.amountMinor,
              frequency: cashFlow.frequency,
              category: cashFlow.category,
              startDate: toDateInputValue(
                cashFlow.startDate
              ),
              endDate: toDateInputValue(
                cashFlow.endDate
              ),
              notes: cashFlow.notes,
            }}
          />
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: src\app\cash-flow\actions.ts
============================================================
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

export type CashFlowActionState = {
  success: boolean;
  message: string;
};

export type ForecastPlanActionState = {
  success: boolean;
  message: string;
};

function readRequiredText(
  formData: FormData,
  field: string
): string {
  const value = formData.get(field);

  if (
    typeof value !== "string" ||
    value.trim() === ""
  ) {
    throw new Error(`${field} is required.`);
  }

  return value.trim();
}

function readRequiredAmount(
  formData: FormData,
  field: string
): number {
  const rawValue = formData.get(field);

  if (
    typeof rawValue !== "string" ||
    rawValue.trim() === ""
  ) {
    throw new Error(`${field} is required.`);
  }

  const amount = Number(
    rawValue.replaceAll(",", "").trim()
  );

  if (
    !Number.isFinite(amount) ||
    amount <= 0
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must be greater than zero.`
    );
  }

  return amount;
}

function readNonNegativeAmount(
  formData: FormData,
  field: string
): number {
  const rawValue = formData.get(field);

  if (
    typeof rawValue !== "string" ||
    rawValue.trim() === ""
  ) {
    throw new Error(`${field} is required.`);
  }

  const amount = Number(
    rawValue.replaceAll(",", "").trim()
  );

  if (
    !Number.isFinite(amount) ||
    amount < 0
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must be a valid non-negative number.`
    );
  }

  return amount;
}

function readRequiredDate(
  formData: FormData,
  field: string
): Date {
  const rawValue = formData.get(field);

  if (
    typeof rawValue !== "string" ||
    rawValue.trim() === ""
  ) {
    throw new Error(`${field} is required.`);
  }

  const date = new Date(
    `${rawValue}T12:00:00`
  );

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
    throw new Error(`${field} is invalid.`);
  }

  return date;
}

function readOptionalDate(
  formData: FormData,
  field: string
): Date | null {
  const rawValue = formData.get(field);

  if (
    typeof rawValue !== "string" ||
    rawValue.trim() === ""
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  const date = new Date(
    `${rawValue}T12:00:00`
  );

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
    throw new Error(`${field} is invalid.`);
  }

  return date;
}

function readOptionalText(
  formData: FormData,
  field: string
): string | null {
  const value = formData.get(field);

  if (
    typeof value !== "string" ||
    value.trim() === ""
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  return value.trim();
}

function toMinor(amount: number): number {
  return Math.round(amount * 100);
}

function isAllowedFlowType(
  value: string
): boolean {
  return ["INCOME", "EXPENSE"].includes(
    value
  );
}

function isAllowedScope(
  value: string
): boolean {
  return [
    "EGYPT",
    "SAUDI",
    "SHARED",
  ].includes(value);
}

function isAllowedFrequency(
  value: string
): boolean {
  return [
    "MONTHLY",
    "QUARTERLY",
    "SEMIANNUAL",
    "ANNUAL",
    "ONE_TIME",
  ].includes(value);
}

export async function createCashFlow(
  previousState: CashFlowActionState,
  formData: FormData
): Promise<CashFlowActionState> {
  try {
    const name = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "name"
    );

    const flowType = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "flowType"
    );

    const scope = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "scope"
    );

    const currency = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "currency"
    );

    const frequency = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "frequency"
    );

    const category = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "category"
    );

    const amount = readRequiredAmount(
      formData,
      "amount"
    );

    const startDate = readRequiredDate(
      formData,
      "startDate"
    );

    const endDate = readOptionalDate(
      formData,
      "endDate"
    );

    if (!isAllowedFlowType(flowType)) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid cash-flow type.",
      };
    }

    if (!isAllowedScope(scope)) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid cash-flow scope.",
      };
    }

    if (!isAllowedFrequency(frequency)) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid frequency.",
      };
    }

    if (endDate && endDate < startDate) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message:
          "End date cannot be before start date.",
      };
    }

    if (
      frequency === "ONE_TIME" &&
      endDate &&
      endDate.getTime() !==
        startDate.getTime()
    ) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message:
          "A one-time cash flow uses its start date as the occurrence date.",
      };
    }

    const notes = readOptionalText(
      formData,
      "notes"
    );

    await prisma.cashFlow.create({
      data: {
        name,
        flowType,
        scope,
        currency,
        amountMinor: toMinor(amount),
        frequency,
        startDate,
        endDate:
          frequency === "ONE_TIME"
            ? startDate
            : endDate,
        category,
        notes,
      },
    });

    revalidatePath("/");
    revalidatePath("/cash-flow");
    revalidatePath("/database-test");

    return {
      success: true,
      message: `${name} was added successfully.`,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "Create cash flow failed:",
      error
    );

    return {
      success: false,
      message:
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "The cash flow could not be created.",
    };
  }
}

export async function updateCashFlow(
  previousState: CashFlowActionState,
  formData: FormData
): Promise<CashFlowActionState> {
  try {
    const idValue = formData.get("id");

    if (typeof idValue !== "string") {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Cash-flow ID is required.",
      };
    }

    const id = Number(idValue);

    if (
      !Number.isInteger(id) ||
      id <= 0
    ) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid cash-flow ID.",
      };
    }

    const existingCashFlow =
      await prisma.cashFlow.findUnique({
        where: {
          id,
        },
      });

    if (!existingCashFlow) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Cash flow was not found.",
      };
    }

    const name = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "name"
    );

    const flowType = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "flowType"
    );

    const scope = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "scope"
    );

    const currency = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "currency"
    );

    const frequency = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "frequency"
    );

    const category = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "category"
    );

    const amount = readRequiredAmount(
      formData,
      "amount"
    );

    const startDate = readRequiredDate(
      formData,
      "startDate"
    );

    const endDate = readOptionalDate(
      formData,
      "endDate"
    );

    if (!isAllowedFlowType(flowType)) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid cash-flow type.",
      };
    }

    if (!isAllowedScope(scope)) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid cash-flow scope.",
      };
    }

    if (!isAllowedFrequency(frequency)) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid frequency.",
      };
    }

    if (endDate && endDate < startDate) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message:
          "End date cannot be before start date.",
      };
    }

    const notes = readOptionalText(
      formData,
      "notes"
    );

    await prisma.cashFlow.update({
      where: {
        id,
      },
      data: {
        name,
        flowType,
        scope,
        currency,
        amountMinor: toMinor(amount),
        frequency,
        startDate,
        endDate:
          frequency === "ONE_TIME"
            ? startDate
            : endDate,
        category,
        notes,
      },
    });

    revalidatePath("/");
    revalidatePath("/cash-flow");
    revalidatePath(`/cash-flow/${id}/edit`);
    revalidatePath("/database-test");

    return {
      success: true,
      message: `${name} was updated successfully.`,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "Update cash flow failed:",
      error
    );

    return {
      success: false,
      message:
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "The cash flow could not be updated.",
    };
  }
}

export async function deleteCashFlow(
  formData: FormData
) {
  const id = Number(formData.get("id"));

  if (
    !Number.isInteger(id) ||
    id <= 0
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Invalid cash-flow ID."
    );
  }

  await prisma.cashFlow.delete({
    where: {
      id,
    },
  });

  revalidatePath("/");
  revalidatePath("/cash-flow");
  revalidatePath("/database-test");
}

export async function saveForecastPlan(
  previousState: ForecastPlanActionState,
  formData: FormData
): Promise<ForecastPlanActionState> {
  try {
    const name = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "name"
    );

    const forecastYear = Number(
      formData.get("forecastYear")
    );

    const baseCurrency =
      readRequiredText(
        formData,
        "baseCurrency"
      );

    const openingBalance =
      readNonNegativeAmount(
        formData,
        "openingBalance"
      );

    const safetyThreshold =
      readNonNegativeAmount(
        formData,
        "safetyThreshold"
      );

    const notes = readOptionalText(
      formData,
      "notes"
    );

    if (
      !Number.isInteger(forecastYear) ||
      forecastYear < 2000 ||
      forecastYear > 2100
    ) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Forecast year is invalid.",
      };
    }

    const allowedCurrencies = [
      "EGP",
      "SAR",
      "USD",
      "EUR",
      "KWD",
    ];

    if (
      !allowedCurrencies.includes(
        baseCurrency
      )
    ) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message:
          "Forecast base currency is invalid.",
      };
    }

    const savedPlan =
      await prisma.forecastPlan.upsert({
        where: {
          name,
        },
        update: {
          forecastYear,
          baseCurrency,
          openingBalanceMinor:
            toMinor(openingBalance),
          safetyThresholdMinor:
            toMinor(safetyThreshold),
          notes,
        },
        create: {
          name,
          forecastYear,
          baseCurrency,
          openingBalanceMinor:
            toMinor(openingBalance),
          safetyThresholdMinor:
            toMinor(safetyThreshold),
          notes,
        },
      });

    console.log(
      "Forecast plan saved:",
      {
        name: savedPlan.name,
        openingBalanceMinor:
          savedPlan.openingBalanceMinor,
        safetyThresholdMinor:
          savedPlan.safetyThresholdMinor,
      }
    );

    revalidatePath("/cash-flow");
    revalidatePath(
      "/cash-flow?scope=EGYPT"
    );
    revalidatePath(
      "/cash-flow?scope=SAUDI"
    );

    return {
      success: true,
      message:
        `Forecast settings were saved. ` +
        `Safety threshold: ${safetyThreshold.toLocaleString(
          "en-US",
          {
            maximumFractionDigits: 2,
          }
        )} ${baseCurrency}.`,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "Save forecast plan failed:",
      error
    );

    return {
      success: false,
      message:
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "Forecast settings could not be saved.",
    };
  }
}

"use client";

import {
  Bar,
  CartesianGrid,
  ComposedChart,
  Legend,
  Line,
  ResponsiveContainer,
  Tooltip,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
} from "recharts";

type CashFlowChartRow = {
  month: string;
  income: number;
  expenses: number;
  net: number;
  cumulative: number;
};

type CashFlowChartProps = {
  data: CashFlowChartRow[];
  currency: string;
};

function formatCompactAmount(
  value: number,
  currency: string
): string {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
    style: "currency",
    currency,
    notation: "compact",
    maximumFractionDigits: 1,
  }).format(value);
}

function formatFullAmount(
  value: number,
  currency: string
): string {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
    style: "currency",
    currency,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  }).format(value);
}

export function CashFlowChart({
  data,
  currency,
}: CashFlowChartProps) {
  return (
    <div className="h-[420px] w-full">
      <ResponsiveContainer
        width="100%"
        height="100%"
      >
        <ComposedChart
          data={data}
          margin={{
            top: 20,
            right: 24,
            bottom: 10,
            left: 12,
          }}
        >
          <CartesianGrid
            stroke="#1e293b"
            strokeDasharray="4 4"
            vertical={false}
          />

          <XAxis
            dataKey="month"
            stroke="#64748b"
            tick={{
              fill: "#94a3b8",
              fontSize: 12,
            }}
            tickLine={false}
            axisLine={{
              stroke: "#334155",
            }}
          />

          <YAxis
            stroke="#64748b"
            tick={{
              fill: "#94a3b8",
              fontSize: 12,
            }}
            tickFormatter={(value: number) =>
              formatCompactAmount(
                value,
                currency
              )
            }
            tickLine={false}
            axisLine={false}
            width={90}
          />

          <Tooltip
            contentStyle={{
              backgroundColor: "#0f172a",
              border: "1px solid #334155",
              borderRadius: "12px",
              color: "#f8fafc",
            }}
            labelStyle={{
              color: "#f8fafc",
              fontWeight: 600,
            }}
            formatter={(
              value,
              name
            ) => [
              formatFullAmount(
                Number(value ?? 0),
                currency
              ),
              String(name),
            ]}
          />

          <Legend
            wrapperStyle={{
              paddingTop: "18px",
              color: "#cbd5e1",
            }}
          />

          <Bar
            dataKey="income"
            name="Income"
            fill="#10b981"
            radius={[5, 5, 0, 0]}
          />

          <Bar
            dataKey="expenses"
            name="Expenses and commitments"
            fill="#f59e0b"
            radius={[5, 5, 0, 0]}
          />

          <Line
            type="monotone"
            dataKey="net"
            name="Monthly net"
            stroke="#38bdf8"
            strokeWidth={2}
            dot={{
              r: 3,
              fill: "#38bdf8",
            }}
          />

          <Line
            type="monotone"
            dataKey="cumulative"
            name="Closing reserve"
            stroke="#a78bfa"
            strokeWidth={3}
            dot={{
              r: 4,
              fill: "#a78bfa",
            }}
          />
        </ComposedChart>
      </ResponsiveContainer>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src\app\cash-flow\cash-flow-form.tsx

"use client";

import {
  useActionState,
  useEffect,
  useRef,
  useState,
} from "react";

import {
  createCashFlow,
  type CashFlowActionState,
} from "./actions";

type CashFlowFormProps = {
  selectedScope: "EGYPT" | "SAUDI";
};

const initialState: CashFlowActionState = {
  success: false,
  message: "",
};

export function CashFlowForm({
  selectedScope,
}: CashFlowFormProps) {
  const formRef =
    useRef<HTMLFormElement>(null);

  const [flowType, setFlowType] =
    useState<"INCOME" | "EXPENSE">(
      "INCOME"
    );

  const [frequency, setFrequency] =
    useState("MONTHLY");

  const [state, formAction, isPending] =
    useActionState(
      createCashFlow,
      initialState
    );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!state.success) {
      return;
    }

    formRef.current?.reset();
    setFlowType("INCOME");
    setFrequency("MONTHLY");
  }, [state.success]);

  const scopeLabel =
    selectedScope === "SAUDI"
      ? "Saudi Arabia"
      : "Egypt";

  return (
    <form
      ref={formRef}
      action={formAction}
      className="space-y-5"
    >
      {/* Scope is controlled by the active forecast page.
          This prevents accidentally assigning an Egyptian
          expense to the Saudi forecast or vice versa. */}
      <input
        type="hidden"
        name="scope"
        value={selectedScope}
      />

      {state.message ? (
        <div
          role="alert"
          aria-live="polite"
          className={`rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm ${
            state.success
              ? "border-emerald-500/20 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300"
              : "border-rose-500/20 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300"
          }`}
        >
          {state.message}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <div className="rounded-xl border border-blue-500/20 bg-blue-500/10 p-4">
        <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-blue-300">
          Forecast scope
        </p>

        <p className="mt-1 font-semibold text-blue-100">
          {scopeLabel}
        </p>

        <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-blue-200/70">
          This cash flow will be assigned
          automatically to the active forecast.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div>
        <label
          htmlFor="cashFlowName"
          className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
        >
          Cash-flow name
        </label>

        <input
          id="cashFlowName"
          name="name"
          required
          disabled={isPending}
          placeholder={
            selectedScope === "SAUDI"
              ? "Saudi salary"
              : "Apartment rent"
          }
          className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none placeholder:text-slate-600 focus:border-blue-500 disabled:opacity-60"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <div>
          <label
            htmlFor="flowType"
            className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
          >
            Type
          </label>

          <select
            id="flowType"
            name="flowType"
            value={flowType}
            onChange={(event) => {
              setFlowType(
                event.target.value as
                  | "INCOME"
                  | "EXPENSE"
              );
            }}
            disabled={isPending}

            Currency

            id="currency"
