            defaultValue={

                ? "SAR"

FILE: src\app\cash-flow\confirm-delete-cash-flow-button.tsx

"use client";

import { useRef, useState } from "react";

type ConfirmDeleteCashFlowButtonProps = {
  cashFlowId: number;
  cashFlowName: string;
  cashFlowLabel: string;
  deleteAction: (
    formData: FormData
  ) => void | Promise<void>;
};

export function ConfirmDeleteCashFlowButton({
  cashFlowId,
  cashFlowName,
  cashFlowLabel,
  deleteAction,
}: ConfirmDeleteCashFlowButtonProps) {
  const dialogRef =
    useRef<HTMLDialogElement>(null);

  const [isDeleting, setIsDeleting] =
    useState(false);

  function openDialog() {
    dialogRef.current?.showModal();
  }

  function closeDialog() {
    if (!isDeleting) {
      dialogRef.current?.close();
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={openDialog}
        className="text-sm font-semibold text-rose-300 transition hover:text-rose-200"
      >
        Delete
      </button>

      <dialog
        ref={dialogRef}
        onCancel={(event) => {
          if (isDeleting) {
            event.preventDefault();
          }
        }}
        className="m-auto w-[calc(100%-2rem)] max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 p-0 text-slate-100 shadow-2xl backdrop:bg-black/70"
      >
        <div className="p-6">
          <div className="flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-rose-500/10 text-xl text-rose-300">
            ×
          </div>

          <h2 className="mt-5 text-xl font-semibold text-white">
            Delete cash flow?
          </h2>

          <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-400">
            You are about to permanently delete:
          </p>

          <div className="mt-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4">
            <p className="font-semibold text-slate-200">
              {cashFlowName}
            </p>

            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
              {cashFlowLabel}
            </p>
          </div>

          <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
            The monthly forecast and first deficit month will
            be recalculated automatically.
          </p>

          <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={closeDialog}
              disabled={isDeleting}
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-200 transition hover:bg-slate-700 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>

            <form
              action={async (formData) => {
                setIsDeleting(true);

                try {
                  await deleteAction(formData);
                  dialogRef.current?.close();
                } finally {
                  setIsDeleting(false);
                }
              }}
            >
              <input
                type="hidden"
                name="id"
                value={cashFlowId}
              />

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={isDeleting}
                className="w-full rounded-xl bg-rose-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-rose-500 disabled:cursor-wait disabled:opacity-60"
              >
                {isDeleting
                  ? "Deleting..."
                  : "Delete permanently"}
              </button>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      </dialog>
    </>
  );
}

"use client";

import {
  useActionState,
  useEffect,
  useState,
} from "react";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";

import {
  saveForecastPlan,
  type ForecastPlanActionState,
} from "./actions";

type ForecastSettingsFormProps = {
  plan: {
    name: string;
    forecastYear: number;
    baseCurrency: string;
    openingBalanceMinor: number;
    safetyThresholdMinor: number;
    notes: string | null;
  };
};

const initialState: ForecastPlanActionState = {
  success: false,
  message: "",
};

function toMajor(
  amountMinor: number
): string {
  return (
    amountMinor / 100
  ).toFixed(2);
}

export function ForecastSettingsForm({
  plan,
}: ForecastSettingsFormProps) {
  const router = useRouter();

  const [
    hasUnsavedChanges,
    setHasUnsavedChanges,
  ] = useState(false);

  const [state, formAction, isPending] =
    useActionState(
      saveForecastPlan,
      initialState
    );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (state.success) {
      setHasUnsavedChanges(false);
      router.refresh();
    }
  }, [state.success, router]);

  return (
    <form
      action={(formData) => {
        setHasUnsavedChanges(false);
        formAction(formData);
      }}
      onChange={() => {
        setHasUnsavedChanges(true);
      }}
      className="space-y-5"
    >
      {state.message &&
      !hasUnsavedChanges ? (
        <div
          role="alert"
          aria-live="polite"
          className={`rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm ${
            state.success
              ? "border-emerald-500/20 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300"
              : "border-rose-500/20 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300"
          }`}
        >
          {state.message}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {hasUnsavedChanges ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-amber-500/20 bg-amber-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-amber-300">
          You have unsaved forecast changes.
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <div>
        <label
          htmlFor="forecastName"
          className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
        >
          Forecast name
        </label>

        <input
          id="forecastName"
          name="name"
          required
          readOnly
          value={plan.name}
          className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-4 py-3 text-slate-400"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <div>
          <label
            htmlFor="forecastYear"
            className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
          >
            Forecast year
          </label>

          <input
            id="forecastYear"
            name="forecastYear"
            type="number"
            min="2000"
            max="2100"
            required
            defaultValue={
              plan.forecastYear
            }
            disabled={isPending}
            className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none focus:border-blue-500 disabled:opacity-60"
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label
            htmlFor="baseCurrency"
            className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
          >
            Base currency
          </label>

          <select
            id="baseCurrency"
            name="baseCurrency"
            defaultValue={
              plan.baseCurrency
            }
            disabled={isPending}
            className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none focus:border-blue-500 disabled:opacity-60"
          >
            <option value="EGP">
              EGP
            </option>

            <option value="SAR">
              SAR
            </option>

            <option value="USD">
              USD
            </option>

            <option value="EUR">
              EUR
            </option>

            <option value="KWD">
              KWD
            </option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div>
        <label
          htmlFor="openingBalance"
          className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
        >
          Opening reserve balance
        </label>

        <input
          id="openingBalance"
          name="openingBalance"
          type="number"
          min="0"
          step="0.01"
          required
          defaultValue={toMajor(
            plan.openingBalanceMinor
          )}
          disabled={isPending}
          className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none focus:border-blue-500 disabled:opacity-60"
        />

        <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
          Confirmed funds available at the
          beginning of the forecast. Do not
          allocate the same funds to another
          forecast.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div>
        <label
          htmlFor="safetyThreshold"
          className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
        >
          Safety threshold
        </label>

        <input
          id="safetyThreshold"
          name="safetyThreshold"
          type="number"
          min="0"
          step="0.01"
          required
          defaultValue={toMajor(
            plan.safetyThresholdMinor
          )}
          disabled={isPending}
          className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none focus:border-blue-500 disabled:opacity-60"
        />

        <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
          The forecast status becomes WATCH
          if a projected closing reserve falls
          below this amount.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div>
        <label
          htmlFor="forecastNotes"
          className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
        >
          Notes
        </label>

        <textarea
          id="forecastNotes"
          name="notes"
          rows={4}
          defaultValue={
            plan.notes ?? ""
          }
          disabled={isPending}
          className="mt-2 w-full resize-none rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none focus:border-blue-500 disabled:opacity-60"
        />
      </div>

      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={isPending}
        className="w-full rounded-xl bg-violet-600 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-violet-500 disabled:cursor-wait disabled:bg-violet-800 disabled:text-slate-300"
      >
        {isPending
          ? "Saving settings..."
          : "Save forecast settings"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}

FILE: src\app\cash-flow\page.tsx
============================================================
import Link from "next/link";

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

import { deleteCashFlow } from "./actions";
import { CashFlowChart } from "./cash-flow-chart";
import { CashFlowForm } from "./cash-flow-form";
import { ConfirmDeleteCashFlowButton } from "./confirm-delete-cash-flow-button";
import { ForecastSettingsForm } from "./forecast-settings-form";

// This route reads dynamic URL parameters and live SQLite data.
// It is intentionally allowed to block instead of being validated
// as an instant navigation by Next.js Cache Components.
export const instant = false;

type CashFlowPageProps = {
  searchParams: Promise<{
    scope?: string;
  }>;
};

type ForecastScope = "EGYPT" | "SAUDI";

type ForecastStatus =
  | "INCOMPLETE"
  | "UNDERFUNDED"
  | "WATCH"
  | "SAFE";

type MonthRow = {
  key: string;
  label: string;
  shortLabel: string;
  incomeMinor: number;
  recurringExpensesMinor: number;
  liabilityPaymentsMinor: number;
};

function formatMoney(
  amountMinor: number,
  currency: string
): string {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
    style: "currency",
    currency,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  }).format(amountMinor / 100);
}

function occursInMonth(
  flow: {
    frequency: string;
    startDate: Date;
    endDate: Date | null;
  },
  year: number,
  monthIndex: number
): boolean {
  const monthStart = new Date(
    year,
    monthIndex,
    1,
    12
  );

  const monthEnd = new Date(
    year,
    monthIndex + 1,
    0,
    12
  );

  if (flow.startDate > monthEnd) {
    return false;
  }

  if (
    flow.endDate &&
    flow.endDate < monthStart
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  const startYear =
    flow.startDate.getFullYear();

  const startMonth =
    flow.startDate.getMonth();

  const monthDifference =
    (year - startYear) * 12 +
    (monthIndex - startMonth);

  if (monthDifference < 0) {
    return false;
  }

  if (flow.frequency === "ONE_TIME") {
    return (
      year === startYear &&
      monthIndex === startMonth
    );
  }

  const frequencySteps: Record<
    string,
    number
  > = {
    MONTHLY: 1,
    QUARTERLY: 3,
    SEMIANNUAL: 6,
    ANNUAL: 12,
  };

  const step =
    frequencySteps[flow.frequency];

  if (!step) {
    return false;
  }

  return monthDifference % step === 0;
}

export default async function CashFlowPage({
  searchParams,
}: CashFlowPageProps) {
  const { scope: rawScope } =
    await searchParams;

  const selectedScope: ForecastScope =
    rawScope === "SAUDI"
      ? "SAUDI"
      : "EGYPT";

  const isSaudi =
    selectedScope === "SAUDI";

  const locationName = isSaudi
    ? "Saudi Arabia"
    : "Egypt";

  const planName = isSaudi
    ? "Saudi 2027"
    : "Egypt 2027";

  // These defaults are used only if no saved ForecastPlan exists.
  // Values are stored in minor units: halalas for SAR and piastres for EGP.
  const defaultPlan = isSaudi
    ? {
        name: "Saudi 2027",
        forecastYear: 2027,
        baseCurrency: "SAR",
        openingBalanceMinor: 0,
        safetyThresholdMinor:
          10_000_000,
        notes:
          "Enter the confirmed Saudi opening balance allocated to this forecast.",
      }
    : {
        name: "Egypt 2027",
        forecastYear: 2027,
        baseCurrency: "EGP",
        openingBalanceMinor:
          152_522_942,
        safetyThresholdMinor:
          20_000_000,
        notes:
          "Bank USD resources and AZ Halan assigned to Egypt 2027.",
      };

  const savedPlan =
    await prisma.forecastPlan.findUnique({
      where: {
        name: planName,
      },
    });

  const effectivePlan =
    savedPlan ?? defaultPlan;

  const forecastYear =
    effectivePlan.forecastYear;

  const [
    cashFlows,
    scheduledPayments,
    exchangeRates,
  ] = await Promise.all([
    prisma.cashFlow.findMany({
      where: {
        scope: {
          in: [
            selectedScope,
            "SHARED",
          ],
        },
      },
      orderBy: [
        {
          flowType: "asc",
        },
        {
          name: "asc",
        },
      ],
    }),

    prisma.liabilityPayment.findMany({
      where: {
        status: {
          in: [
            "PLANNED",
            "DEFERRED",
          ],
        },
        paymentDate: {
          gte: new Date(
            `${forecastYear}-01-01T00:00:00`
          ),
          lte: new Date(
            `${forecastYear}-12-31T23:59:59`
          ),
        },
        liability: {
          country: isSaudi
            ? "Saudi Arabia"
            : "Egypt",
        },
      },
      include: {
        liability: true,
      },
      orderBy: {
        paymentDate: "asc",
      },
    }),

    prisma.exchangeRate.findMany({
      where: {
        toCurrency: "EGP",
      },
      orderBy: {
        rateDate: "desc",
      },
    }),
  ]);

  // The stored exchange rates use EGP as the common intermediary.
  // Example: USD -> EGP -> SAR when the plan base currency is SAR.
  const rateMap: Record<
    string,
    number
  > = {
    EGP: 1,
  };

  for (const rate of exchangeRates) {
    if (!rateMap[rate.fromCurrency]) {
      rateMap[rate.fromCurrency] =
        rate.rate;
    }
  }

  function convertToBaseMinor(
    amountMinor: number,
    fromCurrency: string
  ): number {
    const baseCurrency =
      effectivePlan.baseCurrency;

    if (fromCurrency === baseCurrency) {
      return amountMinor;
    }

    const fromToEgpRate =
      fromCurrency === "EGP"
        ? 1
        : rateMap[fromCurrency];

    const baseToEgpRate =
      baseCurrency === "EGP"
        ? 1
        : rateMap[baseCurrency];

    if (
      !fromToEgpRate ||
      !baseToEgpRate
    ) {
      return 0;
    }

    const amountInEgpMinor =
      amountMinor * fromToEgpRate;

    return Math.round(
      amountInEgpMinor /
        baseToEgpRate
    );
  }

  const months: MonthRow[] =
    Array.from({
      length: 12,
    }).map((_, monthIndex) => {
      const date = new Date(
        forecastYear,
        monthIndex,
        1
      );

      return {
        key: `${forecastYear}-${String(
          monthIndex + 1
        ).padStart(2, "0")}`,

        label:
          new Intl.DateTimeFormat(
            "en-US",
            {
              month: "long",
              year: "numeric",
            }
          ).format(date),

        shortLabel:
          new Intl.DateTimeFormat(
            "en-US",
            {
              month: "short",
            }
          ).format(date),

        incomeMinor: 0,
        recurringExpensesMinor: 0,
        liabilityPaymentsMinor: 0,
      };
    });

  // Apply every recurring or one-time cash flow
  // to the months in which it occurs.
  for (const flow of cashFlows) {
    for (
      let monthIndex = 0;
      monthIndex < 12;
      monthIndex += 1
    ) {
      if (
        !occursInMonth(
          flow,
          forecastYear,
          monthIndex
        )
      ) {
        continue;
      }

      const convertedMinor =
        convertToBaseMinor(
          flow.amountMinor,
          flow.currency
        );

      if (flow.flowType === "INCOME") {
        months[
          monthIndex
        ].incomeMinor +=
          convertedMinor;
      } else {
        months[
          monthIndex
        ].recurringExpensesMinor +=
          convertedMinor;
      }
    }
  }

  // Liability payments are kept separate from living expenses
  // so property installments remain visible as commitments.
  for (const payment of scheduledPayments) {
    const paymentYear =
      payment.paymentDate.getFullYear();

    const monthIndex =
      payment.paymentDate.getMonth();

    if (
      paymentYear !== forecastYear ||
      monthIndex < 0 ||
      monthIndex > 11
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    months[
      monthIndex
    ].liabilityPaymentsMinor +=
      convertToBaseMinor(
        payment.amountMinor,
        payment.liability.currency
      );
  }

  // The forecast starts from the reserve assigned specifically
  // to this plan, not from total household liquidity.
  let cumulativeMinor =
    effectivePlan.openingBalanceMinor;

  let firstDeficitMonth:
    | string
    | null = null;

  const forecast = months.map(
    (month) => {
      const totalExpensesMinor =
        month.recurringExpensesMinor +
        month.liabilityPaymentsMinor;

      const netMinor =
        month.incomeMinor -
        totalExpensesMinor;

      cumulativeMinor += netMinor;

      if (
        cumulativeMinor < 0 &&
        firstDeficitMonth === null
      ) {
        firstDeficitMonth =
          month.label;
      }

      return {
        ...month,
        totalExpensesMinor,
        netMinor,
        cumulativeMinor,
      };
    }
  );

  const chartData = forecast.map(
    (month) => ({
      month: month.shortLabel,
      income:
        month.incomeMinor / 100,
      expenses:
        month.totalExpensesMinor /
        100,
      net: month.netMinor / 100,
      cumulative:
        month.cumulativeMinor / 100,
    })
  );

  const annualIncomeMinor =
    forecast.reduce(
      (total, month) =>
        total + month.incomeMinor,
      0
    );

  const annualExpensesMinor =
    forecast.reduce(
      (total, month) =>
        total +
        month.totalExpensesMinor,
      0
    );

  const annualNetMinor =
    annualIncomeMinor -
    annualExpensesMinor;

  const minimumProjectedBalanceMinor =
    forecast.length > 0
      ? Math.min(
          ...forecast.map(
            (month) =>
              month.cumulativeMinor
          )
        )
      : effectivePlan.openingBalanceMinor;

  const closingBalanceMinor =
    forecast.at(-1)
      ?.cumulativeMinor ??
    effectivePlan.openingBalanceMinor;

  const safetyReserveMinor =
    effectivePlan.safetyThresholdMinor;

  const negativeNetMonths =
    forecast.filter(
      (month) => month.netMinor < 0
    );

  const negativeNetMonthCount =
    negativeNetMonths.length;

  const worstMonth =
    forecast.length > 0
      ? forecast.reduce(
          (worst, month) =>
            month.netMinor <
            worst.netMinor
              ? month
              : worst
        )
      : null;

  const firstBelowSafetyMonth =
    forecast.find(
      (month) =>
        month.cumulativeMinor <
        safetyReserveMinor
    ) ?? null;

  const fundingGapMinor = Math.max(
    -minimumProjectedBalanceMinor,
    0
  );

  const reserveBufferMinor =
    minimumProjectedBalanceMinor -
    safetyReserveMinor;

  // A forecast must contain both income and expenses before
  // SAFE or WATCH can be treated as a meaningful assessment.
  const incomeFlowCount =
    cashFlows.filter(
      (flow) =>
        flow.flowType === "INCOME"
    ).length;

  const expenseFlowCount =
    cashFlows.filter(
      (flow) =>
        flow.flowType === "EXPENSE"
    ).length;

  const scheduledPaymentCount =
    scheduledPayments.length;

  const hasOpeningReserve =
    effectivePlan.openingBalanceMinor >
    0;

  const missingDataItems: string[] =
    [];

  if (!hasOpeningReserve) {
    missingDataItems.push(
      "Opening reserve has not been entered."
    );
  }

  if (incomeFlowCount === 0) {
    missingDataItems.push(
      "No recurring or one-time income has been entered."
    );
  }

  if (expenseFlowCount === 0) {
    missingDataItems.push(
      "No living or recurring expenses have been entered."
    );
  }

  if (
    !isSaudi &&
    scheduledPaymentCount === 0
  ) {
    missingDataItems.push(
      "No Egyptian property or contractual payments are scheduled."
    );
  }

  const forecastIsComplete =
    missingDataItems.length === 0;

  const forecastStatus: ForecastStatus =
    !forecastIsComplete
      ? "INCOMPLETE"
      : fundingGapMinor > 0
        ? "UNDERFUNDED"
        : firstBelowSafetyMonth
          ? "WATCH"
          : "SAFE";

  const healthColors: Record<
    ForecastStatus,
    {
      section: string;
      badge: string;
    }
  > = {
    INCOMPLETE: {
      section:
        "border-blue-500/30 bg-blue-500/10",
      badge:
        "bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-200",
    },
    UNDERFUNDED: {
      section:
        "border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-500/10",
      badge:
        "bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-200",
    },
    WATCH: {
      section:
        "border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10",
      badge:
        "bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-200",
    },
    SAFE: {
      section:
        "border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10",
      badge:
        "bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-200",
    },
  };

  const healthDescription =
    forecastStatus === "INCOMPLETE"
      ? "The forecast does not yet contain enough data for a reliable SAFE, WATCH, or UNDERFUNDED assessment."
      : forecastStatus ===
          "UNDERFUNDED"
        ? "The projected reserve becomes negative. Additional funding or lower outflows are required."
        : forecastStatus === "WATCH"
          ? "The reserve remains positive but falls below the saved safety threshold."
          : "The projected reserve remains above the saved safety threshold throughout the forecast.";

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-950 text-slate-100">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1500px] px-6 py-10">
        <header className="mb-8 flex flex-col justify-between gap-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-end">
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-400">
              Mizan Local
            </p>

            <h1 className="mt-3 text-4xl font-bold text-white">
              {locationName}{" "}
              {forecastYear} Cash Flow
              Forecast
            </h1>

            <p className="mt-3 max-w-3xl text-sm leading-6 text-slate-400">
              {isSaudi
                ? "Monthly Saudi income, living costs, relocation costs, and local commitments."
                : "Monthly Egyptian income, family expenses, and scheduled property commitments."}
            </p>

            <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
              <Link
                href="/cash-flow?scope=EGYPT"
                className={`rounded-xl border px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold transition ${
                  !isSaudi
                    ? "border-blue-500 bg-blue-500/15 text-blue-200"
                    : "border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-800"
                }`}
              >
                Egypt Forecast
              </Link>

              <Link
                href="/cash-flow?scope=SAUDI"
                className={`rounded-xl border px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold transition ${
                  isSaudi
                    ? "border-blue-500 bg-blue-500/15 text-blue-200"
                    : "border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-800"
                }`}
              >
                Saudi Forecast
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>

          <Link
            href="/"
            className="rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-slate-200 transition hover:bg-slate-800"
          >
            Back to Dashboard
          </Link>
        </header>

        <section className="mb-8 grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
          <SummaryCard
            label="Opening reserve"
            value={formatMoney(
              effectivePlan.openingBalanceMinor,
              effectivePlan.baseCurrency
            )}
          />

          <SummaryCard
            label="Forecast year"
            value={String(
              forecastYear
            )}
          />

          <SummaryCard
            label="Base currency"
            value={
              effectivePlan.baseCurrency
            }
          />
        </section>

        <section className="mb-8 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">
            First cumulative deficit
            month
          </p>

          <p
            className={`mt-2 text-2xl font-bold ${
              firstDeficitMonth
                ? "text-rose-300"
                : "text-emerald-300"
            }`}
          >
            {firstDeficitMonth ??
              `No cumulative deficit in ${forecastYear}`}
          </p>

          <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
            Includes the saved opening
            reserve, applicable cash
            flows, and scheduled
            commitments.
          </p>
        </section>

        <section className="mb-8 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-5">
          <SummaryCard
            label="Annual income"
            value={formatMoney(
              annualIncomeMinor,
              effectivePlan.baseCurrency
            )}
            valueClassName="text-emerald-300"
          />

          <SummaryCard
            label="Annual outflows"
            value={formatMoney(
              annualExpensesMinor,
              effectivePlan.baseCurrency
            )}
            valueClassName="text-amber-300"
          />

          <SummaryCard
            label="Annual net"
            value={formatMoney(
              annualNetMinor,
              effectivePlan.baseCurrency
            )}
            valueClassName={
              annualNetMinor < 0
                ? "text-rose-300"
                : "text-emerald-300"
            }
          />

          <SummaryCard
            label="Lowest closing reserve"
            value={formatMoney(
              minimumProjectedBalanceMinor,
              effectivePlan.baseCurrency
            )}
            valueClassName={
              minimumProjectedBalanceMinor <
              0
                ? "text-rose-300"
                : "text-white"
            }
          />

          <SummaryCard
            label="Year-end closing reserve"
            value={formatMoney(
              closingBalanceMinor,
              effectivePlan.baseCurrency
            )}
            valueClassName={
              closingBalanceMinor < 0
                ? "text-rose-300"
                : "text-violet-300"
            }
          />
        </section>

        <section
          className={`mb-8 rounded-2xl border p-6 ${healthColors[forecastStatus].section}`}
        >
          <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-5 lg:flex-row lg:items-start">
            <div>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold text-white">
                  Forecast health
                </h2>

                <span
                  className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold ${healthColors[forecastStatus].badge}`}
                >
                  {forecastStatus}
                </span>
              </div>

              <p className="mt-3 max-w-3xl text-sm leading-6 text-slate-300">
                {healthDescription}
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-slate-950/40 px-4 py-3">
              <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-400">
                Safety threshold
              </p>

              <p className="mt-1 font-semibold text-white">
                {formatMoney(
                  safetyReserveMinor,
                  effectivePlan.baseCurrency
                )}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {missingDataItems.length >
          0 ? (
            <div className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-blue-400/20 bg-slate-950/40 p-4">
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-blue-200">
                Complete these items
                before relying on the
                forecast:
              </p>

              <ul className="mt-3 space-y-2 text-sm text-slate-300">
                {missingDataItems.map(
                  (item) => (
                    <li
                      key={item}
                      className="flex gap-2"
                    >
                      <span className="text-blue-300">
                        •
                      </span>

                      <span>
                        {item}
                      </span>
                    </li>
                  )
                )}
              </ul>
            </div>
          ) : null}

          <div className="mt-6 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-5">
            <HealthMetric
              label="Income flows"
              value={String(
                incomeFlowCount
              )}
              className={
                incomeFlowCount > 0
                  ? "text-emerald-300"
                  : "text-blue-300"
              }
            />

            <HealthMetric
              label="Expense flows"
              value={String(
                expenseFlowCount
              )}
              className={
                expenseFlowCount > 0
                  ? "text-emerald-300"
                  : "text-blue-300"
              }
            />

            <HealthMetric
              label="Scheduled payments"
              value={String(
                scheduledPaymentCount
              )}
            />

            <HealthMetric
              label="Negative months"
              value={String(
                negativeNetMonthCount
              )}
            />

            <HealthMetric
              label="Worst month"
              value={
                worstMonth?.label ??
                "None"
              }
              secondary={formatMoney(
                worstMonth?.netMinor ??
                  0,
                effectivePlan.baseCurrency
              )}
              className={
                (worstMonth?.netMinor ??
                  0) < 0
                  ? "text-rose-300"
                  : "text-emerald-300"
              }
            />

            <HealthMetric
              label="First below safety"
              value={
                firstBelowSafetyMonth
                  ?.label ??
                "Not reached"
              }
            />

            <HealthMetric
              label="Funding gap"
              value={formatMoney(
                fundingGapMinor,
                effectivePlan.baseCurrency
              )}
              className={
                fundingGapMinor > 0
                  ? "text-rose-300"
                  : "text-emerald-300"
              }
            />

            <HealthMetric
              label="Buffer vs safety"
              value={formatMoney(
                reserveBufferMinor,
                effectivePlan.baseCurrency
              )}
              className={
                reserveBufferMinor < 0
                  ? "text-amber-300"
                  : "text-emerald-300"
              }
            />

            <HealthMetric
              label="Opening reserve"
              value={
                hasOpeningReserve
                  ? "Entered"
                  : "Missing"
              }
              className={
                hasOpeningReserve
                  ? "text-emerald-300"
                  : "text-blue-300"
              }
            />
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="mb-8 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
          <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-start">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-white">
                Monthly cash-flow
                trajectory
              </h2>

              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-400">
                Income, expenses,
                monthly movement, and
                projected closing
                reserve.
              </p>
            </div>

            <span className="rounded-full bg-violet-500/10 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-violet-300">
              {
                effectivePlan.baseCurrency
              }{" "}
              | {forecastYear}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6">
            <CashFlowChart
              data={chartData}
              currency={
                effectivePlan.baseCurrency
              }
            />
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="grid items-start gap-8 xl:grid-cols-[0.7fr_1.3fr]">
          <div className="space-y-6">
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-white">
                Forecast settings
              </h2>

              <div className="mt-6">
                <ForecastSettingsForm
                  plan={
                    effectivePlan
                  }
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-white">
                Add recurring cash flow
              </h2>

              <div className="mt-6">
                <CashFlowForm
                  selectedScope={
                    selectedScope
                  }
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-6">
            <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900">
              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                <table className="min-w-full divide-y divide-slate-800">
                  <thead>
                    <tr className="text-left text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                      <th className="px-4 py-4">
                        Month
                      </th>

                      <th className="px-4 py-4 text-right">
                        Income
                      </th>

                      <th className="px-4 py-4 text-right">
                        Living /
                        recurring
                      </th>

                      <th className="px-4 py-4 text-right">
                        Commitments
                      </th>

                      <th className="px-4 py-4 text-right">
                        Net
                      </th>

                      <th className="px-4 py-4 text-right">
                        Cumulative
                      </th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>

                  <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-800">
                    {forecast.map(
                      (month) => (
                        <tr
                          key={
                            month.key
                          }
                          className="transition hover:bg-slate-800/40"
                        >
                          <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-4 font-medium">
                            {
                              month.label
                            }
                          </td>

                          <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-4 text-right text-emerald-300">
                            {formatMoney(
                              month.incomeMinor,
                              effectivePlan.baseCurrency
                            )}
                          </td>

                          <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-4 text-right">
                            {formatMoney(
                              month.recurringExpensesMinor,
                              effectivePlan.baseCurrency
                            )}
                          </td>

                          <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-4 text-right text-amber-300">
                            {formatMoney(
                              month.liabilityPaymentsMinor,
                              effectivePlan.baseCurrency
                            )}
                          </td>

                          <td
                            className={`whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-4 text-right font-semibold ${
                              month.netMinor <
                              0
                                ? "text-rose-300"
                                : "text-emerald-300"
                            }`}
                          >
                            {formatMoney(
                              month.netMinor,
                              effectivePlan.baseCurrency
                            )}
                          </td>

                          <td
                            className={`whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-4 text-right font-semibold ${
                              month.cumulativeMinor <
                              0
                                ? "text-rose-300"
                                : "text-white"
                            }`}
                          >
                            {formatMoney(
                              month.cumulativeMinor,
                              effectivePlan.baseCurrency
                            )}
                          </td>
                        </tr>
                      )
                    )}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900">
              <div className="border-b border-slate-800 px-5 py-4">
                <h2 className="font-semibold text-white">
                  Entered cash flows
                </h2>

                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-400">
                  Only flows assigned
                  to {locationName} or
                  Shared are shown.
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="divide-y divide-slate-800">
                {cashFlows.length ===
                0 ? (
                  <p className="px-5 py-8 text-sm text-slate-400">
                    No income or
                    expenses have been
                    entered for this
                    forecast.
                  </p>
                ) : (
                  cashFlows.map(
                    (flow) => (
                      <div
                        key={
                          flow.id
                        }
                        className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 px-5 py-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center"
                      >
                        <div>
                          <p className="font-medium text-white">
                            {
                              flow.name
                            }
                          </p>

                          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
                            {
                              flow.flowType
                            }{" "}
                            |{" "}
                            {
                              flow.scope
                            }{" "}
                            |{" "}
                            {
                              flow.frequency
                            }{" "}
                            |{" "}
                            {
                              flow.currency
                            }
                          </p>
                        </div>

                        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4">
                          <span
                            className={
                              flow.flowType ===
                              "INCOME"
                                ? "font-semibold text-emerald-300"
                                : "font-semibold text-rose-300"
                            }
                          >
                            {formatMoney(
                              flow.amountMinor,
                              flow.currency
                            )}
                          </span>

                          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                            <Link
                              href={`/cash-flow/${flow.id}/edit`}
                              className="text-sm font-semibold text-blue-300 transition hover:text-blue-200"
                            >
                              Edit
                            </Link>

                            <ConfirmDeleteCashFlowButton
                              cashFlowId={
                                flow.id
                              }
                              cashFlowName={
                                flow.name
                              }
                              cashFlowLabel={`${flow.flowType} | ${flow.scope} | ${flow.frequency} | ${formatMoney(
                                flow.amountMinor,
                                flow.currency
                              )}`}
                              deleteAction={
                                deleteCashFlow
                              }
                            />
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )
                  )
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

function SummaryCard({
  label,
  value,
  valueClassName = "text-white",
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  valueClassName?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-5">
      <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">
        {label}
      </p>

      <p
        className={`mt-2 text-lg font-bold ${valueClassName}`}
      >
        {value}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

function HealthMetric({
  label,
  value,
  secondary,
  className = "text-white",
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  secondary?: string;
  className?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-slate-950/40 p-4">
      <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-400">
        {label}
      </p>

      <p
        className={`mt-2 font-bold ${className}`}
      >
        {value}
      </p>

      {secondary ? (
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-300">
          {secondary}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

export default async function DatabaseTestPage() {

    accounts,
    assets,
    liabilities,
    cashFlows,
    goals,
    exchangeRates,
    snapshots,
  ] = await Promise.all([
    prisma.account.count(),
    prisma.asset.count(),
    prisma.liability.count(),
    prisma.cashFlow.count(),
    prisma.goal.count(),
    prisma.exchangeRate.count(),
    prisma.snapshot.count(),
  ]);

  const tables = [
    { name: "Accounts", count: accounts },
    { name: "Assets", count: assets },
    { name: "Liabilities", count: liabilities },
    { name: "Cash Flows", count: cashFlows },
    { name: "Goals", count: goals },
    { name: "Exchange Rates", count: exchangeRates },
    { name: "Snapshots", count: snapshots },
  ];

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-950 p-8 text-white">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl">
        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-emerald-400">

        </p>

        </h1>

        <p className="mt-3 text-slate-400">
          Prisma is connected to the local SQLite database.
        </p>

          {tables.map((table) => (
            <div
              key={table.name}
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-5"
            >
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">{table.name}</p>
              <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-semibold">{table.count}</p>

      </div>

"use client";

  useState,

  updateInvestment,
  type InvestmentActionState,
} from "@/app/investments/actions";

type EditInvestmentFormProps = {
  holding: {
    id: number;
    symbol: string;
    name: string;
    assetClass: string;
    market: string;
    quoteCurrency: string;
    priceProvider: string;
    providerSymbol: string | null;
    country: string | null;
    sector: string | null;
    accountName: string | null;
    quantityText: string;
    averageCostMinor: number;
    latestPriceMinor: number | null;
    feesMinor: number;
    acquisitionDate: string;
    priceDate: string;
    riskLevel: string;
    liquidityClass: string;
    includedInNetWorth: boolean;
    notes: string | null;
  };
};

const initialState:
  InvestmentActionState = {
    success: false,
    message: "",
  };

function toMajor(
  amountMinor: number | null
): string {
  if (amountMinor === null) {
    return "";
  }

  return (
    amountMinor / 100
  ).toFixed(4);
}

export function EditInvestmentForm({
  holding,

  const [state, formAction, isPending] =

      updateInvestment,
      initialState

    hasUnsavedChanges,
    setHasUnsavedChanges,
  ] = useState(false);

  const [
    selectedProvider,
    setSelectedProvider,
  ] = useState(
    holding.priceProvider
  );

  return (
    <form
      action={(formData) => {
        setHasUnsavedChanges(false);
        formAction(formData);
      }}
      onChange={() => {
        setHasUnsavedChanges(true);
      }}

        type="hidden"

function Field({
  id,
  label,
  type = "text",
  required = false,
  disabled = false,
  placeholder,
  defaultValue,
  step,
  min,
}: {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  type?: string;
  required?: boolean;
  disabled?: boolean;
  placeholder?: string;
  defaultValue?: string;
  step?: string;
  min?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div>
      <label
        htmlFor={id}
        className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
      >
        {label}
      </label>

      <input
        id={id}
        name={id}
        type={type}
        required={required}
        disabled={disabled}
        placeholder={placeholder}
        defaultValue={defaultValue}
        step={step}
        min={min}
        className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none placeholder:text-slate-600 focus:border-blue-500 disabled:opacity-60"
      />
    </div>
  );
}

function SelectField({
  id,
  label,
  defaultValue,
  options,
  disabled,
}: {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  defaultValue: string;
  options: readonly (
    readonly [string, string]
  )[];
  disabled: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <div>
      <label
        htmlFor={id}
        className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
      >
        {label}
      </label>

      <select
        id={id}
        name={id}
        defaultValue={defaultValue}
        disabled={disabled}
        className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white"
      >
        {options.map(
          ([value, text]) => (
            <option
              key={value}
              value={value}
            >
              {text}
            </option>
          )
        )}
      </select>
    </div>
  );
}

import { notFound } from "next/navigation";

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

import { EditInvestmentForm } from "./edit-investment-form";

export const instant = false;

type EditInvestmentPageProps = {
  params: Promise<{
    id: string;
  }>;
};

function toDateInputValue(
  date: Date | null
): string {
  if (!date) {
    return "";
  }

  return date
    .toISOString()
    .slice(0, 10);
}

export default async function EditInvestmentPage({
  params,
}: EditInvestmentPageProps) {
  const { id: rawId } =
    await params;

  const holdingId = Number(rawId);

  if (
    !Number.isInteger(holdingId) ||
    holdingId <= 0
  ) {
    notFound();
  }

  const holding =
    await prisma.holding.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: holdingId,
      },
      include: {
        instrument: true,
      },
    });

  if (!holding) {
    notFound();
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-950 text-slate-100">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-6 py-10">
        <header className="mb-8">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-400">
            Mizan Local
          </p>

          <h1 className="mt-3 text-4xl font-bold text-white">
            Edit Investment
          </h1>

          <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-400">
            Update the holding, valuation,
            market source, provider symbol,
            and automatic pricing settings.
          </p>
        </header>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
          <EditInvestmentForm
            holding={{
              id: holding.id,
              symbol:
                holding.instrument.symbol,
              name:
                holding.instrument.name,
              assetClass:
                holding.instrument
                  .assetClass,
              market:
                holding.instrument.market,
              quoteCurrency:
                holding.instrument
                  .quoteCurrency,
              priceProvider:
                holding.instrument
                  .priceProvider,
              providerSymbol:
                holding.instrument
                  .providerSymbol,
              country:
                holding.instrument.country,
              sector:
                holding.instrument.sector,
              accountName:
                holding.accountName,
              quantityText:
                holding.quantityText,
              averageCostMinor:
                holding.averageCostMinor,
              latestPriceMinor:
                holding.latestPriceMinor,
              feesMinor:
                holding.feesMinor,
              acquisitionDate:
                toDateInputValue(
                  holding.acquisitionDate
                ),
              priceDate:
                toDateInputValue(
                  holding.lastValuedAt
                ) ||
                new Date()
                  .toISOString()
                  .slice(0, 10),
              riskLevel:
                holding.riskLevel,
              liquidityClass:
                holding.liquidityClass,
              includedInNetWorth:
                holding.includedInNetWorth,
              notes: holding.notes,
            }}
          />
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
