"use server";

import Decimal from "decimal.js";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { fetchBinancePrice } from "@/lib/market-data/binance";
import { fetchCoinMarketCapPairPrice } from "@/lib/market-data/coinmarketcap";
import { parseCryptoPair } from "@/lib/market-data/crypto-pair";

export type InvestmentActionState = {
  success: boolean;
  message: string;
};

export type PriceUpdateActionState = {
  success: boolean;
  message: string;
};

const allowedAssetClasses = [
  "CRYPTO",
  "SAUDI_EQUITY",
  "US_EQUITY",
  "US_ETF",
  "EGYPT_EQUITY",
  "EGYPT_ETF",
  "PHYSICAL_GOLD",
  "DIGITAL_GOLD",
  "GOLD_ETF",
  "MUTUAL_FUND",
  "MONEY_MARKET_FUND",
  "FIXED_INCOME_FUND",
  "EQUITY_FUND",
  "BALANCED_FUND",
  "REIT",
  "OTHER",
] as const;

const allowedMarkets = [
  "CRYPTO",
  "TASI",
  "NASDAQ",
  "NYSE",
  "NYSE_ARCA",
  "EGX",
  "GOLD",
  "FUND",
  "OTC",
] as const;

const allowedCurrencies = [
  "EGP",
  "SAR",
  "USD",
  "USDT",
  "USDC",
  "FDUSD",
  "TUSD",
  "BUSD",
  "EUR",
  "KWD",
] as const;

const initialPriceUpdateState: PriceUpdateActionState = {
  success: false,
  message: "",
};

function describeUnknownError(error: unknown): string {
  if (error instanceof Error) {
    const details: string[] = [];

    if (error.message.trim()) details.push(error.message.trim());

    if (error.cause) {
      const cause = describeUnknownError(error.cause);
      if (cause && !details.includes(cause)) details.push(`Cause: ${cause}`);
    }

    if (details.length === 0 && error.stack) {
      const stackLine = error.stack
        .split("\n")
        .map((line) => line.trim())
        .find((line) => line && line !== "Error");

      if (stackLine) details.push(stackLine);
    }

    return details.join(" | ") || error.name || "Unknown Error object";
  }

  if (typeof error === "string") return error.trim() || "Empty error string";

  try {
    const serialized = JSON.stringify(error, null, 2);
    if (serialized && serialized !== "{}" && serialized !== "null") {
      return serialized;
    }
  } catch {
    // Continue to String conversion.
  }

  const value = String(error);
  return value && value !== "[object Object]" && value !== "undefined"
    ? value
    : "Unknown non-Error value";
}

function readRequiredText(formData: FormData, field: string): string {
  const value = formData.get(field);
  if (typeof value !== "string" || value.trim() === "") {
    throw new Error(`${field} is required.`);
  }
  return value.trim();
}

function readOptionalText(formData: FormData, field: string): string | null {
  const value = formData.get(field);
  return typeof value === "string" && value.trim() ? value.trim() : null;
}

function readNonNegativeAmount(formData: FormData, field: string): Decimal {
  const rawValue = formData.get(field);
  if (typeof rawValue !== "string" || rawValue.trim() === "") {
    throw new Error(`${field} is required.`);
  }

  let value: Decimal;
  try {
    value = new Decimal(rawValue.replaceAll(",", "").trim());
  } catch {
    throw new Error(`${field} must be a valid number.`);
  }

  if (!value.isFinite() || value.isNegative()) {
    throw new Error(`${field} must be a valid non-negative number.`);
  }

  return value;
}

function readPositiveQuantity(formData: FormData): Decimal {
  const quantity = readNonNegativeAmount(formData, "quantity");
  if (quantity.lte(0)) throw new Error("Quantity must be greater than zero.");
  return quantity;
}

function readOptionalDate(formData: FormData, field: string): Date | null {
  const rawValue = formData.get(field);
  if (typeof rawValue !== "string" || rawValue.trim() === "") return null;

  const date = new Date(`${rawValue}T12:00:00`);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) throw new Error(`${field} is invalid.`);
  return date;
}

function decimalToSafeInteger(value: Decimal, errorMessage: string): number {
  if (!value.isInteger()) {
    throw new Error("Internal calculation did not produce an integer.");
  }

  const maximum = new Decimal(Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER);
  const minimum = new Decimal(Number.MIN_SAFE_INTEGER);
  if (value.gt(maximum) || value.lt(minimum)) throw new Error(errorMessage);

  return value.toNumber();
}

// Monetary values currently use two-decimal minor units.
function toMinor(amount: Decimal): number {
  return decimalToSafeInteger(
    amount.mul(100).toDecimalPlaces(0, Decimal.ROUND_HALF_UP),
    "The monetary value is too large to store safely."
  );
}

function calculatePositionValues(
  quantity: Decimal,
  averageCostMinor: number,
  latestPriceMinor: number,
  feesMinor: number
) {
  const remainingCost = quantity
    .mul(averageCostMinor)
    .plus(feesMinor)
    .toDecimalPlaces(0, Decimal.ROUND_HALF_UP);

  const marketValue = quantity
    .mul(latestPriceMinor)
    .toDecimalPlaces(0, Decimal.ROUND_HALF_UP);

  return {
    remainingCostMinor: decimalToSafeInteger(
      remainingCost,
      "The calculated cost basis is too large to store safely."
    ),
    marketValueMinor: decimalToSafeInteger(
      marketValue,
      "The calculated market value is too large to store safely."
    ),
    unrealizedPnlMinor: decimalToSafeInteger(
      marketValue.minus(remainingCost),
      "The calculated unrealized profit or loss is too large to store safely."
    ),
  };
}

function validateClassification(
  assetClass: string,
  market: string,
  quoteCurrency: string
): InvestmentActionState | null {
  if (!allowedAssetClasses.includes(assetClass as (typeof allowedAssetClasses)[number])) {
    return { success: false, message: "Invalid investment asset class." };
  }
  if (!allowedMarkets.includes(market as (typeof allowedMarkets)[number])) {
    return { success: false, message: "Invalid market." };
  }
  if (!allowedCurrencies.includes(quoteCurrency as (typeof allowedCurrencies)[number])) {
    return { success: false, message: "Invalid quote currency." };
  }
  return null;
}

function normalizeProviderConfiguration(
  assetClass: string,
  quoteCurrency: string,
  priceProvider: string,
  providerSymbol: string | null
): { providerSymbol: string | null; automaticUpdate: boolean } {
  if (priceProvider === "MANUAL") {
    return { providerSymbol: null, automaticUpdate: false };
  }

  if (!providerSymbol) {
    throw new Error("A provider pair or symbol is required for automatic updates.");
  }

  const normalized = providerSymbol.trim().toUpperCase();

  if (assetClass === "CRYPTO") {
    const pair = parseCryptoPair(normalized);
    if (pair.quoteSymbol !== quoteCurrency) {
      throw new Error(
        `Pair ${pair.pair} is quoted in ${pair.quoteSymbol}, but the holding quote currency is ${quoteCurrency}. Change the quote currency to ${pair.quoteSymbol}.`
      );
    }
    return { providerSymbol: pair.pair, automaticUpdate: true };
  }

  return { providerSymbol: normalized, automaticUpdate: true };
}

async function revalueInstrumentHoldings(
  transaction: Parameters<Parameters<typeof prisma.$transaction>[0]>[0],
  holdings: Array<{
    id: number;
    quantityText: string;
    averageCostMinor: number;
    feesMinor: number;
    remainingCostMinor: number | null;
  }>,
  priceMinor: number,
  priceCurrency: string,
  priceDate: Date
) {
  for (const holding of holdings) {
    const quantity = new Decimal(holding.quantityText);
    const remainingCostMinor =
      holding.remainingCostMinor ??
      decimalToSafeInteger(
        quantity
          .mul(holding.averageCostMinor)
          .plus(holding.feesMinor)
          .toDecimalPlaces(0, Decimal.ROUND_HALF_UP),
        "Calculated cost basis is too large."
      );

    const marketValueMinor = decimalToSafeInteger(
      quantity.mul(priceMinor).toDecimalPlaces(0, Decimal.ROUND_HALF_UP),
      "Calculated market value is too large."
    );

    await transaction.holding.update({
      where: { id: holding.id },
      data: {
        latestPriceMinor: priceMinor,
        latestPriceCurrency: priceCurrency,
        marketValueMinor,
        remainingCostMinor,
        unrealizedPnlMinor: marketValueMinor - remainingCostMinor,
        lastValuedAt: priceDate,
      },
    });
  }
}

export async function createInvestment(
  _previousState: InvestmentActionState,
  formData: FormData
): Promise<InvestmentActionState> {
  try {
    const symbol = readRequiredText(formData, "symbol").toUpperCase();
    const name = readRequiredText(formData, "name");
    const assetClass = readRequiredText(formData, "assetClass");
    const market = readRequiredText(formData, "market");
    const quoteCurrency = readRequiredText(formData, "quoteCurrency").toUpperCase();
    const quantity = readPositiveQuantity(formData);
    const averageCost = readNonNegativeAmount(formData, "averageCost");
    const latestPrice = readNonNegativeAmount(formData, "latestPrice");
    const fees = readNonNegativeAmount(formData, "fees");

    const validation = validateClassification(assetClass, market, quoteCurrency);
    if (validation) return validation;

    const priceProvider = readRequiredText(formData, "priceProvider").toUpperCase();
    const providerConfig = normalizeProviderConfiguration(
      assetClass,
      quoteCurrency,
      priceProvider,
      readOptionalText(formData, "providerSymbol")
    );

    const averageCostMinor = toMinor(averageCost);
    const latestPriceMinor = toMinor(latestPrice);
    const feesMinor = toMinor(fees);
    const values = calculatePositionValues(
      quantity,
      averageCostMinor,
      latestPriceMinor,
      feesMinor
    );

    const acquisitionDate = readOptionalDate(formData, "acquisitionDate");
    const priceDate = readOptionalDate(formData, "priceDate") ?? new Date();

    await prisma.$transaction(async (transaction) => {
      const instrument = await transaction.instrument.upsert({
        where: { market_symbol: { market, symbol } },
        update: {
          name,
          assetClass,
          quoteCurrency,
          priceProvider,
          providerSymbol: providerConfig.providerSymbol,
          country: readOptionalText(formData, "country"),
          sector: readOptionalText(formData, "sector"),
          automaticUpdate: providerConfig.automaticUpdate,
          isActive: true,
        },
        create: {
          symbol,
          name,
          assetClass,
          market,
          quoteCurrency,
          priceProvider,
          providerSymbol: providerConfig.providerSymbol,
          country: readOptionalText(formData, "country"),
          sector: readOptionalText(formData, "sector"),
          automaticUpdate: providerConfig.automaticUpdate,
          isActive: true,
        },
      });

      const holding = await transaction.holding.create({
        data: {
          instrumentId: instrument.id,
          accountName: readOptionalText(formData, "accountName"),
          quantityText: quantity.toFixed(),
          averageCostMinor,
          costCurrency: quoteCurrency,
          acquisitionDate,
          feesMinor,
          remainingCostMinor: values.remainingCostMinor,
          latestPriceMinor,
          latestPriceCurrency: quoteCurrency,
          marketValueMinor: values.marketValueMinor,
          unrealizedPnlMinor: values.unrealizedPnlMinor,
          lastValuedAt: priceDate,
          riskLevel: readRequiredText(formData, "riskLevel"),
          liquidityClass: readRequiredText(formData, "liquidityClass"),
          includedInNetWorth: formData.get("includedInNetWorth") === "on",
          notes: readOptionalText(formData, "notes"),
        },
      });

      await transaction.investmentTransaction.create({
        data: {
          holdingId: holding.id,
          transactionType: "BUY",
          transactionDate: acquisitionDate ?? new Date(),
          quantityText: quantity.toFixed(),
          unitPriceMinor: averageCostMinor,
          grossAmountMinor: decimalToSafeInteger(
            quantity.mul(averageCostMinor).toDecimalPlaces(0, Decimal.ROUND_HALF_UP),
            "Opening transaction value is too large."
          ),
          feesMinor,
          taxesMinor: 0,
          netAmountMinor: values.remainingCostMinor,
          currency: quoteCurrency,
          notes: "Opening position created from the holding form.",
        },
      });

      await transaction.marketPrice.create({
        data: {
          instrumentId: instrument.id,
          priceMinor: latestPriceMinor,
          currency: quoteCurrency,
          priceDate,
          provider: "MANUAL",
          priceType: "CLOSE",
          isOfficial: false,
          isDelayed: false,
          isManual: true,
          notes: "Initial price entered while creating the holding.",
        },
      });
    });

    revalidatePath("/");
    revalidatePath("/investments");
    revalidatePath("/database-test");
    return { success: true, message: `${symbol} holding was created successfully.` };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Create investment failed:", error);
    return { success: false, message: describeUnknownError(error) };
  }
}

export async function updateManualPrice(
  _previousState: InvestmentActionState,
  formData: FormData
): Promise<InvestmentActionState> {
  try {
    const instrumentId = Number(formData.get("instrumentId"));
    if (!Number.isInteger(instrumentId) || instrumentId <= 0) {
      return { success: false, message: "Invalid instrument ID." };
    }

    const instrument = await prisma.instrument.findUnique({
      where: { id: instrumentId },
      include: { holdings: true },
    });
    if (!instrument) return { success: false, message: "Instrument was not found." };

    const priceMinor = toMinor(readNonNegativeAmount(formData, "latestPrice"));
    const priceDate = readOptionalDate(formData, "priceDate") ?? new Date();

    await prisma.$transaction(async (transaction) => {
      await transaction.marketPrice.upsert({
        where: {
          instrumentId_priceDate_provider_priceType: {
            instrumentId,
            priceDate,
            provider: "MANUAL",
            priceType: "CLOSE",
          },
        },
        update: {
          priceMinor,
          currency: instrument.quoteCurrency,
          fetchedAt: new Date(),
          isManual: true,
          isOfficial: false,
          isDelayed: false,
          notes: "Manual price updated.",
        },
        create: {
          instrumentId,
          priceMinor,
          currency: instrument.quoteCurrency,
          priceDate,
          provider: "MANUAL",
          priceType: "CLOSE",
          isManual: true,
          isOfficial: false,
          isDelayed: false,
          notes: "Manual price update.",
        },
      });

      await revalueInstrumentHoldings(
        transaction,
        instrument.holdings,
        priceMinor,
        instrument.quoteCurrency,
        priceDate
      );
    });

    revalidatePath("/");
    revalidatePath("/investments");
    return { success: true, message: `${instrument.symbol} price was updated successfully.` };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Update manual price failed:", error);
    return { success: false, message: describeUnknownError(error) };
  }
}

export async function deleteHolding(formData: FormData) {
  const holdingId = Number(formData.get("holdingId"));
  if (!Number.isInteger(holdingId) || holdingId <= 0) {
    throw new Error("Invalid holding ID.");
  }

  await prisma.holding.delete({ where: { id: holdingId } });
  revalidatePath("/");
  revalidatePath("/investments");
}

export async function updateInvestment(
  _previousState: InvestmentActionState,
  formData: FormData
): Promise<InvestmentActionState> {
  try {
    const holdingId = Number(formData.get("holdingId"));
    if (!Number.isInteger(holdingId) || holdingId <= 0) {
      return { success: false, message: "Invalid holding ID." };
    }

    const existingHolding = await prisma.holding.findUnique({
      where: { id: holdingId },
      include: { instrument: true },
    });
    if (!existingHolding) {
      return { success: false, message: "Investment holding was not found." };
    }

    const symbol = readRequiredText(formData, "symbol").toUpperCase();
    const name = readRequiredText(formData, "name");
    const assetClass = readRequiredText(formData, "assetClass");
    const market = readRequiredText(formData, "market");
    const quoteCurrency = readRequiredText(formData, "quoteCurrency").toUpperCase();
    const quantity = readPositiveQuantity(formData);
    const averageCost = readNonNegativeAmount(formData, "averageCost");
    const latestPrice = readNonNegativeAmount(formData, "latestPrice");
    const fees = readNonNegativeAmount(formData, "fees");

    const validation = validateClassification(assetClass, market, quoteCurrency);
    if (validation) return validation;

    const priceProvider = readRequiredText(formData, "priceProvider").toUpperCase();
    const providerConfig = normalizeProviderConfiguration(
      assetClass,
      quoteCurrency,
      priceProvider,
      readOptionalText(formData, "providerSymbol")
    );

    const duplicate = await prisma.instrument.findFirst({
      where: { market, symbol, id: { not: existingHolding.instrumentId } },
    });
    if (duplicate) {
      return { success: false, message: "Another instrument already uses this market and symbol." };
    }

    const averageCostMinor = toMinor(averageCost);
    const latestPriceMinor = toMinor(latestPrice);
    const feesMinor = toMinor(fees);
    const values = calculatePositionValues(
      quantity,
      averageCostMinor,
      latestPriceMinor,
      feesMinor
    );
    const priceDate = readOptionalDate(formData, "priceDate") ?? new Date();

    await prisma.$transaction(async (transaction) => {
      await transaction.instrument.update({
        where: { id: existingHolding.instrumentId },
        data: {
          symbol,
          name,
          assetClass,
          market,
          quoteCurrency,
          priceProvider,
          providerSymbol: providerConfig.providerSymbol,
          country: readOptionalText(formData, "country"),
          sector: readOptionalText(formData, "sector"),
          automaticUpdate: providerConfig.automaticUpdate,
          isActive: true,
        },
      });

      await transaction.holding.update({
        where: { id: holdingId },
        data: {
          accountName: readOptionalText(formData, "accountName"),
          quantityText: quantity.toFixed(),
          averageCostMinor,
          costCurrency: quoteCurrency,
          acquisitionDate: readOptionalDate(formData, "acquisitionDate"),
          feesMinor,
          remainingCostMinor: values.remainingCostMinor,
          latestPriceMinor,
          latestPriceCurrency: quoteCurrency,
          marketValueMinor: values.marketValueMinor,
          unrealizedPnlMinor: values.unrealizedPnlMinor,
          lastValuedAt: priceDate,
          riskLevel: readRequiredText(formData, "riskLevel"),
          liquidityClass: readRequiredText(formData, "liquidityClass"),
          includedInNetWorth: formData.get("includedInNetWorth") === "on",
          notes: readOptionalText(formData, "notes"),
        },
      });

      await transaction.marketPrice.upsert({
        where: {
          instrumentId_priceDate_provider_priceType: {
            instrumentId: existingHolding.instrumentId,
            priceDate,
            provider: "MANUAL",
            priceType: "CLOSE",
          },
        },
        update: {
          priceMinor: latestPriceMinor,
          currency: quoteCurrency,
          fetchedAt: new Date(),
          isManual: true,
          isOfficial: false,
          isDelayed: false,
          notes: "Manual valuation updated while editing the holding.",
        },
        create: {
          instrumentId: existingHolding.instrumentId,
          priceMinor: latestPriceMinor,
          currency: quoteCurrency,
          priceDate,
          provider: "MANUAL",
          priceType: "CLOSE",
          isManual: true,
          isOfficial: false,
          isDelayed: false,
          notes: "Manual valuation created while editing the holding.",
        },
      });
    });

    revalidatePath("/");
    revalidatePath("/investments");
    revalidatePath(`/investments/${holdingId}/edit`);
    return {
      success: true,
      message: `${symbol} was updated successfully. Automatic updates are ${
        providerConfig.automaticUpdate ? "enabled" : "disabled"
      }.`,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Update investment failed:", error);
    return { success: false, message: describeUnknownError(error) };
  }
}

export async function updateBinancePrices(
  _previousState: PriceUpdateActionState = initialPriceUpdateState
): Promise<PriceUpdateActionState> {
  const run = await prisma.priceUpdateRun.create({
    data: { provider: "BINANCE", status: "RUNNING" },
  });

  try {
    const instruments = await prisma.instrument.findMany({
      where: {
        priceProvider: "BINANCE",
        automaticUpdate: true,
        isActive: true,
        providerSymbol: { not: null },
      },
      include: { holdings: true },
      orderBy: { symbol: "asc" },
    });

    await prisma.priceUpdateRun.update({
      where: { id: run.id },
      data: { requestedCount: instruments.length },
    });

    if (instruments.length === 0) {
      await prisma.priceUpdateRun.update({
        where: { id: run.id },
        data: { status: "COMPLETED", completedAt: new Date() },
      });
      return { success: false, message: "No active Binance instruments were found." };
    }

    let successCount = 0;
    let failureCount = 0;
    const errors: string[] = [];

    for (const instrument of instruments) {
      try {
        if (!instrument.providerSymbol) throw new Error("Binance pair is missing.");
        const price = await fetchBinancePrice(instrument.providerSymbol);

        await prisma.$transaction(async (transaction) => {
          await transaction.marketPrice.create({
            data: {
              instrumentId: instrument.id,
              priceMinor: price.priceMinor,
              currency: instrument.quoteCurrency,
              priceDate: price.priceDate,
              fetchedAt: new Date(),
              provider: "BINANCE",
              sourceUrl: price.sourceUrl,
              priceType: "SPOT",
              isOfficial: true,
              isDelayed: false,
              isManual: false,
              notes: `Binance public ticker price for ${price.symbol}.`,
            },
          });

          await revalueInstrumentHoldings(
            transaction,
            instrument.holdings,
            price.priceMinor,
            instrument.quoteCurrency,
            price.priceDate
          );
        });

        successCount += 1;
      } catch (error) {
        failureCount += 1;
        errors.push(`${instrument.symbol}: ${describeUnknownError(error)}`);
      }
    }

    const status = failureCount === 0 ? "COMPLETED" : successCount > 0 ? "PARTIAL" : "FAILED";
    await prisma.priceUpdateRun.update({
      where: { id: run.id },
      data: {
        status,
        completedAt: new Date(),
        successCount,
        failureCount,
        errorMessage: errors.length ? errors.join("\n").slice(0, 4_000) : null,
      },
    });

    revalidatePath("/");
    revalidatePath("/investments");
    return {
      success: successCount > 0,
      message: `Binance update finished: ${successCount} updated, ${failureCount} failed.${
        errors.length ? ` ${errors[0]}` : ""
      }`,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    const message = describeUnknownError(error);
    await prisma.priceUpdateRun.update({
      where: { id: run.id },
      data: {
        status: "FAILED",
        completedAt: new Date(),
        failureCount: 1,
        errorMessage: message.slice(0, 4_000),
      },
    });
    return { success: false, message };
  }
}

export async function updateCoinMarketCapPrices(
  _previousState: PriceUpdateActionState
): Promise<PriceUpdateActionState> {
  const run = await prisma.priceUpdateRun.create({
    data: { provider: "COINMARKETCAP", status: "RUNNING" },
  });

  try {
    const instruments = await prisma.instrument.findMany({
      where: {
        priceProvider: "COINMARKETCAP",
        automaticUpdate: true,
        isActive: true,
        providerSymbol: { not: null },
      },
      include: { holdings: true },
      orderBy: { symbol: "asc" },
    });

    await prisma.priceUpdateRun.update({
      where: { id: run.id },
      data: { requestedCount: instruments.length },
    });

    if (instruments.length === 0) {
      await prisma.priceUpdateRun.update({
        where: { id: run.id },
        data: { status: "COMPLETED", completedAt: new Date() },
      });
      return { success: false, message: "No active CoinMarketCap instruments were found." };
    }

    let successCount = 0;
    let failureCount = 0;
    const errors: string[] = [];

    for (const instrument of instruments) {
      try {
        if (!instrument.providerSymbol) throw new Error("CoinMarketCap market pair is missing.");
        const price = await fetchCoinMarketCapPairPrice(instrument.providerSymbol);

        await prisma.$transaction(async (transaction) => {
          await transaction.marketPrice.upsert({
            where: {
              instrumentId_priceDate_provider_priceType: {
                instrumentId: instrument.id,
                priceDate: price.priceDate,
                provider: "COINMARKETCAP",
                priceType: "SPOT",
              },
            },
            update: {
              priceMinor: price.priceMinor,
              currency: price.quoteSymbol,
              fetchedAt: new Date(),
              sourceUrl: price.sourceUrl,
              isOfficial: true,
              isDelayed: false,
              isManual: false,
              notes: `CoinMarketCap quote for ${price.name} (${price.baseSymbol}), pair ${price.pair}, CMC ID ${price.cmcId}.`,
            },
            create: {
              instrumentId: instrument.id,
              priceMinor: price.priceMinor,
              currency: price.quoteSymbol,
              priceDate: price.priceDate,
              fetchedAt: new Date(),
              provider: "COINMARKETCAP",
              sourceUrl: price.sourceUrl,
              priceType: "SPOT",
              isOfficial: true,
              isDelayed: false,
              isManual: false,
              notes: `CoinMarketCap quote for ${price.name} (${price.baseSymbol}), pair ${price.pair}, CMC ID ${price.cmcId}.`,
            },
          });

          await revalueInstrumentHoldings(
            transaction,
            instrument.holdings,
            price.priceMinor,
            price.quoteSymbol,
            price.priceDate
          );
        });

        successCount += 1;
      } catch (error) {
        failureCount += 1;
        const message = describeUnknownError(error);
        console.error(`CoinMarketCap update failed for ${instrument.symbol}:`, {
          error,
          message,
          providerSymbol: instrument.providerSymbol,
          quoteCurrency: instrument.quoteCurrency,
        });
        errors.push(`${instrument.symbol}: ${message}`);
      }
    }

    const status = failureCount === 0 ? "COMPLETED" : successCount > 0 ? "PARTIAL" : "FAILED";
    await prisma.priceUpdateRun.update({
      where: { id: run.id },
      data: {
        status,
        completedAt: new Date(),
        successCount,
        failureCount,
        errorMessage: errors.length ? errors.join("\n").slice(0, 4_000) : null,
      },
    });

    revalidatePath("/");
    revalidatePath("/investments");
    return {
      success: successCount > 0,
      message: `CoinMarketCap update finished: ${successCount} updated, ${failureCount} failed.${
        errors.length ? ` ${errors[0]}` : ""
      }`,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    const message = describeUnknownError(error);
    await prisma.priceUpdateRun.update({
      where: { id: run.id },
      data: {
        status: "FAILED",
        completedAt: new Date(),
        failureCount: 1,
        errorMessage: message.slice(0, 4_000),
      },
    });
    return { success: false, message };
  }
}

export async function updateAllPrices(
  _previousState: PriceUpdateActionState
): Promise<PriceUpdateActionState> {
  try {
    // Only invoke providers that currently have active,
    // automatically updated instruments assigned to them.
    const activeInstruments =
      await prisma.instrument.findMany({
        where: {
          automaticUpdate: true,
          isActive: true,
          providerSymbol: {
            not: null,
          },
        },
        select: {
          priceProvider: true,
        },
      });

    const configuredProviders =
      new Set(
        activeInstruments.map(
          (instrument) =>
            instrument.priceProvider
        )
      );

    if (configuredProviders.size === 0) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message:
          "No instruments are currently configured for automatic price updates.",
      };
    }

    const results: {
      provider: string;
      result: PriceUpdateActionState;
    }[] = [];

    // Run providers sequentially. This keeps API usage predictable
    // and makes the update history easier to read.
    if (
      configuredProviders.has("BINANCE")
    ) {
      const result =
        await updateBinancePrices(
          initialPriceUpdateState
        );

      results.push({
        provider: "Binance",
        result,
      });
    }

    if (
      configuredProviders.has(
        "COINMARKETCAP"
      )
    ) {
      const result =
        await updateCoinMarketCapPrices(
          initialPriceUpdateState
        );

      results.push({
        provider: "CoinMarketCap",
        result,
      });
    }

    const unsupportedProviders =
      Array.from(
        configuredProviders
      ).filter(
        (provider) =>
          ![
            "BINANCE",
            "COINMARKETCAP",
          ].includes(provider)
      );

    for (
      const provider of
      unsupportedProviders
    ) {
      results.push({
        provider,
        result: {
          success: false,
          message:
            `${provider} is configured, ` +
            `but its automatic updater has not been implemented yet.`,
        },
      });
    }

    const successfulResults =
      results.filter(
        ({ result }) =>
          result.success
      );

    const failedResults =
      results.filter(
        ({ result }) =>
          !result.success
      );

    revalidatePath("/");
    revalidatePath("/investments");

    const summary = results
      .map(
        ({ provider, result }) =>
          `${provider}: ${result.message}`
      )
      .join(" | ");

    return {
      success:
        successfulResults.length > 0 &&
        failedResults.length === 0,

      message:
        `Price update completed. ` +
        `${successfulResults.length} provider(s) succeeded, ` +
        `${failedResults.length} provider(s) failed. ` +
        summary,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    const message =
      describeUnknownError(error);

    console.error(
      "Update all prices failed:",
      error
    );

    return {
      success: false,
      message:
        `Price update failed: ${message}`,
    };
  }
}

FILE: src\app\investments\confirm-delete-holding-button.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import { useRef, useState } from "react";

type ConfirmDeleteHoldingButtonProps = {
  holdingId: number;
  instrumentLabel: string;
  deleteAction: (
    formData: FormData
  ) => void | Promise<void>;
};

export function ConfirmDeleteHoldingButton({
  holdingId,
  instrumentLabel,
  deleteAction,
}: ConfirmDeleteHoldingButtonProps) {
  const dialogRef =
    useRef<HTMLDialogElement>(null);

  const [isDeleting, setIsDeleting] =
    useState(false);

  return (
    <>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => {
          dialogRef.current?.showModal();
        }}
        className="text-sm font-semibold text-rose-300 transition hover:text-rose-200"
      >
        Delete
      </button>

      <dialog
        ref={dialogRef}
        onCancel={(event) => {
          if (isDeleting) {
            event.preventDefault();
          }
        }}
        className="m-auto w-[calc(100%-2rem)] max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 p-0 text-slate-100 shadow-2xl backdrop:bg-black/70"
      >
        <div className="p-6">
          <div className="flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-rose-500/10 text-xl text-rose-300">
            ×
          </div>

          <h2 className="mt-5 text-xl font-semibold text-white">
            Delete investment holding?
          </h2>

          <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-400">
            This will permanently delete the
            holding and its transaction history.
          </p>

          <p className="mt-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4 font-semibold text-slate-200">
            {instrumentLabel}
          </p>

          <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
            The shared instrument and market-price
            history will remain available for any
            other holdings.
          </p>

          <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={isDeleting}
              onClick={() => {
                dialogRef.current?.close();
              }}
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-700 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>

            <form
              action={async (formData) => {
                setIsDeleting(true);

                try {
                  await deleteAction(formData);
                  dialogRef.current?.close();
                } finally {
                  setIsDeleting(false);
                }
              }}
            >
              <input
                type="hidden"
                name="holdingId"
                value={holdingId}
              />

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={isDeleting}
                className="w-full rounded-xl bg-rose-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-rose-500 disabled:cursor-wait disabled:opacity-60"
              >
                {isDeleting
                  ? "Deleting..."
                  : "Delete permanently"}
              </button>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      </dialog>
    </>
  );
}

function Field({
  id,
  label,
  type = "text",
  required = false,
  disabled = false,
  placeholder,
  defaultValue,
  step,
  min,
}: {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  type?: string;
  required?: boolean;
  disabled?: boolean;
  placeholder?: string;
  defaultValue?: string;
  step?: string;
  min?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div>
      <label
        htmlFor={id}
        className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
      >
        {label}
      </label>

      <input
        id={id}
        name={id}
        type={type}
        required={required}
        disabled={disabled}
        placeholder={placeholder}
        defaultValue={defaultValue}
        step={step}
        min={min}
        className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none placeholder:text-slate-600 focus:border-blue-500 disabled:opacity-60"
      />
    </div>
  );
}

function CurrencySelect({
  name,
  label,
  defaultValue,
  disabled,
}: {
  name: string;
  label: string;
  defaultValue: string;
  disabled: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <div>
      <label
        htmlFor={name}
        className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
      >
        {label}
      </label>

      <select
        id={name}
        name={name}
        defaultValue={defaultValue}
        disabled={disabled}
        className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white"
      >
        {[
          "EGP",
          "SAR",
          "USD",
          "USDT",
          "USDC",
          "EUR",
          "KWD",
        ].map((currency) => (
          <option
            key={currency}
            value={currency}
          >
            {currency}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
    </div>
  );
}

function SelectField({
  id,
  label,
  defaultValue,
  options,
  disabled,
}: {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  defaultValue: string;
  options: readonly (
    readonly [string, string]
  )[];
  disabled: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <div>
      <label
        htmlFor={id}
        className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
      >
        {label}
      </label>

      <select
        id={id}
        name={id}
        defaultValue={defaultValue}
        disabled={disabled}
        className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white"
      >
        {options.map(
          ([value, labelText]) => (
            <option
              key={value}
              value={value}
            >
              {labelText}
            </option>
          )
        )}
      </select>
    </div>
  );
}

function getAssetClassDefaults(
  assetClass: string
) {
  const defaults = {
    market: "CRYPTO",
    quoteCurrency: "USD",
    symbolPlaceholder: "BTC",
    namePlaceholder: "Bitcoin",
    quantityPlaceholder:
      "0.18374628",
    accountPlaceholder:
      "Ledger or Binance",
    providerPlaceholder:
      "BTCUSDT or bitcoin",
    country: "Global",
    sectorPlaceholder:
      "Digital assets",
    riskLevel: "VERY_HIGH",
    liquidity: "HIGH",
  };

  if (
    assetClass === "SAUDI_EQUITY"
  ) {
    return {
      ...defaults,
      market: "TASI",
      quoteCurrency: "SAR",
      symbolPlaceholder: "2222",
      namePlaceholder:
        "Saudi Aramco",
      quantityPlaceholder: "100",
      accountPlaceholder:
        "Saudi broker",
      providerPlaceholder: "2222",
      country: "Saudi Arabia",
      sectorPlaceholder: "Energy",
      riskLevel: "HIGH",
    };
  }

  if (
    assetClass === "US_EQUITY" ||
    assetClass === "US_ETF"
  ) {
    return {
      ...defaults,
      market:
        assetClass === "US_ETF"
          ? "NYSE_ARCA"
          : "NASDAQ",
      quoteCurrency: "USD",
      symbolPlaceholder:
        assetClass === "US_ETF"
          ? "VOO"
          : "AAPL",
      namePlaceholder:
        assetClass === "US_ETF"
          ? "Vanguard S&P 500 ETF"
          : "Apple Inc.",
      quantityPlaceholder: "10",
      accountPlaceholder: "IBKR",
      providerPlaceholder:
        assetClass === "US_ETF"
          ? "VOO"
          : "AAPL",
      country: "United States",
      sectorPlaceholder:
        assetClass === "US_ETF"
          ? "Broad US equities"
          : "Technology",
      riskLevel: "HIGH",
    };
  }

  if (
    assetClass ===
      "EGYPT_EQUITY" ||
    assetClass === "EGYPT_ETF"
  ) {
    return {
      ...defaults,
      market: "EGX",
      quoteCurrency: "EGP",
      symbolPlaceholder: "COMI",
      namePlaceholder:
        "Commercial International Bank",
      quantityPlaceholder: "1000",
      accountPlaceholder:
        "Egyptian broker",
      providerPlaceholder: "COMI",
      country: "Egypt",
      sectorPlaceholder: "Banking",
      riskLevel: "HIGH",
    };
  }

  if (
    assetClass.includes("GOLD")
  ) {
    return {
      ...defaults,
      market: "GOLD",
      quoteCurrency: "EGP",
      symbolPlaceholder: "GOLD24K",
      namePlaceholder:
        "24K physical gold",
      quantityPlaceholder: "100",
      accountPlaceholder:
        "Safe or vault",
      providerPlaceholder:
        "XAU or local-24k-gram",
      country: "Egypt",
      sectorPlaceholder:
        "Gold / precious metals",
      riskLevel: "MEDIUM",
      liquidity: "MEDIUM",
    };
  }

  if (
    assetClass.includes("FUND")
  ) {
    return {
      ...defaults,
      market: "FUND",
      quoteCurrency: "EGP",
      symbolPlaceholder:
        "FUND-CODE",
      namePlaceholder:
        "Investment fund name",
      quantityPlaceholder:
        "1543.738291",
      accountPlaceholder:
        "Fund provider",
      providerPlaceholder:
        "ISIN or provider code",
      country: "Egypt",
      sectorPlaceholder:
        "Money market or equity fund",
      riskLevel: "MEDIUM",
      liquidity: "MEDIUM",
    };
  }

  return defaults;
}

FILE: src\app\investments\manual-price-form.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import {
  useActionState,
  useEffect,
  useRef,
} from "react";

import {
  updateManualPrice,
  type InvestmentActionState,
} from "./actions";

type ManualPriceFormProps = {
  instrumentId: number;
  symbol: string;
  currency: string;
  latestPriceMinor: number | null;
};

const initialState: InvestmentActionState = {
  success: false,
  message: "",
};

function toMajor(
  amountMinor: number | null
): string {
  if (amountMinor === null) {
    return "";
  }

  return (amountMinor / 100).toFixed(4);
}

export function ManualPriceForm({
  instrumentId,
  symbol,
  currency,
  latestPriceMinor,
}: ManualPriceFormProps) {
  const dialogRef =
    useRef<HTMLDialogElement>(null);

  const [state, formAction, isPending] =
    useActionState(
      updateManualPrice,
      initialState
    );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (state.success) {
      const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
        dialogRef.current?.close();
      }, 800);

      return () =>
        window.clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }, [state.success]);

  return (
    <>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => {
          dialogRef.current?.showModal();
        }}
        className="text-sm font-semibold text-amber-300 transition hover:text-amber-200"
      >
        Update price
      </button>

      <dialog
        ref={dialogRef}
        className="m-auto w-[calc(100%-2rem)] max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 p-0 text-slate-100 shadow-2xl backdrop:bg-black/70"
      >
        <form
          action={formAction}
          className="space-y-5 p-6"
        >
          <input
            type="hidden"
            name="instrumentId"
            value={instrumentId}
          />

          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-amber-300">
              Manual valuation
            </p>

            <h2 className="mt-2 text-xl font-semibold text-white">
              Update {symbol} price
            </h2>

            <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-400">
              The new price will revalue all
              holdings linked to this instrument.
            </p>
          </div>

          {state.message ? (
            <div
              role="alert"
              aria-live="polite"
              className={`rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm ${
                state.success
                  ? "border-emerald-500/20 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300"
                  : "border-rose-500/20 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300"
              }`}
            >
              {state.message}
            </div>
          ) : null}

          <div>
            <label
              htmlFor={`latestPrice-${instrumentId}`}
              className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
            >
              Latest price ({currency})
            </label>

            <input
              id={`latestPrice-${instrumentId}`}
              name="latestPrice"
              type="number"
              min="0"
              step="0.0001"
              required
              defaultValue={toMajor(
                latestPriceMinor
              )}
              disabled={isPending}
              className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none focus:border-amber-500 disabled:opacity-60"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label
              htmlFor={`priceDate-${instrumentId}`}
              className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300"
            >
              Price date
            </label>

            <input
              id={`priceDate-${instrumentId}`}
              name="priceDate"
              type="date"
              required
              defaultValue={new Date()
                .toISOString()
                .slice(0, 10)}
              disabled={isPending}
              className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none focus:border-amber-500 disabled:opacity-60"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 border-t border-slate-800 pt-5 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={isPending}
              onClick={() => {
                dialogRef.current?.close();
              }}
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-700 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={isPending}
              className="rounded-xl bg-amber-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-amber-500 disabled:cursor-wait disabled:opacity-60"
            >
              {isPending
                ? "Updating..."
                : "Save price"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </dialog>
    </>
  );
}

============================================================
FILE: src\app\investments\page.tsx
