import Link from "next/link";
import Decimal from "decimal.js";

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

import { deleteHolding } from "./actions";
import {
  UpdateAllPricesButton,
} from "./update-all-prices-button";
import { ConfirmDeleteHoldingButton } from "./confirm-delete-holding-button";
import { InvestmentForm } from "./investment-form";
import { ManualPriceForm } from "./manual-price-form";

export const instant = false;

type Category = "ALL" | "CRYPTO" | "SAUDI" | "US" | "EGYPT" | "GOLD" | "FUNDS";

type PageProps = {
  searchParams: Promise<{ category?: string }>;
};

const cryptoQuoteCurrencies = new Set([
  "USDT",
  "USDC",
  "FDUSD",
  "TUSD",
  "BUSD",
  "DAI",
]);

function formatMoney(
  amountMinor: number,
  currency: string
): string {
  const normalizedCurrency =
    currency.trim().toUpperCase();

  const amount = amountMinor / 100;

  // Stablecoins are not ISO 4217 currencies, so Intl.NumberFormat
  // cannot format them using style: "currency".
  if (
    cryptoQuoteCurrencies.has(
      normalizedCurrency
    )
  ) {
    const formattedAmount =
      new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
        minimumFractionDigits: 2,
        maximumFractionDigits: 2,
      }).format(amount);

    return `${normalizedCurrency} ${formattedAmount}`;
  }

  try {
    return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
      style: "currency",
      currency: normalizedCurrency,
      maximumFractionDigits: 2,
    }).format(amount);
  } catch {
    // Keep the page usable if a future provider returns
    // another non-ISO currency or token code.
    const formattedAmount =
      new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
        minimumFractionDigits: 2,
        maximumFractionDigits: 2,
      }).format(amount);

    return `${normalizedCurrency} ${formattedAmount}`;
  }
}

function normalizeCurrency(currency: string): string {
  const value = currency.trim().toUpperCase();
  return ["USDT", "USDC", "FDUSD", "TUSD", "BUSD"].includes(value)
    ? "USD"
    : value;
}

function convertMinor(
  amountMinor: number,
  fromCurrency: string,
  toCurrency: string,
  rateMap: Record<string, number>
): number | null {
  const from = normalizeCurrency(fromCurrency);
  const to = normalizeCurrency(toCurrency);

  if (from === to) return amountMinor;

  // ExchangeRate values represent one unit of the source currency in EGP.
  const fromToEgp = from === "EGP" ? 1 : rateMap[from];
  const toToEgp = to === "EGP" ? 1 : rateMap[to];

  if (!fromToEgp || !toToEgp) return null;
  return Math.round((amountMinor * fromToEgp) / toToEgp);
}

function categoryFor(assetClass: string): Category {
  if (assetClass === "CRYPTO") return "CRYPTO";
  if (["SAUDI_EQUITY", "REIT"].includes(assetClass)) return "SAUDI";
  if (["US_EQUITY", "US_ETF"].includes(assetClass)) return "US";
  if (["EGYPT_EQUITY", "EGYPT_ETF"].includes(assetClass)) return "EGYPT";
  if (assetClass.includes("GOLD")) return "GOLD";
  if (assetClass.includes("FUND")) return "FUNDS";
  return "ALL";
}

function formatQuantity(quantityText: string): string {
  try {
    return new Decimal(quantityText).toDecimalPlaces(8).toString();
  } catch {
    return quantityText;
  }
}

function freshness(lastValuedAt: Date | null, maximumStaleHours: number) {
  if (!lastValuedAt) {
    return { label: "NO PRICE", className: "bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300" };
  }

  const hours = (Date.now() - lastValuedAt.getTime()) / 3_600_000;
  return hours <= maximumStaleHours
    ? { label: "FRESH", className: "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300" }
    : { label: "STALE", className: "bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-300" };
}

function formatDateTime(date: Date | null): string {
  if (!date) return "Never";
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "short",
    year: "numeric",
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
  }).format(date);
}

function DualCurrencyValue({
  amountMinor,
  currency,
  rateMap,
  className = "text-white",
}: {
  amountMinor: number;
  currency: string;
  rateMap: Record<string, number>;
  className?: string;
}) {
  const egpMinor = convertMinor(amountMinor, currency, "EGP", rateMap);

  return (
    <div>
      <p className={`font-semibold ${className}`}>
        {formatMoney(amountMinor, currency)}
      </p>
      {normalizeCurrency(currency) !== "EGP" && egpMinor !== null ? (
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
          {formatMoney(egpMinor, "EGP")}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

function PortfolioCard({
  label,
  usdMinor,
  egpMinor,
  className = "text-white",
}: {
  label: string;
  usdMinor: number;
  egpMinor: number;
  className?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-5">
      <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">{label}</p>
      <p className={`mt-2 text-xl font-bold ${className}`}>
        {formatMoney(usdMinor, "USD")}
      </p>
      <p className="mt-1 text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">
        {formatMoney(egpMinor, "EGP")}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

function SimpleCard({
  label,
  value,
  className = "text-white",
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  className?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-5">
      <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">{label}</p>
      <p className={`mt-2 text-xl font-bold ${className}`}>{value}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

export default async function InvestmentsPage({ searchParams }: PageProps) {
  const { category: rawCategory } = await searchParams;
  const allowed: Category[] = ["ALL", "CRYPTO", "SAUDI", "US", "EGYPT", "GOLD", "FUNDS"];
  const selectedCategory = allowed.includes(rawCategory as Category)
    ? (rawCategory as Category)
    : "ALL";

  const [
  holdings,
  rates,
  updateRuns,
] = await Promise.all([
  prisma.holding.findMany({
    include: {
      instrument: {
        include: {
          // The latest MarketPrice identifies the provider
          // that actually supplied the current valuation.
          prices: {
            orderBy: [
              {
                priceDate: "desc",
              },
              {
                fetchedAt: "desc",
              },
            ],
            take: 1,
          },
        },
      },
    },
    orderBy: {
      updatedAt: "desc",
    },
  }),

  prisma.exchangeRate.findMany({
    where: {
      toCurrency: "EGP",
    },
    orderBy: {
      rateDate: "desc",
    },
  }),

  prisma.priceUpdateRun.findMany({
    orderBy: {
      startedAt: "desc",
    },
    take: 8,
  }),
]);

  const rateMap: Record<string, number> = { EGP: 1 };
  for (const rate of rates) {
    if (!rateMap[rate.fromCurrency]) rateMap[rate.fromCurrency] = rate.rate;
  }

  const visibleHoldings = holdings.filter(
    (holding) =>
      selectedCategory === "ALL" ||
      categoryFor(holding.instrument.assetClass) === selectedCategory
  );

  let totalCostUsdMinor = 0;
  let totalMarketUsdMinor = 0;
  let totalPnlUsdMinor = 0;
  let totalCostEgpMinor = 0;
  let totalMarketEgpMinor = 0;
  let totalPnlEgpMinor = 0;
  let freshCount = 0;
  let attentionCount = 0;

  const allocation = new Map<string, number>();

  for (const holding of holdings) {
    const nativeCurrency =
      holding.latestPriceCurrency ?? holding.instrument.quoteCurrency;
    const costMinor = holding.remainingCostMinor ?? 0;
    const marketMinor = holding.marketValueMinor ?? 0;
    const pnlMinor = holding.unrealizedPnlMinor ?? 0;

    const costUsd = convertMinor(costMinor, holding.costCurrency, "USD", rateMap);
    const marketUsd = convertMinor(marketMinor, nativeCurrency, "USD", rateMap);
    const pnlUsd = convertMinor(pnlMinor, nativeCurrency, "USD", rateMap);
    const costEgp = convertMinor(costMinor, holding.costCurrency, "EGP", rateMap);
    const marketEgp = convertMinor(marketMinor, nativeCurrency, "EGP", rateMap);
    const pnlEgp = convertMinor(pnlMinor, nativeCurrency, "EGP", rateMap);

    if (costUsd !== null) totalCostUsdMinor += costUsd;
    if (marketUsd !== null) totalMarketUsdMinor += marketUsd;
    if (pnlUsd !== null) totalPnlUsdMinor += pnlUsd;
    if (costEgp !== null) totalCostEgpMinor += costEgp;
    if (marketEgp !== null) totalMarketEgpMinor += marketEgp;
    if (pnlEgp !== null) totalPnlEgpMinor += pnlEgp;

    const status = freshness(
      holding.lastValuedAt,
      holding.instrument.maximumStaleHours
    );
    if (status.label === "FRESH") freshCount += 1;
    else attentionCount += 1;

    const category = categoryFor(holding.instrument.assetClass);
    allocation.set(category, (allocation.get(category) ?? 0) + (marketEgp ?? 0));
  }

  const unrealizedReturn =
    totalCostUsdMinor === 0 ? 0 : (totalPnlUsdMinor / totalCostUsdMinor) * 100;

  const filters: { value: Category; label: string }[] = [
    { value: "ALL", label: "All" },
    { value: "CRYPTO", label: "Crypto" },
    { value: "SAUDI", label: "Saudi" },
    { value: "US", label: "US" },
    { value: "EGYPT", label: "Egypt" },
    { value: "GOLD", label: "Gold" },
    { value: "FUNDS", label: "Funds" },
  ];

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-950 text-slate-100">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1650px] px-6 py-10">
        <header className="mb-8 flex flex-col justify-between gap-5 lg:flex-row lg:items-end">
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-400">
              Mizan Local
            </p>
            <h1 className="mt-3 text-4xl font-bold text-white">
              Investments Portfolio
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-3 max-w-3xl text-sm leading-6 text-slate-400">
              Native investment currencies are primary. EGP equivalents appear below them for household reporting.
            </p>
            <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {filters.map((filter) => (
                <Link
                  key={filter.value}
                  href={`/investments?category=${filter.value}`}
                  className={`rounded-xl border px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold transition ${
                    selectedCategory === filter.value
                      ? "border-blue-500 bg-blue-500/15 text-blue-200"
                      : "border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-800"
                  }`}
                >
                  {filter.label}
                </Link>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex shrink-0 items-start gap-3">
            <UpdateAllPricesButton />

            <Link
                href="/"
                className="rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-4 py-3 text-center text-sm font-medium text-slate-200 transition hover:bg-slate-800"
            >
                Back to Dashboard
            </Link>
            </div>
        </header>

        <section className="mb-8 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-5">
          <PortfolioCard label="Market value" usdMinor={totalMarketUsdMinor} egpMinor={totalMarketEgpMinor} />
          <PortfolioCard label="Cost basis" usdMinor={totalCostUsdMinor} egpMinor={totalCostEgpMinor} className="text-slate-200" />
          <PortfolioCard
            label="Unrealized P&L"
            usdMinor={totalPnlUsdMinor}
            egpMinor={totalPnlEgpMinor}
            className={totalPnlUsdMinor < 0 ? "text-rose-300" : "text-emerald-300"}
          />
          <SimpleCard
            label="Unrealized return"
            value={`${unrealizedReturn >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${unrealizedReturn.toFixed(2)}%`}
            className={unrealizedReturn < 0 ? "text-rose-300" : "text-emerald-300"}
          />
          <SimpleCard
            label="Price coverage"
            value={`${freshCount} fresh / ${attentionCount} attention`}
            className={attentionCount > 0 ? "text-amber-300" : "text-emerald-300"}
          />
        </section>

        <section className="mb-8 grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[1.35fr_0.65fr]">
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-white">Allocation by category</h2>
            <div className="mt-6 space-y-4">
              {allocation.size === 0 ? (
                <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">Add an investment to see allocation.</p>
              ) : (
                Array.from(allocation.entries())
                  .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])
                  .map(([category, value]) => {
                    const percentage = totalMarketEgpMinor === 0 ? 0 : (value / totalMarketEgpMinor) * 100;
                    return (
                      <div key={category}>
                        <div className="flex justify-between gap-4 text-sm">
                          <span className="font-medium text-slate-300">{category}</span>
                          <span className="font-semibold text-white">{percentage.toFixed(1)}%</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="mt-2 h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-800">
                          <div className="h-full rounded-full bg-blue-500" style={{ width: `${Math.min(percentage, 100)}%` }} />
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-white">Valuation status</h2>
            <div className="mt-6 space-y-4 text-sm">
              <div className="flex justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-3"><span className="text-slate-400">Fresh</span><strong className="text-emerald-300">{freshCount}</strong></div>
              <div className="flex justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-3"><span className="text-slate-400">Attention</span><strong className="text-amber-300">{attentionCount}</strong></div>
              <div className="flex justify-between"><span className="text-slate-400">Total holdings</span><strong>{holdings.length}</strong></div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="space-y-8">
  {/* Holdings stays at the top and uses the full page width. */}
  <div>
    <div className="mb-4 flex flex-col justify-between gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:items-end">
      <div>
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-white">
          Holdings
        </h2>

        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-400">
          Showing {visibleHoldings.length} of{" "}
          {holdings.length} holdings.
        </p>
      </div>

      <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
        Native currency is shown first. The EGP equivalent
        appears below it.
      </p>
    </div>

    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900">
      <div className="overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="min-w-full divide-y divide-slate-800">
          <thead>
            <tr className="text-left text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
              <th className="px-4 py-4">
                Instrument
              </th>

              <th className="px-4 py-4">
                Class
              </th>

              <th className="px-4 py-4 text-right">
                Quantity
              </th>

              <th className="px-4 py-4 text-right">
                Average cost
              </th>

              <th className="px-4 py-4 text-right">
                Latest price
              </th>

              <th className="px-4 py-4 text-right">
                Market value
              </th>

              <th className="px-4 py-4 text-right">
                Unrealized
              </th>

              <th className="px-4 py-4">
                Price status
              </th>

              <th className="px-4 py-4 text-right">
                Actions
              </th>
            </tr>
          </thead>

          <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-800">
            {visibleHoldings.length === 0 ? (
              <tr>
                <td
                  colSpan={9}
                  className="px-5 py-12 text-center text-sm text-slate-400"
                >
                  No holdings match this category.
                </td>
              </tr>
            ) : (
              visibleHoldings.map((holding) => {
                const instrument =
                  holding.instrument;
                const latestMarketPrice =
                instrument.prices[0] ?? null;

                const lastSuccessfulProvider =
                latestMarketPrice?.provider ?? "NONE";

                const nativeCurrency =
                  holding.latestPriceCurrency ??
                  instrument.quoteCurrency;

                const costMinor =
                  holding.remainingCostMinor ?? 0;

                const marketMinor =
                  holding.marketValueMinor ?? 0;

                const pnlMinor =
                  holding.unrealizedPnlMinor ?? 0;

                const returnPercentage =
                  costMinor === 0
                    ? 0
                    : (pnlMinor / costMinor) * 100;

                const status = freshness(
                  holding.lastValuedAt,
                  instrument.maximumStaleHours
                );

                return (
                  <tr
                    key={holding.id}
                    className="align-top transition hover:bg-slate-800/40"
                  >
                    <td className="px-4 py-4">
                      <p className="font-semibold text-white">
                        {instrument.symbol}
                      </p>

                      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-400">
                        {instrument.name}
                      </p>

                      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-600">
                        {holding.accountName ?? "No broker"} |{" "}
                        {instrument.market}
                      </p>
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-4 py-4">
                      <span className="rounded-full bg-blue-500/10 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-300">
                        {instrument.assetClass.replaceAll(
                          "_",
                          " "
                        )}
                      </span>
                    </td>

                    <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-4 text-right">
                      {formatQuantity(
                        holding.quantityText
                      )}
                    </td>

                    <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-4 text-right">
                      <DualCurrencyValue
                        amountMinor={
                          holding.averageCostMinor
                        }
                        currency={
                          holding.costCurrency
                        }
                        rateMap={rateMap}
                        className="text-slate-300"
                      />
                    </td>

                    <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-4 text-right">
                      <DualCurrencyValue
                        amountMinor={
                          holding.latestPriceMinor ?? 0
                        }
                        currency={nativeCurrency}
                        rateMap={rateMap}
                      />
                    </td>

                    <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-4 text-right">
                      <DualCurrencyValue
                        amountMinor={marketMinor}
                        currency={nativeCurrency}
                        rateMap={rateMap}
                      />
                    </td>

                    <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-4 text-right">
                      <DualCurrencyValue
                        amountMinor={pnlMinor}
                        currency={nativeCurrency}
                        rateMap={rateMap}
                        className={
                          pnlMinor < 0
                            ? "text-rose-300"
                            : "text-emerald-300"
                        }
                      />

                      <p
                        className={`mt-1 text-xs ${
                          returnPercentage < 0
                            ? "text-rose-400"
                            : "text-emerald-400"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {returnPercentage >= 0 ? "+" : ""}
                        {returnPercentage.toFixed(2)}%
                      </p>
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-4 py-4">
                      <span
                        className={`rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${status.className}`}
                      >
                        {status.label}
                      </span>

                      <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-500">
                        {formatDateTime(
                          holding.lastValuedAt
                        )}
                      </p>

                      <div className="mt-2 space-y-1 text-xs">
                        <p className="text-slate-500">
                            Last source:{" "}
                            <span className="font-semibold text-slate-300">
                            {lastSuccessfulProvider}
                            </span>
                        </p>

                        <p className="text-slate-600">
                            Configured: {instrument.priceProvider}
                        </p>

                        {latestMarketPrice?.priceType ? (
                            <p className="text-slate-600">
                            Type: {latestMarketPrice.priceType}
                            </p>
                        ) : null}
                        </div>
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-4 py-4 text-right">
                      <div className="flex min-w-[120px] flex-col items-end gap-2">
                        <Link
                          href={`/investments/${holding.id}/edit`}
                          className="text-sm font-semibold text-blue-300 transition hover:text-blue-200"
                        >
                          Edit
                        </Link>

                        <ManualPriceForm
                          instrumentId={instrument.id}
                          symbol={instrument.symbol}
                          currency={nativeCurrency}
                          latestPriceMinor={
                            holding.latestPriceMinor
                          }
                        />

                        <ConfirmDeleteHoldingButton
                          holdingId={holding.id}
                          instrumentLabel={`${instrument.symbol} | ${instrument.name}`}
                          deleteAction={deleteHolding}
                        />
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })
            )}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
<section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900">
  <div className="border-b border-slate-800 px-6 py-5">
    <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-white">
      Price update history
    </h2>

    <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-400">
      Recent manual and provider update attempts, including
      full failure messages.
    </p>
  </div>

  {updateRuns.length === 0 ? (
    <p className="px-6 py-8 text-sm text-slate-400">
      No automatic price update has been attempted yet.
    </p>
  ) : (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto">
      <table className="min-w-full divide-y divide-slate-800">
        <thead>
          <tr className="text-left text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
            <th className="px-5 py-4">
              Provider
            </th>

            <th className="px-5 py-4">
              Status
            </th>

            <th className="px-5 py-4 text-right">
              Requested
            </th>

            <th className="px-5 py-4 text-right">
              Updated
            </th>

            <th className="px-5 py-4 text-right">
              Failed
            </th>

            <th className="px-5 py-4">
              Started
            </th>

            <th className="px-5 py-4">
              Details
            </th>
          </tr>
        </thead>

        <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-800">
          {updateRuns.map((run) => {
            const statusClass =
              run.status === "COMPLETED"
                ? "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300"
                : run.status === "PARTIAL"
                  ? "bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-300"
                  : run.status === "RUNNING"
                    ? "bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-300"
                    : "bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300";

            return (
              <tr
                key={run.id}
                className="align-top transition hover:bg-slate-800/40"
              >
                <td className="px-5 py-4 font-semibold text-white">
                  {run.provider}
                </td>

                <td className="px-5 py-4">
                  <span
                    className={`rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${statusClass}`}
                  >
                    {run.status}
                  </span>
                </td>

                <td className="px-5 py-4 text-right">
                  {run.requestedCount}
                </td>

                <td className="px-5 py-4 text-right text-emerald-300">
                  {run.successCount}
                </td>

                <td className="px-5 py-4 text-right">
                  <span
                    className={
                      run.failureCount > 0
                        ? "font-semibold text-rose-300"
                        : "text-slate-400"
                    }
                  >
                    {run.failureCount}
                  </span>
                </td>

                <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4 text-sm text-slate-400">
                  {formatDateTime(run.startedAt)}
                </td>

                <td className="max-w-xl px-5 py-4">
                  {run.errorMessage ? (
                    <details>
                      <summary className="cursor-pointer text-sm font-semibold text-rose-300 hover:text-rose-200">
                        Show error
                      </summary>

                      <pre className="mt-3 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words rounded-xl border border-rose-500/20 bg-slate-950 p-4 text-xs leading-5 text-rose-200">
                        {run.errorMessage}
                      </pre>
                    </details>
                  ) : (
                    <span className="text-sm text-slate-500">
                      No errors
                    </span>
                  )}
                </td>
              </tr>
            );
          })}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  )}
</section>
  {/* The form returns to a fixed-width left column below Holdings. */}
  <div className="grid items-start gap-8 xl:grid-cols-[500px_minmax(0,1fr)]">
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
      <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-white">
        Add investment
      </h2>

      <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-400">
        Crypto sources always use a market pair, such as
        BTCUSDC or BTCUSDT.
      </p>

      <div className="mt-6">
        <InvestmentForm />
      </div>
    </div>

    {/* Reserved for transactions, holding details, or performance later. */}
    <div className="hidden xl:block" />
  </div>
</section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}


============================================================
FILE: src\app\investments\update-all-prices-button.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import {
  useActionState,
  useEffect,
  useState,
} from "react";

import {
  updateAllPrices,
  type PriceUpdateActionState,
} from "./actions";

const initialState:
  PriceUpdateActionState = {
    success: false,
    message: "",
  };

export function UpdateAllPricesButton() {
  const [
    state,
    formAction,
    isPending,
  ] = useActionState(
    updateAllPrices,
    initialState
  );

  const [
    showNotification,
    setShowNotification,
  ] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!state.message) {
      return;
    }

    setShowNotification(true);

    // Hide successful updates automatically.
    // Failed updates remain slightly longer for readability.
    const timeoutDuration =
      state.success ? 5_000 : 9_000;

    const timeoutId =
      window.setTimeout(() => {
        setShowNotification(false);
      }, timeoutDuration);

    return () => {
      window.clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    };
  }, [state.message, state.success]);

  return (
    <>
      {/* The button has a stable size and never changes
          position when the update result appears. */}
      <form action={formAction}>
        <button
          type="submit"
          disabled={isPending}
          className="min-w-[150px] rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-blue-500 disabled:cursor-wait disabled:bg-blue-800 disabled:text-blue-200"
        >
          {isPending
            ? "Updating prices..."
            : "Update all prices"}
        </button>
      </form>

      {/* The result is rendered outside the page layout,
          so it does not move the header or the button. */}
      {showNotification &&
      state.message ? (
        <div
          role="status"
          aria-live="polite"
          className={`fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 w-[calc(100%-3rem)] max-w-lg rounded-2xl border p-4 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-sm ${
            state.success
              ? "border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-950/95 text-emerald-100"
              : "border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-950/95 text-amber-100"
          }`}
        >
          <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
            <div
              className={`mt-0.5 flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-sm font-bold ${
                state.success
                  ? "bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300"
                  : "bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300"
              }`}
            >
              {state.success ? "✓" : "!"}
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <p className="font-semibold">
                {state.success
                  ? "Prices updated"
                  : "Price update needs attention"}
              </p>

              <p className="mt-1 break-words text-xs leading-5 opacity-90">
                {state.message}
              </p>
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setShowNotification(false);
              }}
              aria-label="Dismiss notification"
              className="shrink-0 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 text-lg leading-none opacity-60 transition hover:bg-white/10 hover:opacity-100"
            >
              ×
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useActionState } from "react";

import {
  updateLiability,
  type LiabilityActionState,
} from "@/app/liabilities/actions";

type EditLiabilityFormProps = {
  liability: {
    id: number;
    name: string;
    liabilityType: string;
    accountingTreatment: string;
    country: string | null;
    currency: string;
    originalAmountMinor: number;
    remainingAmountMinor: number;
    monthlyPaymentMinor: number | null;
    startDate: string;
    endDate: string;
    notes: string | null;
  };
};

const initialState: LiabilityActionState = {
  success: false,
  message: "",
};

function toMajor(
  amountMinor: number | null
): string {
  if (amountMinor === null) {
    return "";
  }

  return (amountMinor / 100).toFixed(2);
}

export function EditLiabilityForm({
  liability,
}: EditLiabilityFormProps) {

      updateLiability,

FILE: src\app\liabilities\[id]\edit\page.tsx
============================================================
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

import { EditLiabilityForm } from "./edit-liability-form";

export const instant = false;

type EditLiabilityPageProps = {
  params: Promise<{
    id: string;
  }>;
};

function toDateInputValue(
  date: Date | null
): string {
  if (!date) {
    return "";
  }

  return date.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

export default async function EditLiabilityPage({
  params,
}: EditLiabilityPageProps) {
  const { id: rawId } = await params;
  const id = Number(rawId);

  if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
    notFound();
  }

  const liability =
    await prisma.liability.findUnique({
      where: {
        id,
      },
    });

  if (!liability) {
    notFound();
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-950 text-slate-100">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-6 py-10">
        <header className="mb-8">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-400">
            Mizan Local
          </p>

          <h1 className="mt-3 text-4xl font-bold text-white">
            Edit Liability
          </h1>

          <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-400">
            Update the contract balance, accounting
            treatment, dates, or fallback payment.
          </p>
        </header>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
          <EditLiabilityForm
            liability={{
              id: liability.id,
              name: liability.name,
              liabilityType:
                liability.liabilityType,
              accountingTreatment:
                liability.accountingTreatment,
              country: liability.country,
              currency: liability.currency,
              originalAmountMinor:
                liability.originalAmountMinor,
              remainingAmountMinor:
                liability.remainingAmountMinor,
              monthlyPaymentMinor:
                liability.monthlyPaymentMinor,
              startDate: toDateInputValue(
                liability.startDate
              ),
              endDate: toDateInputValue(
                liability.endDate
              ),
              notes: liability.notes,
            }}
          />
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useActionState } from "react";

  updatePayment,

type EditPaymentFormProps = {
  liabilityId: number;

  success: false,
  message: "",

function toMajor(amountMinor: number): string {

}

export function EditPaymentForm({

          defaultChecked={
            payment.isRecurringEstimate
          }
          disabled={isPending}

        />

            Estimated payment
          </span>

          <span className="mt-1 block text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
            Disable this when the payment has been
            confirmed from the developer schedule.
          </span>
        </span>
      </label>

      <div className="flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 border-t border-slate-800 pt-6 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
        <Link
          href={`/liabilities/${liabilityId}/schedule`}
          className="rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 px-5 py-3 text-center text-sm font-semibold text-slate-200 transition hover:bg-slate-700"
        >
          Cancel
        </Link>

        <button
          type="submit"
          disabled={isPending}
          className="rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-blue-500 disabled:cursor-wait disabled:bg-blue-800"
        >
          {isPending
            ? "Saving..."
            : "Save changes"}
        </button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

import { notFound } from "next/navigation";

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

import { EditPaymentForm } from "./edit-payment-form";

export const instant = false;

type EditPaymentPageProps = {
  params: Promise<{
    id: string;
    paymentId: string;
  }>;
};

function toDateInputValue(date: Date): string {
  return date.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

export default async function EditPaymentPage({
  params,
}: EditPaymentPageProps) {
  const {
    id: rawLiabilityId,
    paymentId: rawPaymentId,
  } = await params;

  const liabilityId = Number(rawLiabilityId);
  const paymentId = Number(rawPaymentId);

  if (
    !Number.isInteger(liabilityId) ||
    liabilityId <= 0 ||
    !Number.isInteger(paymentId) ||
    paymentId <= 0
  ) {
    notFound();
  }

  const payment =
    await prisma.liabilityPayment.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: paymentId,
      },
      include: {
        liability: true,
      },
    });

  if (
    !payment ||
    payment.liabilityId !== liabilityId
  ) {
    notFound();
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-950 text-slate-100">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-6 py-10">
        <header className="mb-8">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-400">
            Mizan Local
          </p>

          <h1 className="mt-3 text-4xl font-bold text-white">
            Edit Scheduled Payment
          </h1>

          <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-400">
            Update the date, amount, status, source,
            or payment classification.
          </p>
        </header>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
          <EditPaymentForm
            liabilityId={payment.liabilityId}
            liabilityName={
              payment.liability.name
            }
            currency={
              payment.liability.currency
            }
            payment={{
              id: payment.id,
              paymentDate: toDateInputValue(
                payment.paymentDate
              ),
              amountMinor: payment.amountMinor,
              paymentType: payment.paymentType,
              status: payment.status,
              description: payment.description,
              isRecurringEstimate:
                payment.isRecurringEstimate,
            }}
          />
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: src\app\liabilities\[id]\schedule\actions.ts

"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

export type PaymentActionState = {
  success: boolean;
  message: string;
};

function toMinor(amount: number): number {
  return Math.round(amount * 100);
}

export async function createPayment(
  previousState: PaymentActionState,
  formData: FormData
): Promise<PaymentActionState> {
  try {
    const liabilityId = Number(formData.get("liabilityId"));
    const amount = Number(formData.get("amount"));
    const paymentDateRaw = formData.get("paymentDate");
    const paymentTypeRaw = formData.get("paymentType");
    const statusRaw = formData.get("status");
    const descriptionRaw = formData.get("description");

    if (!Number.isInteger(liabilityId) || liabilityId <= 0) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid liability ID.",
      };
    }

    if (!Number.isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Payment amount must be greater than zero.",
      };
    }

    if (
      typeof paymentDateRaw !== "string" ||
      paymentDateRaw.trim() === ""
    ) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Payment date is required.",
      };
    }

    const paymentDate = new Date(
      `${paymentDateRaw}T12:00:00`
    );

    if (Number.isNaN(paymentDate.getTime())) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Payment date is invalid.",
      };
    }

    const paymentType =
      typeof paymentTypeRaw === "string"
        ? paymentTypeRaw
        : "INSTALLMENT";

    const status =
      typeof statusRaw === "string"
        ? statusRaw
        : "PLANNED";

    const description =
      typeof descriptionRaw === "string" &&
      descriptionRaw.trim()
        ? descriptionRaw.trim()
        : null;

    const isRecurringEstimate =
      formData.get("isRecurringEstimate") === "on";

    await prisma.liabilityPayment.create({
      data: {
        liabilityId,
        paymentDate,
        amountMinor: toMinor(amount),
        paymentType,
        status,
        description,
        isRecurringEstimate,
      },
    });

    revalidatePath(`/liabilities/${liabilityId}/schedule`);
    revalidatePath("/liabilities");
    revalidatePath("/");

    return {
      success: true,
      message: "Payment was added successfully.",
    };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Create payment failed:", error);

    return {
      success: false,
      message:
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "Payment could not be created.",
    };
  }
}

export async function deletePayment(formData: FormData) {
  const paymentId = Number(formData.get("paymentId"));
  const liabilityId = Number(formData.get("liabilityId"));

  if (!Number.isInteger(paymentId) || paymentId <= 0) {
    throw new Error("Invalid payment ID.");
  }

  await prisma.liabilityPayment.delete({
    where: {
      id: paymentId,
    },
  });

  revalidatePath(`/liabilities/${liabilityId}/schedule`);
  revalidatePath("/");
}

export async function generateRecurringPayments(
  previousState: PaymentActionState,
  formData: FormData
): Promise<PaymentActionState> {
  try {
    const liabilityId = Number(formData.get("liabilityId"));
    const amount = Number(formData.get("amount"));
    const startDateRaw = formData.get("startDate");
    const endDateRaw = formData.get("endDate");
    const frequencyRaw = formData.get("frequency");
    const paymentTypeRaw = formData.get("paymentType");
    const descriptionRaw = formData.get("description");

    if (!Number.isInteger(liabilityId) || liabilityId <= 0) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid liability ID.",
      };
    }

    if (!Number.isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Payment amount must be greater than zero.",
      };
    }

    if (
      typeof startDateRaw !== "string" ||
      startDateRaw.trim() === "" ||
      typeof endDateRaw !== "string" ||
      endDateRaw.trim() === ""
    ) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Start date and end date are required.",
      };
    }

    const startDate = new Date(`${startDateRaw}T12:00:00`);
    const endDate = new Date(`${endDateRaw}T12:00:00`);

    if (
      Number.isNaN(startDate.getTime()) ||
      Number.isNaN(endDate.getTime())
    ) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "The selected date range is invalid.",
      };
    }

    if (endDate < startDate) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "End date cannot be before start date.",
      };
    }

    const frequency =
      typeof frequencyRaw === "string"
        ? frequencyRaw
        : "MONTHLY";

    const monthsByFrequency: Record<string, number> = {
      MONTHLY: 1,
      QUARTERLY: 3,
      SEMIANNUAL: 6,
      ANNUAL: 12,
    };

    const monthStep = monthsByFrequency[frequency];

    if (!monthStep) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid payment frequency.",
      };
    }

    const liability = await prisma.liability.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: liabilityId,
      },
      include: {
        payments: true,
      },
    });

    if (!liability) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Liability was not found.",
      };
    }

    const paymentType =
      typeof paymentTypeRaw === "string"
        ? paymentTypeRaw
        : "INSTALLMENT";

    const description =
      typeof descriptionRaw === "string" &&
      descriptionRaw.trim()
        ? descriptionRaw.trim()
        : null;

    const generatedDates: Date[] = [];
    const cursor = new Date(startDate);

    while (cursor <= endDate) {
      generatedDates.push(new Date(cursor));

      cursor.setMonth(cursor.getMonth() + monthStep);
    }

    if (generatedDates.length === 0) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "No payment dates were generated.",
      };
    }

    if (generatedDates.length > 240) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message:
          "The generated schedule exceeds the maximum of 240 payments.",
      };
    }

    const existingKeys = new Set(
      liability.payments.map((payment) => {
        const dateKey = payment.paymentDate
          .toISOString()
          .slice(0, 10);

        return `${dateKey}:${payment.paymentType}`;
      })
    );

    const newDates = generatedDates.filter((date) => {
      const dateKey = date.toISOString().slice(0, 10);

      return !existingKeys.has(`${dateKey}:${paymentType}`);
    });

    if (newDates.length === 0) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message:
          "All generated payments already exist for the selected dates and type.",
      };
    }

    const existingOutstandingMinor =
      liability.payments
        .filter(
          (payment) =>
            payment.status !== "PAID" &&
            payment.status !== "CANCELLED"
        )
        .reduce(
          (total, payment) =>
            total + payment.amountMinor,
          0
        );

    const amountMinor = toMinor(amount);
    const generatedTotalMinor =
      amountMinor * newDates.length;

    const availableToScheduleMinor = Math.max(
      liability.remainingAmountMinor -
        existingOutstandingMinor,
      0
    );

    if (generatedTotalMinor > availableToScheduleMinor) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message:
          `Generated payments total ${(
            generatedTotalMinor / 100
          ).toLocaleString()} ${liability.currency}, ` +
          `but only ${(
            availableToScheduleMinor / 100
          ).toLocaleString()} ${liability.currency} remains unscheduled.`,
      };
    }

    await prisma.$transaction(
      newDates.map((paymentDate) =>
        prisma.liabilityPayment.create({
          data: {
            liabilityId,
            paymentDate,
            amountMinor,
            paymentType,
            status: "PLANNED",
            description,
            isRecurringEstimate: true,
          },
        })
      )
    );

    revalidatePath(
      `/liabilities/${liabilityId}/schedule`
    );
    revalidatePath("/liabilities");
    revalidatePath("/");

    const skippedCount =
      generatedDates.length - newDates.length;

    return {
      success: true,
      message:
        `${newDates.length} payments were generated successfully.` +
        (skippedCount > 0
          ? ` ${skippedCount} duplicate payments were skipped.`
          : ""),
    };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "Generate recurring payments failed:",
      error
    );

    return {
      success: false,
      message:
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "The recurring schedule could not be generated.",
    };
  }
}
export async function updatePayment(
  previousState: PaymentActionState,
  formData: FormData
): Promise<PaymentActionState> {
  try {
    const paymentId = Number(formData.get("paymentId"));
    const liabilityId = Number(formData.get("liabilityId"));
    const amountRaw = formData.get("amount");
    const paymentDateRaw = formData.get("paymentDate");
    const paymentTypeRaw = formData.get("paymentType");
    const statusRaw = formData.get("status");
    const descriptionRaw = formData.get("description");

    if (
      !Number.isInteger(paymentId) ||
      paymentId <= 0
    ) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid payment ID.",
      };
    }

    if (
      !Number.isInteger(liabilityId) ||
      liabilityId <= 0
    ) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid liability ID.",
      };
    }

    const amount =
      typeof amountRaw === "string"
        ? Number(amountRaw.replaceAll(",", "").trim())
        : Number.NaN;

    if (!Number.isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Payment amount must be greater than zero.",
      };
    }

    if (
      typeof paymentDateRaw !== "string" ||
      paymentDateRaw.trim() === ""
    ) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Payment date is required.",
      };
    }

    const paymentDate = new Date(
      `${paymentDateRaw}T12:00:00`
    );

    if (Number.isNaN(paymentDate.getTime())) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Payment date is invalid.",
      };
    }

    const payment = await prisma.liabilityPayment.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: paymentId,
      },
      include: {
        liability: {
          include: {
            payments: true,
          },
        },
      },
    });

    if (!payment || payment.liabilityId !== liabilityId) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Scheduled payment was not found.",
      };
    }

    const paymentType =
      typeof paymentTypeRaw === "string"
        ? paymentTypeRaw
        : "INSTALLMENT";

    const status =
      typeof statusRaw === "string"
        ? statusRaw
        : "PLANNED";

    const allowedStatuses = [
      "PLANNED",
      "PAID",
      "DEFERRED",
      "CANCELLED",
    ];

    if (!allowedStatuses.includes(status)) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid payment status.",
      };
    }

    const description =
      typeof descriptionRaw === "string" &&
      descriptionRaw.trim()
        ? descriptionRaw.trim()
        : null;

    const isRecurringEstimate =
      formData.get("isRecurringEstimate") === "on";

    const amountMinor = toMinor(amount);

    const otherOutstandingMinor =
      payment.liability.payments
        .filter(
          (otherPayment) =>
            otherPayment.id !== paymentId &&
            otherPayment.status !== "PAID" &&
            otherPayment.status !== "CANCELLED"
        )
        .reduce(
          (total, otherPayment) =>
            total + otherPayment.amountMinor,
          0
        );

    const editedPaymentCountsAsOutstanding =
      status !== "PAID" && status !== "CANCELLED";

    const totalAfterUpdateMinor =
      otherOutstandingMinor +
      (editedPaymentCountsAsOutstanding
        ? amountMinor
        : 0);

    if (
      totalAfterUpdateMinor >
      payment.liability.remainingAmountMinor
    ) {
      const availableMinor = Math.max(
        payment.liability.remainingAmountMinor -
          otherOutstandingMinor,
        0
      );

      return {
        success: false,
        message:
          `The payment is too large. Only ${(
            availableMinor / 100
          ).toLocaleString()} ${
            payment.liability.currency
          } remains available to schedule.`,
      };
    }

    const duplicatePayment =
      await prisma.liabilityPayment.findFirst({
        where: {
          liabilityId,
          paymentDate,
          paymentType,
          id: {
            not: paymentId,
          },
        },
      });

    if (duplicatePayment) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message:
          "Another payment with the same date and type already exists.",
      };
    }

    await prisma.liabilityPayment.update({
      where: {
        id: paymentId,
      },
      data: {
        paymentDate,
        amountMinor,
        paymentType,
        status,
        description,
        isRecurringEstimate,
      },
    });

    revalidatePath(
      `/liabilities/${liabilityId}/schedule`
    );
    revalidatePath(
      `/liabilities/${liabilityId}/schedule/${paymentId}/edit`
    );
    revalidatePath("/liabilities");
    revalidatePath("/");

    return {
      success: true,
      message: "Payment was updated successfully.",
    };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Update payment failed:", error);

    return {
      success: false,
      message:
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "The payment could not be updated.",
    };
  }
}

============================================================
FILE: src\app\liabilities\[id]\schedule\bulk-payment-form.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import {
  useActionState,
  useEffect,
  useRef,
} from "react";

import {
  generateRecurringPayments,
  type PaymentActionState,
} from "./actions";

const initialState: PaymentActionState = {
  success: false,
  message: "",
};

export function BulkPaymentForm({
  liabilityId,
}: {
  liabilityId: number;
}) {
  const formRef = useRef<HTMLFormElement>(null);

  const [state, formAction, isPending] =
    useActionState(
      generateRecurringPayments,
      initialState
    );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (state.success) {
      formRef.current?.reset();
    }
  }, [state.success]);

  return (
    <form
      ref={formRef}
      action={formAction}
      className="space-y-5"
    >
      <input
        type="hidden"
        name="liabilityId"
        value={liabilityId}
      />

      {state.message ? (
        <div
          role="alert"
          aria-live="polite"
          className={`rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm ${
            state.success
              ? "border-emerald-500/20 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300"
              : "border-rose-500/20 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300"
          }`}
        >
          {state.message}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <div>
          <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300">
            First payment date
          </label>

          <input
            name="startDate"
            type="date"
            required
            disabled={isPending}
            className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white disabled:opacity-60"
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300">
            Last payment date
          </label>

          <input
            name="endDate"
            type="date"
            required
            disabled={isPending}
            className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white disabled:opacity-60"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300">
          Amount per payment
        </label>

        <input
          name="amount"
          type="number"
          min="0.01"
          step="0.01"
          required
          disabled={isPending}
          placeholder="98000"
          className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white outline-none placeholder:text-slate-600 focus:border-blue-500 disabled:opacity-60"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <div>
          <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300">
            Frequency
          </label>

          <select
            name="frequency"
            defaultValue="MONTHLY"
            disabled={isPending}
            className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white disabled:opacity-60"
          >
            <option value="MONTHLY">Monthly</option>
            <option value="QUARTERLY">
              Every 3 months
            </option>
            <option value="SEMIANNUAL">
              Every 6 months
            </option>
            <option value="ANNUAL">Annual</option>
          </select>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300">
            Payment type
          </label>

          <select
            name="paymentType"
            defaultValue="INSTALLMENT"
            disabled={isPending}
            className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white disabled:opacity-60"
          >
            <option value="INSTALLMENT">
              Installment
            </option>
            <option value="MAINTENANCE_DEPOSIT">
              Maintenance deposit
            </option>
            <option value="HANDOVER_PAYMENT">
              Handover payment
            </option>
            <option value="FINAL_PAYMENT">
              Final payment
            </option>
            <option value="EXTRA_PAYMENT">
              Extra payment
            </option>
            <option value="OTHER">Other</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300">
          Description
        </label>

        <input
          name="description"
          disabled={isPending}
          placeholder="Estimated monthly installments for 2027"
          className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white placeholder:text-slate-600 disabled:opacity-60"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="rounded-xl border border-amber-500/20 bg-amber-500/10 p-4">
        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-amber-200">
          Generated payments are marked as estimates
        </p>

        <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-amber-300/70">
          Use this only when the detailed developer schedule is
          unavailable. Replace estimates later with confirmed
          payments.
        </p>
      </div>

      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={isPending}
        className="w-full rounded-xl bg-violet-600 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-violet-500 disabled:cursor-wait disabled:opacity-60"
      >
        {isPending
          ? "Generating schedule..."
          : "Generate recurring schedule"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useRef, useState } from "react";

type ConfirmDeletePaymentButtonProps = {
  paymentId: number;
  liabilityId: number;
  paymentLabel: string;
  deleteAction: (
    formData: FormData
  ) => void | Promise<void>;
};

export function ConfirmDeletePaymentButton({
  paymentId,
  liabilityId,
  paymentLabel,
  deleteAction,
}: ConfirmDeletePaymentButtonProps) {
  const dialogRef =
    useRef<HTMLDialogElement>(null);

  const [isDeleting, setIsDeleting] =
    useState(false);

  function openDialog() {
    dialogRef.current?.showModal();
  }

  function closeDialog() {
    if (!isDeleting) {
      dialogRef.current?.close();
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={openDialog}
        className="text-sm font-semibold text-rose-300 transition hover:text-rose-200"
      >
        Delete
      </button>

      <dialog
        ref={dialogRef}
        onCancel={(event) => {
          if (isDeleting) {
            event.preventDefault();
          }
        }}
        className="m-auto w-[calc(100%-2rem)] max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 p-0 text-slate-100 shadow-2xl backdrop:bg-black/70"
      >
        <div className="p-6">
          <div className="flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300">
            <span className="text-xl">×</span>
          </div>

          <h2 className="mt-5 text-xl font-semibold text-white">
            Delete scheduled payment?
          </h2>

          <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-400">
            You are about to permanently delete:
          </p>

          <p className="mt-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-200">
            {paymentLabel}
          </p>

          <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
            The yearly total and unscheduled contract balance
            will be recalculated automatically.
          </p>

          <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={closeDialog}
              disabled={isDeleting}
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-200 transition hover:bg-slate-700 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>

            <form
              action={async (formData) => {
                setIsDeleting(true);

                try {
                  await deleteAction(formData);
                  dialogRef.current?.close();
                } finally {
                  setIsDeleting(false);
                }
              }}
            >
              <input
                type="hidden"
                name="paymentId"
                value={paymentId}
              />

              <input
                type="hidden"
                name="liabilityId"
                value={liabilityId}
              />

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={isDeleting}
                className="w-full rounded-xl bg-rose-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-rose-500 disabled:cursor-wait disabled:opacity-60"
              >
                {isDeleting
                  ? "Deleting..."
                  : "Delete permanently"}
              </button>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      </dialog>
    </>
  );
}

import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

import { deletePayment } from "./actions";
import { BulkPaymentForm } from "./bulk-payment-form";
import { PaymentForm } from "./payment-form";
import { ConfirmDeletePaymentButton } from "./confirm-delete-payment-button";

export const instant = false;

type SchedulePageProps = {
  params: Promise<{
    id: string;
  }>;
};

function formatMoney(
  amountMinor: number,
  currency: string
): string {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
    style: "currency",
    currency,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  }).format(amountMinor / 100);
}

function formatDate(date: Date): string {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
    year: "numeric",
    month: "short",
    day: "2-digit",
  }).format(date);
}

export default async function SchedulePage({
  params,
}: SchedulePageProps) {
  const { id: rawId } = await params;
  const liabilityId = Number(rawId);

  if (
    !Number.isInteger(liabilityId) ||
    liabilityId <= 0
  ) {
    notFound();
  }

  const liability = await prisma.liability.findUnique({
    where: {
      id: liabilityId,
    },
    include: {
      payments: {
        orderBy: {
          paymentDate: "asc",
        },
      },
    },
  });

  if (!liability) {
    notFound();
  }

  const plannedPayments = liability.payments.filter(
    (payment) =>
      payment.status !== "CANCELLED" &&
      payment.status !== "PAID"
  );

  const totalPlannedMinor = plannedPayments.reduce(
    (total, payment) => total + payment.amountMinor,
    0
  );

  const unscheduledBalanceMinor = Math.max(
    liability.remainingAmountMinor - totalPlannedMinor,
    0
  );

  const yearlyTotals = new Map<number, number>();

  for (const payment of plannedPayments) {
    const year = payment.paymentDate.getFullYear();

    yearlyTotals.set(
      year,
      (yearlyTotals.get(year) ?? 0) +
        payment.amountMinor
    );
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-950 text-slate-100">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6 py-10">
        <header className="mb-8 flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-end">
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-400">
              Mizan Local
            </p>

            <h1 className="mt-3 text-4xl font-bold text-white">
              {liability.name} Payment Schedule
            </h1>

            <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-slate-400">
              Remaining contract balance:{" "}
              {formatMoney(
                liability.remainingAmountMinor,
                liability.currency
              )}
            </p>
          </div>

          <Link
            href="/liabilities"
            className="rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-slate-200 transition hover:bg-slate-800"
          >
            Back to Liabilities
          </Link>
        </header>

        <section className="mb-8 grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-5">
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">
              Remaining contract
            </p>

            <p className="mt-2 text-xl font-bold text-white">
              {formatMoney(
                liability.remainingAmountMinor,
                liability.currency
              )}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-5">
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">
              Scheduled planned payments
            </p>

            <p className="mt-2 text-xl font-bold text-white">
              {formatMoney(
                totalPlannedMinor,
                liability.currency
              )}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-5">
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">
              Unscheduled balance
            </p>

            <p className="mt-2 text-xl font-bold text-white">
              {formatMoney(
                unscheduledBalanceMinor,
                liability.currency
              )}
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="grid items-start gap-8 xl:grid-cols-[0.7fr_1.3fr]">
          <div className="space-y-6">
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-white">
                Add scheduled payment
              </h2>

              <div className="mt-6">
                <PaymentForm
                  liabilityId={liability.id}
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-white">
                Generate recurring schedule
              </h2>

              <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-400">
                Create monthly, quarterly, semiannual, or
                annual estimated payments for a selected date
                range.
              </p>

              <div className="mt-6">
                <BulkPaymentForm
                  liabilityId={liability.id}

          <div className="space-y-6">
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-white">
                Yearly totals
              </h2>

              <div className="mt-4 space-y-3">
                {yearlyTotals.size === 0 ? (
                  <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">
                    No planned payments yet.
                  </p>
                ) : (
                  Array.from(yearlyTotals.entries()).map(
                    ([year, total]) => (
                      <div
                        key={year}
                        className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-3"
                      >
                        <span>{year}</span>

                        <strong>
                          {formatMoney(
                            total,
                            liability.currency

                        </strong>
                      </div>

                )}
              </div>

                        >
                          No scheduled payments have been
                          added.

                      liability.payments.map((payment) => (

"use client";

import { useActionState, useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import {
  createPayment,
  type PaymentActionState,
} from "./actions";

const initialState: PaymentActionState = {
  success: false,
  message: "",
};

export function PaymentForm({
  liabilityId,
}: {
  liabilityId: number;
}) {
  const formRef = useRef<HTMLFormElement>(null);

  const [state, formAction, isPending] = useActionState(
    createPayment,
    initialState
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (state.success) {
      formRef.current?.reset();
    }
  }, [state.success]);

  return (
    <form
      ref={formRef}
      action={formAction}
      className="space-y-5"
    >
      <input
        type="hidden"
        name="liabilityId"
        value={liabilityId}
      />

      {state.message ? (
        <div
          className={`rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm ${
            state.success
              ? "border-emerald-500/20 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300"
              : "border-rose-500/20 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300"
          }`}
        >
          {state.message}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <div>
        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300">
          Payment date
        </label>
        <input
          name="paymentDate"
          type="date"
          required
          disabled={isPending}
          className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-white"
        />
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-300">
          Amount
        </label>
        <input

          >
            <option value="INSTALLMENT">Installment</option>
            <option value="MAINTENANCE_DEPOSIT">
              Maintenance deposit
            </option>

              Handover payment
            </option>
