============================================================
FILE: src\app\liabilities\actions.ts
============================================================
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

export type LiabilityActionState = {
  success: boolean;
  message: string;
};

function readRequiredText(
  formData: FormData,
  field: string
): string {
  const value = formData.get(field);

  if (typeof value !== "string" || value.trim() === "") {
    throw new Error(`${field} is required.`);
  }

  return value.trim();
}

function readRequiredAmount(
  formData: FormData,
  field: string
): number {
  const raw = formData.get(field);

  if (typeof raw !== "string" || raw.trim() === "") {
    throw new Error(`${field} is required.`);
  }

  const value = Number(raw.replaceAll(",", ""));

  if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0) {
    throw new Error(`${field} must be a valid non-negative number.`);
  }

  return value;
}

function readOptionalAmount(
  formData: FormData,
  field: string
): number | null {
  const raw = formData.get(field);

  if (typeof raw !== "string" || raw.trim() === "") {
    return null;
  }

  const value = Number(raw.replaceAll(",", ""));

  if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0) {
    throw new Error(`${field} must be a valid non-negative number.`);
  }

  return value;
}

function readOptionalDate(
  formData: FormData,
  field: string
): Date | null {
  const raw = formData.get(field);

  if (typeof raw !== "string" || raw.trim() === "") {
    return null;
  }

  const date = new Date(`${raw}T12:00:00`);

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
    throw new Error(`${field} is invalid.`);
  }

  return date;
}

function toMinor(amount: number): number {
  return Math.round(amount * 100);
}

export async function createLiability(
  previousState: LiabilityActionState,
  formData: FormData
): Promise<LiabilityActionState> {
  try {
    const name = readRequiredText(formData, "name");
    const liabilityType = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "liabilityType"
    );
    const accountingTreatment = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "accountingTreatment"
    );
    const country = readRequiredText(formData, "country");
    const currency = readRequiredText(formData, "currency");

    const originalAmount = readRequiredAmount(
      formData,
      "originalAmount"
    );
    const remainingAmount = readRequiredAmount(
      formData,
      "remainingAmount"
    );
    const monthlyPayment = readOptionalAmount(
      formData,
      "monthlyPayment"
    );

    if (remainingAmount > originalAmount) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message:
          "Remaining amount cannot exceed the original amount.",
      };
    }

    const includedInNetWorth =
      accountingTreatment === "NET_WORTH_DEBT";

    const notesValue = formData.get("notes");
    const notes =
      typeof notesValue === "string" && notesValue.trim()
        ? notesValue.trim()
        : null;

    await prisma.liability.create({
      data: {
        name,
        liabilityType,
        accountingTreatment,
        country,
        currency,
        originalAmountMinor: toMinor(originalAmount),
        remainingAmountMinor: toMinor(remainingAmount),
        monthlyPaymentMinor:
          monthlyPayment === null
            ? null
            : toMinor(monthlyPayment),
        startDate: readOptionalDate(formData, "startDate"),
        endDate: readOptionalDate(formData, "endDate"),
        includedInNetWorth,
        notes,
      },
    });

    revalidatePath("/");
    revalidatePath("/liabilities");
    revalidatePath("/database-test");

    return {
      success: true,
      message: `${name} was added successfully.`,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Create liability failed:", error);

    return {
      success: false,
      message:
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "The liability could not be created.",
    };
  }
}

export async function deleteLiability(formData: FormData) {
  const id = Number(formData.get("id"));

  if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
    throw new Error("Invalid liability ID.");
  }

  await prisma.liability.delete({
    where: { id },
  });

  revalidatePath("/");
  revalidatePath("/liabilities");
  revalidatePath("/database-test");
}

export async function updateLiability(
  previousState: LiabilityActionState,
  formData: FormData
): Promise<LiabilityActionState> {
  try {
    const idValue = formData.get("id");
      
    if (typeof idValue !== "string") {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Liability ID is required.",
      };
    }

    const id = Number(idValue);

    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid liability ID.",
      };
    }

    const existingLiability =
      await prisma.liability.findUnique({
        where: {
          id,
        },
        include: {
          payments: true,
        },
      });

    if (!existingLiability) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Liability was not found.",
      };
    }

    const name = readRequiredText(formData, "name");

    const liabilityType = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "liabilityType"
    );

    const accountingTreatment = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "accountingTreatment"
    );

    const country = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "country"
    );

    const currency = readRequiredText(
      formData,
      "currency"
    );

    const originalAmount = readRequiredAmount(
      formData,
      "originalAmount"
    );

    const remainingAmount = readRequiredAmount(
  formData,
  "remainingAmount"
);

const monthlyPayment = readOptionalAmount(
  formData,
  "monthlyPayment"
);

    if (remainingAmount > originalAmount) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message:
          "Remaining amount cannot exceed the original amount.",
      };
    }

    const allowedTreatments = [
      "NET_WORTH_DEBT",
      "CASH_FLOW_COMMITMENT",
    ];

    if (
      !allowedTreatments.includes(
        accountingTreatment
      )
    ) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid accounting treatment.",
      };
    }

    const activeScheduledPayments =
  await prisma.liabilityPayment.aggregate({
    where: {
      liabilityId: id,
      status: {
        in: ["PLANNED", "DEFERRED"],
      },
    },
    _sum: {
      amountMinor: true,
    },
  });

const scheduledOutstandingMinor =
  activeScheduledPayments._sum.amountMinor ?? 0;

const remainingAmountMinor =
  toMinor(remainingAmount);

if (
  remainingAmountMinor <
  scheduledOutstandingMinor
) {
  return {
    success: false,
    message:
      `Remaining amount cannot be lower than the active scheduled payments. ` +
      `Entered: ${remainingAmount.toLocaleString()} ${currency}. ` +
      `Active schedule: ${(
        scheduledOutstandingMinor / 100
      ).toLocaleString()} ${currency}.`,
  };
}

    if (
      existingLiability.currency !== currency &&
      existingLiability.payments.length > 0
    ) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message:
          "Currency cannot be changed while the liability has scheduled payments. Delete or migrate the schedule first.",
      };
    }

    const notesValue = formData.get("notes");

    const notes =
      typeof notesValue === "string" &&
      notesValue.trim()
        ? notesValue.trim()
        : null;

    const includedInNetWorth =
      accountingTreatment === "NET_WORTH_DEBT";

    await prisma.liability.update({
      where: {
        id,
      },
      data: {
        name,
        liabilityType,
        accountingTreatment,
        country,
        currency,
        originalAmountMinor:
          toMinor(originalAmount),
        remainingAmountMinor,
        monthlyPaymentMinor:
          monthlyPayment === null
            ? null
            : toMinor(monthlyPayment),
        startDate: readOptionalDate(
          formData,
          "startDate"
        ),
        endDate: readOptionalDate(
          formData,
          "endDate"
        ),
        includedInNetWorth,
        notes,
      },
    });

    revalidatePath("/");
    revalidatePath("/liabilities");
    revalidatePath(`/liabilities/${id}/edit`);
    revalidatePath(
      `/liabilities/${id}/schedule`
    );
    revalidatePath("/database-test");

    return {
      success: true,
      message: `${name} was updated successfully.`,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "Update liability failed:",
      error
    );

    return {
      success: false,
      message:
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "The liability could not be updated.",
    };
  }
}

"use client";

import { useRef, useState } from "react";

type Props = {
  liabilityId: number;
  liabilityName: string;
  deleteAction: (
    formData: FormData
  ) => void | Promise<void>;
};

export function ConfirmDeleteLiabilityButton({
  liabilityId,
  liabilityName,
  deleteAction,
}: Props) {
  const dialogRef =
    useRef<HTMLDialogElement>(null);

  const [isDeleting, setIsDeleting] =
    useState(false);

  return (
    <>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() =>
          dialogRef.current?.showModal()
        }
        className="rounded-lg border border-rose-500/20 bg-rose-500/10 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-rose-300 hover:bg-rose-500/20"
      >
        Delete
      </button>

      <dialog
        ref={dialogRef}
        className="m-auto w-[calc(100%-2rem)] max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 p-0 text-slate-100 shadow-2xl backdrop:bg-black/70"
      >
        <div className="p-6">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold text-white">
            Delete liability?
          </h2>

          <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-400">
            This will permanently delete{" "}
            <strong className="text-slate-200">
              {liabilityName}
            </strong>
            .
          </p>

          <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={isDeleting}
              onClick={() =>
                dialogRef.current?.close()
              }
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-200"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>

            <form
              action={async (formData) => {
                setIsDeleting(true);

                try {
                  await deleteAction(formData);
                  dialogRef.current?.close();
                } finally {
                  setIsDeleting(false);
                }
              }}
            >
              <input
                type="hidden"
                name="id"
                value={liabilityId}
              />

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={isDeleting}
                className="w-full rounded-xl bg-rose-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-rose-500 disabled:opacity-60"
              >
                {isDeleting
                  ? "Deleting..."
                  : "Delete permanently"}
              </button>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      </dialog>
    </>
  );
}

import Link from "next/link";
import { Suspense } from "react";

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { LiabilityForm } from "./liability-form";
import { deleteLiability } from "./actions";

import {
  ConfirmDeleteLiabilityButton,
} from "./confirm-delete-liability-button";

function formatMoney(
  amountMinor: number | null,
  currency: string
) {
  if (amountMinor === null) {
    return "Not recorded";
  }

  return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
    style: "currency",
    currency,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  }).format(amountMinor / 100);
}

function treatmentStyles(treatment: string) {
  if (treatment === "NET_WORTH_DEBT") {
    return "bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300";
  }

  return "bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-300";
}

async function LiabilitiesTable() {
  const liabilities =
    await prisma.liability.findMany({
      orderBy: [
        {
          accountingTreatment: "asc",
        },
        {
          name: "asc",
        },
      ],
    });

  if (liabilities.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-700 bg-slate-900/50 p-10 text-center">
        <h2 className="font-semibold text-white">
          No liabilities yet
        </h2>

        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-400">
          Add debts or future contractual commitments
          using the form.
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900">
      <div className="overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="min-w-full divide-y divide-slate-800">
          <thead>
            <tr className="text-left text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
              <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">
                Liability
              </th>
              <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">
                Treatment
              </th>
              <th className="px-5 py-4 text-right font-medium">
                Original
              </th>
              <th className="px-5 py-4 text-right font-medium">
                Remaining
              </th>
              <th className="px-5 py-4 text-right font-medium">
                Monthly
              </th>
              <th className="px-5 py-4 text-right font-medium">
                Action
              </th>
            </tr>
          </thead>

          <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-800">
            {liabilities.map((liability) => (
              <tr
                key={liability.id}
                className="transition hover:bg-slate-800/40"
              >
                <td className="px-5 py-4">
                  <p className="font-medium text-white">
                    {liability.name}
                  </p>

                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
                    {liability.country ?? "No country"} ·{" "}
                    {liability.currency}
                  </p>
                </td>

                <td className="px-5 py-4">
                  <span
                    className={`rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${treatmentStyles(
                      liability.accountingTreatment
                    )}`}
                  >
                    {liability.accountingTreatment ===
                    "NET_WORTH_DEBT"
                      ? "NET WORTH DEBT"
                      : "FUTURE COMMITMENT"}
                  </span>
                </td>

                <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4 text-right text-sm text-slate-400">
                  {formatMoney(
                    liability.originalAmountMinor,
                    liability.currency
                  )}
                </td>

                <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4 text-right font-semibold text-white">
                  {formatMoney(
                    liability.remainingAmountMinor,
                    liability.currency
                  )}
                </td>

                <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4 text-right text-sm text-slate-300">
                  {formatMoney(
                    liability.monthlyPaymentMinor,
                    liability.currency
                  )}
                </td>

                <td className="px-5 py-4 text-right">
  <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
    <Link
      href={`/liabilities/${liability.id}/edit`}
      className="rounded-lg border border-violet-500/20 bg-violet-500/10 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-violet-300 transition hover:bg-violet-500/20"
    >
      Edit
    </Link>

    <Link
      href={`/liabilities/${liability.id}/schedule`}
      className="rounded-lg border border-blue-500/20 bg-blue-500/10 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-300 transition hover:bg-blue-500/20"
    >
      Schedule
    </Link>

    <ConfirmDeleteLiabilityButton
      liabilityId={liability.id}
      liabilityName={liability.name}
      deleteAction={deleteLiability}
    />
  </div>
</td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { Suspense } from "react";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

function formatMoney(amountMinor: number, currency: string) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
    style: "currency",
    currency,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  }).format(amountMinor / 100);
}

function formatNumber(value: number) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  }).format(value);
}

type CurrencyRates = Record<string, number>;

function convertToEgp(
  amountMinor: number,
  currency: string,
  rates: CurrencyRates
) {
  if (currency === "EGP") {
    return amountMinor;
  }

  const rate = rates[currency];

  if (!rate) {
    return 0;
  }

  return Math.round(amountMinor * rate);
}

function KpiCard({
  label,
  value,
  hint,
  accent = "blue",
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  hint: string;
  accent?: "blue" | "emerald" | "amber" | "violet";
}) {
  const accentClasses = {
    blue: "bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-300 ring-blue-500/20",
    emerald: "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300 ring-emerald-500/20",
    amber: "bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-300 ring-amber-500/20",
    violet: "bg-violet-500/10 text-violet-300 ring-violet-500/20",
  };

  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-5 shadow-lg shadow-black/10">
      <div
        className={`inline-flex rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ring-1 ${accentClasses[accent]}`}
      >
        {label}
      </div>

      <p className="mt-5 text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white">
        {value}
      </p>

      <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-400">{hint}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

function ProgressBar({
  percentage,
  complete,
}: {
  percentage: number;
  complete: boolean;
}) {
  const safePercentage = Math.min(Math.max(percentage, 0), 100);

  return (
    <div className="mt-4 h-2.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-800">
      <div
        className={`h-full rounded-full ${
          complete ? "bg-emerald-500" : "bg-blue-500"
        }`}
        style={{ width: `${safePercentage}%` }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

async function DashboardData() {
  const [
  accounts,
  assets,
  netWorthLiabilities,
  futureCommitments,
  goals,
  exchangeRates,
] = await Promise.all([
  prisma.account.findMany({
    where: {
      includedInNetWorth: true,
    },
    orderBy: {
      name: "asc",
    },
  }),

  prisma.asset.findMany({
    orderBy: {
      name: "asc",
    },
  }),

  prisma.liability.findMany({
    where: {
      includedInNetWorth: true,
    },
    orderBy: {
      name: "asc",
    },
  }),

  prisma.liability.findMany({
    where: {
      accountingTreatment: "CASH_FLOW_COMMITMENT",
    },
    orderBy: {
      name: "asc",
    },
  }),

  prisma.goal.findMany({
    orderBy: [
      {
        priority: "asc",
      },
      {
        name: "asc",
      },
    ],
  }),

  prisma.exchangeRate.findMany({
    where: {
      toCurrency: "EGP",
    },
    orderBy: {
      rateDate: "desc",
    },
  }),
]);

  const rates: CurrencyRates = {
    EGP: 1,
  };

  for (const exchangeRate of exchangeRates) {
    if (!rates[exchangeRate.fromCurrency]) {
      rates[exchangeRate.fromCurrency] = exchangeRate.rate;
    }
  }

  const accountTotalEgpMinor = accounts.reduce((total, account) => {
    return (
      total +
      convertToEgp(account.balanceMinor, account.currency, rates)
    );
  }, 0);

  const assetTotalEgpMinor = assets.reduce((total, asset) => {
    return (
      total +
      convertToEgp(asset.marketValueMinor, asset.currency, rates)
    );
  }, 0);

  const netWorthLiabilityEgpMinor =
  netWorthLiabilities.reduce((total, liability) => {
    return (
      total +
      convertToEgp(
        liability.remainingAmountMinor,
        liability.currency,
        rates
      )
    );
  }, 0);

const futureCommitmentsEgpMinor =
  futureCommitments.reduce((total, liability) => {
    return (
      total +
      convertToEgp(
        liability.remainingAmountMinor,
        liability.currency,
        rates
      )
    );
  }, 0);

  const netWorthEgpMinor =
  accountTotalEgpMinor +
  assetTotalEgpMinor -
  netWorthLiabilityEgpMinor;

  const liquidAccountEgpMinor = accounts
    .filter((account) =>
      ["IMMEDIATE", "HIGH"].includes(account.liquidityClass)
    )
    .reduce((total, account) => {
      return (
        total +
        convertToEgp(account.balanceMinor, account.currency, rates)
      );
    }, 0);

  const liquidAssetEgpMinor = assets
    .filter((asset) =>
      ["IMMEDIATE", "HIGH"].includes(asset.liquidityClass)
    )
    .reduce((total, asset) => {
      return (
        total +
        convertToEgp(asset.marketValueMinor, asset.currency, rates)
      );
    }, 0);

  const liquidNetWorthEgpMinor =
    liquidAccountEgpMinor + liquidAssetEgpMinor;

  const fundedGoals = goals.filter(
    (goal) => goal.fundedAmountMinor >= goal.targetAmountMinor
  ).length;

  const assetsByCurrency = new Map<string, number>();

  for (const account of accounts) {
    assetsByCurrency.set(
      account.currency,
      (assetsByCurrency.get(account.currency) ?? 0) +
        account.balanceMinor
    );
  }

  for (const asset of assets) {
    assetsByCurrency.set(
      asset.currency,
      (assetsByCurrency.get(asset.currency) ?? 0) +
        asset.marketValueMinor
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <section className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
        <KpiCard
          label="Recorded net worth"
          value={formatMoney(netWorthEgpMinor, "EGP")}
          hint="Accounts plus market-value assets, less recorded liabilities."
          accent="blue"
        />

        <KpiCard
          label="Liquid assets"
          value={formatMoney(liquidNetWorthEgpMinor, "EGP")}
          hint="Balances classified as immediate or highly liquid."
          accent="emerald"
        />

        <KpiCard
  label="Future commitments"
  value={formatMoney(futureCommitmentsEgpMinor, "EGP")}
  hint="Contractual payments shown separately and not deducted twice from recorded net worth."
  accent="amber"
/>

        <KpiCard
          label="Funded goals"
          value={`${fundedGoals} / ${goals.length}`}
          hint="Goals that have reached or exceeded their target."
          accent="violet"
        />
      </section>

      <section className="mt-8 grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[1.15fr_0.85fr]">
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900">
          <div className="border-b border-slate-800 px-6 py-5">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-white">
              Assets by currency
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-400">
              Native balances before conversion to EGP.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="divide-y divide-slate-800">
            {assetsByCurrency.size === 0 ? (
              <p className="px-6 py-8 text-sm text-slate-400">
                No accounts or assets have been entered yet.
              </p>
            ) : (
              Array.from(assetsByCurrency.entries()).map(
                ([currency, amountMinor]) => (
                  <div
                    key={currency}
                    className="flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-4"
                  >
                    <div>
                      <p className="font-medium text-white">{currency}</p>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
                        Current native-currency balance
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <p className="font-semibold text-slate-200">
                      {formatMoney(amountMinor, currency)}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                )
              )
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900">
          <div className="border-b border-slate-800 px-6 py-5">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-white">
              Current exchange rates
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-400">
              Latest stored rate to EGP for every currency.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="divide-y divide-slate-800">
            {Object.entries(rates).map(([currency, rate]) => (
              <div
                key={currency}
                className="flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-4"
              >
                <p className="font-medium text-white">{currency} / EGP</p>
                <p className="font-semibold text-slate-200">
                  {formatNumber(rate)}
                </p>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="mt-8">
        <div className="mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold text-white">
            Financial goals
          </h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-400">
            Funding progress based on confirmed amounts only.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2">
          {goals.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-700 p-8 text-sm text-slate-400">
              No goals have been created yet.
            </div>
          ) : (
            goals.map((goal) => {
              const percentage =
                goal.targetAmountMinor === 0
                  ? 100
                  : (goal.fundedAmountMinor /
                      goal.targetAmountMinor) *
                    100;

              const gapMinor = Math.max(
                goal.targetAmountMinor - goal.fundedAmountMinor,
                0
              );

              const complete = gapMinor === 0;

              return (
                <article
                  key={goal.id}
                  className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <h3 className="font-semibold text-white">
                        {goal.name}
                      </h3>

                      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-400">
                        Priority {goal.priority} · {goal.status}
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <span
                      className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${
                        complete
                          ? "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300"
                          : "bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-300"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {percentage.toFixed(1)}%
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <ProgressBar
                    percentage={percentage}
                    complete={complete}
                  />

                  <div className="mt-5 grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                        Funded
                      </p>
                      <p className="mt-1 font-semibold text-white">
                        {formatMoney(
                          goal.fundedAmountMinor,
                          goal.currency
                        )}
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                        Remaining
                      </p>
                      <p className="mt-1 font-semibold text-white">
                        {formatMoney(gapMinor, goal.currency)}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {goal.notes ? (
                    <p className="mt-5 border-t border-slate-800 pt-4 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-400">
                      {goal.notes}
                    </p>
                  ) : null}
                </article>
              );
            })
          )}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
        <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
          <div>
            <h2 className="font-semibold text-white">Database status</h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-400">
              Live data read directly from the local SQLite database.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-xs">
            <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2 text-slate-300">
              {accounts.length} accounts
            </span>
            <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2 text-slate-300">
              {assets.length} assets
            </span>
            <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2 text-slate-300">
              {netWorthLiabilities.length + futureCommitments.length} liabilities
            </span>
            <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2 text-slate-300">
              {goals.length} goals
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    </>
  );
}

FILE: src\lib\market-data\binance.ts
============================================================
import Decimal from "decimal.js";

const BINANCE_API_BASE_URL =
  "https://api.binance.com";

type BinanceTickerPriceResponse = {
  symbol: string;
  price: string;
};

export type BinancePriceResult = {
  symbol: string;
  priceMinor: number;
  priceDate: Date;
  sourceUrl: string;
};

function decimalToSafeInteger(
  value: Decimal,
  errorMessage: string
): number {
  if (!value.isInteger()) {
    throw new Error(
      "Binance price conversion did not produce an integer."
    );
  }

  const maximumSafeInteger = new Decimal(
    Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER
  );

  const minimumSafeInteger = new Decimal(
    Number.MIN_SAFE_INTEGER
  );

  if (
    value.gt(maximumSafeInteger) ||
    value.lt(minimumSafeInteger)
  ) {
    throw new Error(errorMessage);
  }

  return value.toNumber();
}

// Mizan currently stores monetary amounts with two decimal places.
// This is suitable for valuation totals but may round very small
// token prices. A future migration can add per-instrument priceScale.
function priceToMinor(
  priceText: string
): number {
  let price: Decimal;

  try {
    price = new Decimal(priceText);
  } catch {
    throw new Error(
      `Binance returned an invalid price: ${priceText}`
    );
  }

  if (
    !price.isFinite() ||
    price.isNegative()
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `Binance returned an invalid price: ${priceText}`
    );
  }

  const priceMinor = price
    .mul(100)
    .toDecimalPlaces(
      0,
      Decimal.ROUND_HALF_UP
    );

  return decimalToSafeInteger(
    priceMinor,
    "The Binance price is too large to store safely."
  );
}

export async function fetchBinancePrice(
  providerSymbol: string
): Promise<BinancePriceResult> {
  const symbol = providerSymbol
    .trim()
    .toUpperCase();

  if (!symbol) {
    throw new Error(
      "Binance provider symbol is required."
    );
  }

  const sourceUrl =
    `${BINANCE_API_BASE_URL}` +
    `/api/v3/ticker/price` +
    `?symbol=${encodeURIComponent(symbol)}`;

  const response = await fetch(sourceUrl, {
    method: "GET",
    headers: {
      Accept: "application/json",
    },

    // Market prices must be fetched live rather than
    // served from the Next.js request cache.
    cache: "no-store",

    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10_000),
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    const responseText =
      await response.text();

    throw new Error(
      `Binance request failed for ${symbol}: ` +
        `${response.status} ${response.statusText}. ` +
        responseText.slice(0, 200)
    );
  }

  const data =
    (await response.json()) as BinanceTickerPriceResponse;

  if (
    !data ||
    typeof data.symbol !== "string" ||
    typeof data.price !== "string"
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `Binance returned an unexpected response for ${symbol}.`
    );
  }

  if (
    data.symbol.toUpperCase() !== symbol
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `Binance returned ${data.symbol} while ${symbol} was requested.`
    );
  }

  return {
    symbol,
    priceMinor: priceToMinor(data.price),
    priceDate: new Date(),
    sourceUrl,
  };
}

FILE: src\lib\market-data\coinmarketcap.ts
============================================================
import Decimal from "decimal.js";

import { parseCryptoPair } from "./crypto-pair";

const CMC_BASE_URL = "https://pro-api.coinmarketcap.com";
const REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS = 15_000;

/**
 * CoinMarketCap may return quote collections as a currency-keyed object
 * or, in some API response variants, as an array. The adapter supports both.
 */
type CmcQuote = {
  price?: number | string;
  last_updated?: string;
  currency?: string;
  symbol?: string;
  name?: string;
};

type CmcQuoteCollection =
  | Record<string, CmcQuote | undefined>
  | CmcQuote[];

type CmcAsset = {
  id?: number;
  name?: string;
  symbol?: string;
  slug?: string;
  cmc_rank?: number;
  quote?: CmcQuoteCollection;
};

type CmcResponse = {
  status?: {
    error_code?: number;
    error_message?: string | null;
  };
  data?:
    | CmcAsset[]
    | Record<string, CmcAsset | CmcAsset[] | undefined>;
};

export type CoinMarketCapPriceResult = {
  cmcId: number;
  pair: string;
  baseSymbol: string;
  quoteSymbol: string;
  name: string;
  priceMinor: number;
  priceDate: Date;
  sourceUrl: string;
};

function decimalToSafeInteger(value: Decimal, message: string): number {
  if (!value.isInteger()) {
    throw new Error("CoinMarketCap price conversion did not produce an integer.");
  }

  const maximum = new Decimal(Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER);
  const minimum = new Decimal(Number.MIN_SAFE_INTEGER);

  if (value.gt(maximum) || value.lt(minimum)) {
    throw new Error(message);
  }

  return value.toNumber();
}

/**
 * Mizan currently stores market prices in two-decimal minor units.
 * A future priceScale migration will be needed for very low-priced tokens.
 */
function priceToMinor(price: number): number {
  if (!Number.isFinite(price) || price < 0) {
    throw new Error(`CoinMarketCap returned an invalid price: ${price}.`);
  }

  return decimalToSafeInteger(
    new Decimal(price).mul(100).toDecimalPlaces(0, Decimal.ROUND_HALF_UP),
    "CoinMarketCap price is too large to store safely."
  );
}

/**
 * CoinMarketCap uses USD as the valuation proxy for USD-backed stablecoin pairs.
 * The returned holding currency remains the actual pair quote, such as USDC.
 */
function getConvertCurrency(quoteSymbol: string): string {
  const stablecoins = ["USDC", "USDT", "FDUSD", "TUSD", "BUSD"];
  return stablecoins.includes(quoteSymbol) ? "USD" : quoteSymbol;
}

function isCmcAsset(value: unknown): value is CmcAsset {
  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null || Array.isArray(value)) {
    return false;
  }

  const candidate = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  return typeof candidate.symbol === "string" && "quote" in candidate;
}

function flattenAssets(data: CmcResponse["data"]): CmcAsset[] {
  if (!data) return [];
  if (Array.isArray(data)) return data.filter(isCmcAsset);

  const assets: CmcAsset[] = [];

  for (const value of Object.values(data)) {
    if (isCmcAsset(value)) {
      assets.push(value);
    } else if (Array.isArray(value)) {
      assets.push(...value.filter(isCmcAsset));
    }
  }

  return assets;
}

function quoteCurrencyLabel(quote: CmcQuote): string | null {
  const label = quote.currency ?? quote.symbol ?? quote.name;
  return typeof label === "string" && label.trim()
    ? label.trim().toUpperCase()
    : null;
}

function findQuote(
  collection: CmcQuoteCollection | undefined,
  requestedCurrency: string
): { quote: CmcQuote; availableCurrencies: string[] } | null {
  if (!collection) return null;

  const requested = requestedCurrency.toUpperCase();

  if (Array.isArray(collection)) {
    const labels = collection
      .map(quoteCurrencyLabel)
      .filter((value): value is string => Boolean(value));

    const exact = collection.find(
      (item) => quoteCurrencyLabel(item) === requested
    );

    // When convert=<currency> is requested, some response shapes return
    // a one-item quote array without repeating the currency label.
    const fallback = collection.length === 1 ? collection[0] : undefined;
    const quote = exact ?? fallback;

    return quote
      ? { quote, availableCurrencies: labels.length ? labels : [requested] }
      : null;
  }

  const entries = Object.entries(collection);
  const matched = entries.find(
    ([currency]) => currency.trim().toUpperCase() === requested
  );

  if (!matched?.[1]) return null;

  return {
    quote: matched[1],
    availableCurrencies: entries.map(([currency]) => currency),
  };
}

function parseQuotePrice(quote: CmcQuote): number {
  if (typeof quote.price === "number") return quote.price;
  if (typeof quote.price === "string") return Number(quote.price);
  return Number.NaN;
}

export async function fetchCoinMarketCapPairPrice(
  rawPair: string
): Promise<CoinMarketCapPriceResult> {
  const apiKey = process.env.COINMARKETCAP_API_KEY?.trim();

  if (!apiKey) {
    throw new Error("COINMARKETCAP_API_KEY is missing from .env.local.");
  }

  const { pair, baseSymbol, quoteSymbol } = parseCryptoPair(rawPair);
  const convertCurrency = getConvertCurrency(quoteSymbol);

  const searchParams = new URLSearchParams({
    symbol: baseSymbol,
    convert: convertCurrency,
  });

  const sourceUrl =
    `${CMC_BASE_URL}/v3/cryptocurrency/quotes/latest?${searchParams.toString()}`;

  let response: Response;

  try {
    response = await fetch(sourceUrl, {
      method: "GET",
      headers: {
        Accept: "application/json",
        "X-CMC_PRO_API_KEY": apiKey,
      },
      cache: "no-store",
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    const detail =
      error instanceof Error ? error.message || error.name : String(error);

    throw new Error(
      `CoinMarketCap network request failed for ${pair}. ` +
        `Details: ${detail || "No details returned."}`,
      { cause: error }
    );
  }

  const responseText = await response.text();

  if (!responseText.trim()) {
    throw new Error(
      `CoinMarketCap returned an empty response for ${pair}. ` +
        `HTTP ${response.status} ${response.statusText}.`
    );
  }

  let body: CmcResponse;

  try {
    body = JSON.parse(responseText) as CmcResponse;
  } catch (error) {
    throw new Error(
      `CoinMarketCap returned invalid JSON for ${pair}. ` +
        `Response: ${responseText.slice(0, 500)}`,
      { cause: error }
    );
  }

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(
      `CoinMarketCap request failed for ${pair}: ` +
        `HTTP ${response.status} ${response.statusText}. ` +
        `${body.status?.error_message ?? responseText.slice(0, 500)}`
    );
  }

  if (
    typeof body.status?.error_code === "number" &&
    body.status.error_code !== 0
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `CoinMarketCap API error for ${pair}: ` +
        `${body.status.error_message ?? `code ${body.status.error_code}`}`
    );
  }

  const matches = flattenAssets(body.data)
    .filter((asset) => asset.symbol?.toUpperCase() === baseSymbol)
    .sort(
      (first, second) =>
        (first.cmc_rank ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER) -
        (second.cmc_rank ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER)
    );

  const asset = matches[0];

  if (!asset) {
    throw new Error(
      `CoinMarketCap returned no ${baseSymbol} asset for pair ${pair}.`
    );
  }

  if (typeof asset.id !== "number") {
    throw new Error(
      `CoinMarketCap returned ${baseSymbol} without a numeric ID.`
    );
  }

  const resolvedQuote = findQuote(asset.quote, convertCurrency);

  if (!resolvedQuote) {
    const rawQuoteShape = Array.isArray(asset.quote)
      ? `array(${asset.quote.length})`
      : asset.quote
        ? `object(${Object.keys(asset.quote).join(", ") || "empty"})`
        : "missing";

    throw new Error(
      `CoinMarketCap returned ${baseSymbol}, but no ${convertCurrency} quote ` +
        `was available for pair ${pair}. Quote shape: ${rawQuoteShape}.`
    );
  }

  const rawPrice = parseQuotePrice(resolvedQuote.quote);

  if (!Number.isFinite(rawPrice)) {
    throw new Error(
      `CoinMarketCap returned an invalid ${convertCurrency} price for ${baseSymbol}. ` +
        `Received: ${String(resolvedQuote.quote.price)}. ` +
        `Available quote currencies: ${resolvedQuote.availableCurrencies.join(", ")}.`
    );
  }

  const lastUpdatedAt = resolvedQuote.quote.last_updated;
  const parsedDate =
    typeof lastUpdatedAt === "string" && lastUpdatedAt.trim()
      ? new Date(lastUpdatedAt)
      : new Date();

  return {
    cmcId: asset.id,
    pair,
    baseSymbol,
    quoteSymbol,
    name: asset.name ?? baseSymbol,
    priceMinor: priceToMinor(rawPrice),
    priceDate: Number.isNaN(parsedDate.getTime()) ? new Date() : parsedDate,
    sourceUrl,
  };
}


============================================================
FILE: src\lib\market-data\crypto-pair.ts
============================================================
export type ParsedCryptoPair = {
  pair: string;
  baseSymbol: string;
  quoteSymbol: string;
};

const supportedQuoteSymbols = [
  "USDT",
  "USDC",
  "FDUSD",
  "TUSD",
  "BUSD",
  "USD",
  "BTC",
  "ETH",
  "SOL",
  "BNB",
  "SUI",
  "FET",
  "ORDI",
  "ALGO",
  "FLOKI",
  "EUR",
  "GBP",
  "SAR",
  "EGP",
] as const;

export function parseCryptoPair(
  rawPair: string
): ParsedCryptoPair {
  const pair = rawPair
    .trim()
    .toUpperCase()
    .replaceAll("/", "")
    .replaceAll("-", "")
    .replaceAll("_", "")
    .replaceAll(" ", "");

  if (!/^[A-Z0-9]{5,30}$/.test(pair)) {
    throw new Error(
      "Enter a valid crypto pair using letters and numbers, such as BTCUSDC or ETHUSDT."
    );
  }

  // Longest quote symbols must be tested first.
  // Otherwise BTCFDUSD could match USD before FDUSD.
  const orderedQuotes = [
    ...supportedQuoteSymbols,
  ].sort(
    (first, second) =>
      second.length - first.length
  );

  const quoteSymbol =
    orderedQuotes.find((quote) =>
      pair.endsWith(quote)
    );

  if (!quoteSymbol) {
    throw new Error(
      `Could not identify the quote currency in ${pair}.`
    );
  }

  const baseSymbol = pair.slice(
    0,
    -quoteSymbol.length
  );

  if (!baseSymbol) {
    throw new Error(
      `Could not identify the base asset in ${pair}.`
    );
  }

  return {
    pair,
    baseSymbol,
    quoteSymbol,
  };
}

============================================================
FILE: src\lib\prisma.ts
============================================================
import { PrismaBetterSqlite3 } from "@prisma/adapter-better-sqlite3";
import { PrismaClient } from "@/generated/prisma/client";

const globalForPrisma = globalThis as unknown as {
  prisma: PrismaClient | undefined;
};

const adapter = new PrismaBetterSqlite3({
  url: process.env.DATABASE_URL ?? "file:./prisma/dev.db",
});

export const prisma =
  globalForPrisma.prisma ??
  new PrismaClient({
    adapter,
  });

if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production") {
  globalForPrisma.prisma = prisma;
}
